===== FILE: netlify/functions/get-wishes.js =====
// ============================================================
// GET /api/get-wishes?sort=newest|rating&category=all|<id>&cursor=<opaque>&limit=12
// Hanya status 'approved' dan is_private = false. Field status/is_private/ip_hash tidak pernah keluar.
// Kontrak respons sama dengan getWishes() di src/services/wishes.js:
//   { items, nextCursor, total, aggregate: { average, count } }
// ============================================================
import { CATEGORY_IDS, db, fail, json, totalFromRange } from '../lib/shared.js'

const PAGE_DEFAULT = 12
const PAGE_MAX = 50
const OFFSET_MAX = 2000
const COLUMNS = 'id,display_name,is_anonymous,category,message,rating,created_at'

const ORDER = {
    newest: 'created_at.desc,id.desc',
    rating: 'rating.desc,created_at.desc,id.desc',
}

// Cursor "opaque" = offset dalam base64url. Klien mengirim balik apa adanya.
const encodeCursor = (n) => Buffer.from(String(n)).toString('base64url')
function decodeCursor(c) {
    if (!c) return 0
    if (c.length > 24) return null
    const n = Number(Buffer.from(c, 'base64url').toString())
    return Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 0 && n <= OFFSET_MAX ? n : null
}

const CACHE_HEADERS = {
    // Browser: 30 dtk. CDN Netlify: 60 dtk + stale-while-revalidate (hemat kuota Supabase & function).
    'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=30',
    'Netlify-CDN-Cache-Control': 'public, s-maxage=60, stale-while-revalidate=120',
}

export default async (req) => {
    if (req.method !== 'GET') return fail('validation', 'Method not allowed.', 405)

    const params = new URL(req.url).searchParams
    const sort = params.get('sort') || 'newest'
    const category = params.get('category') || 'all'
    const offset = decodeCursor(params.get('cursor'))
    const limit = Math.min(PAGE_MAX, Math.max(1, parseInt(params.get('limit'), 10) || PAGE_DEFAULT))

    if (!ORDER[sort]) return fail('validation', 'Unknown sort.', 400)
    if (category !== 'all' && !CATEGORY_IDS.includes(category)) return fail('validation', 'Unknown category.', 400)
    if (offset === null) return fail('validation', 'Invalid cursor.', 400)

    try {
        const q = new URLSearchParams({
            select: COLUMNS,
            status: 'eq.approved',
            is_private: 'eq.false',
            order: ORDER[sort],
            limit: String(limit),
            offset: String(offset),
        })
        if (category !== 'all') q.set('category', `eq.${category}`)

        const [listRes, statsRes] = await Promise.all([
            db(`wishes?${q}`, { headers: { Prefer: 'count=exact' } }),
            db('rpc/wish_stats', { method: 'POST', body: {} }),
        ])

        // 416 = offset melewati jumlah baris → anggap halaman kosong
        let rows = []
        let total = 0
        if (listRes.status === 416) {
            total = totalFromRange(listRes)
        } else if (listRes.ok) {
            rows = await listRes.json()
            total = totalFromRange(listRes)
        } else {
            throw new Error(`list failed (${listRes.status})`)
        }

        if (!statsRes.ok) throw new Error(`stats failed (${statsRes.status})`)
        const stats = (await statsRes.json())?.[0] || {}

        const items = rows.map((r) => ({
            id: r.id,
            displayName: r.display_name,
            isAnonymous: r.is_anonymous,
            category: r.category,
            message: r.message,
            rating: r.rating,
            createdAt: r.created_at,
        }))

        const nextCursor = offset + items.length < total && items.length > 0
            ? encodeCursor(offset + items.length)
            : null

        return json(
            {
                items,
                nextCursor,
                total,
                aggregate: { count: Number(stats.count) || 0, average: Number(stats.average) || 0 },
            },
            200,
            CACHE_HEADERS
        )
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('[get-wishes]', err?.message || err)
        return fail('server', 'The observatory is quiet right now. Please try again soon.', 500)
    }
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: netlify/functions/submit-wish.js =====
// ============================================================
// POST /api/submit-wish
// Validasi ketat server-side → honeypot → rate limit + cek duplikat → simpan.
// Default status 'pending'. AUTO_APPROVE=true → 'approved' (hanya pesan publik tanpa email/link/telepon).
// Pesan private tersimpan tetapi TIDAK PERNAH dikembalikan oleh get-wishes.
// ============================================================
import {
    CATEGORY_IDS,
    MESSAGE_MIN,
    MESSAGE_MAX,
    NAME_MAX,
    cleanMessage,
    cleanName,
    db,
    fail,
    hasBadWords,
    hashIp,
    json,
    makeCallsign,
    needsReview,
} from '../lib/shared.js'

const LIMIT_PER_HOUR = 10
const LIMIT_PER_DAY = 30
const MAX_BODY = 4000
const HOUR_MS = 3600 * 1000
const DAY_MS = 24 * HOUR_MS

// Satu query untuk dua keperluan: rate limit (jumlah per jam / per hari) dan cek duplikat (24 jam terakhir)
async function recentFromIp(ipHash) {
    const q = new URLSearchParams({
        select: 'message,created_at',
        ip_hash: `eq.${ipHash}`,
        created_at: `gte.${new Date(Date.now() - DAY_MS).toISOString()}`,
        order: 'created_at.desc',
        limit: '20',
    })
    const res = await db(`wishes?${q}`)
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`recent check failed (${res.status})`)
    const rows = await res.json()
    return Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []
}

// Normalisasi untuk membandingkan pesan: huruf kecil, tanpa aksen, tanpa tanda baca, spasi dirapikan
const normalize = (s) =>
    String(s)
        .toLowerCase()
        .normalize('NFKD')
        .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
        .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/gu, ' ')
        .trim()

const toRecord = (r) => ({
    id: r.id,
    displayName: r.display_name,
    isAnonymous: r.is_anonymous,
    category: r.category,
    message: r.message,
    rating: r.rating,
    isPrivate: r.is_private,
    createdAt: r.created_at,
    status: r.status,
})

export default async (req, context) => {
    if (req.method !== 'POST') return fail('validation', 'Method not allowed.', 405)

    // ── Baca body ──
    let data
    try {
        const raw = await req.text()
        if (raw.length > MAX_BODY) return fail('validation', 'That message is too large.', 413)
        data = JSON.parse(raw)
    } catch (_) {
        return fail('validation', 'We could not read that request.', 400)
    }
    if (!data || typeof data !== 'object' || Array.isArray(data)) {
        return fail('validation', 'We could not read that request.', 400)
    }

    // ── Validasi & pembersihan ──
    const message = cleanMessage(data.message)
    if (message.length < MESSAGE_MIN || message.length > MESSAGE_MAX) {
        return fail('validation', `Your message needs ${MESSAGE_MIN} to ${MESSAGE_MAX} characters.`, 400)
    }
    const rating = data.rating
    if (typeof rating !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(rating) || rating < 1 || rating > 5) {
        return fail('validation', 'Please choose a rating from 1 to 5 stars.', 400)
    }
    const category = data.category
    if (typeof category !== 'string' || !CATEGORY_IDS.includes(category)) {
        return fail('validation', 'Please choose a category.', 400)
    }
    const isPrivate = data.isPrivate === true
    if (!isPrivate && data.consent !== true) {
        return fail('validation', 'Please agree to show your message in the Gallery, or send it privately.', 400)
    }

    let displayName = cleanName(data.displayName).slice(0, NAME_MAX)
    const isAnonymous = data.isAnonymous === true || !displayName
    if (!displayName) displayName = makeCallsign()

    if (hasBadWords(message) || hasBadWords(displayName)) {
        return fail('validation', 'Please keep your words kind. Try rephrasing your message.', 400)
    }

    // ── Honeypot: bot mengisi field tersembunyi → pura-pura sukses, tidak disimpan ──
    if (typeof data.website === 'string' && data.website.trim() !== '') {
        return json(
            {
                id: `ghost-${Date.now().toString(36)}`,
                displayName, isAnonymous, category, message, rating, isPrivate,
                createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                status: 'pending',
            },
            201,
            { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' }
        )
    }

    try {
        // ── Rate limit + cek duplikat per IP-hash ──
        const ipHash = hashIp(context?.ip || req.headers.get('x-nf-client-connection-ip'))
        if (ipHash) {
            const recent = await recentFromIp(ipHash)
            const hourAgo = Date.now() - HOUR_MS
            const lastHour = recent.filter((r) => Date.parse(r.created_at) >= hourAgo).length

            if (lastHour >= LIMIT_PER_HOUR || recent.length >= LIMIT_PER_DAY) {
                return fail('rate_limit', 'You have sent a few wishes already. Please come back later.', 429)
            }

            // Pesan yang sama persis dari IP yang sama dalam 24 jam → tolak ramah.
            // Pesan sama dari orang lain (IP berbeda) tetap boleh.
            const mine = normalize(message)
            if (recent.some((r) => normalize(r.message) === mine)) {
                return fail('validation', 'You already sent this wish. Try a new one.', 409)
            }
        }

        // ── Status awal ──
        const autoApprove =
            process.env.AUTO_APPROVE === 'true' &&
            !isPrivate &&
            !needsReview(message) &&
            !needsReview(displayName)

        // ── Simpan ──
        const res = await db('wishes', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { Prefer: 'return=representation' },
            body: {
                display_name: displayName,
                is_anonymous: isAnonymous,
                category,
                message,
                rating,
                is_private: isPrivate,
                status: autoApprove ? 'approved' : 'pending',
                ip_hash: ipHash,
            },
        })
        if (!res.ok) {
            let code = ''
            try { code = (await res.json())?.code || '' } catch (_) { /* abaikan */ }
            throw new Error(`insert failed (${res.status} ${code})`)
        }
        const rows = await res.json()
        return json(toRecord(rows[0]), 201, { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' })
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('[submit-wish]', err?.message || err) // tanpa isi pesan / IP
        return fail('server', 'The observatory is quiet right now. Please try again soon.', 500)
    }
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: netlify/lib/shared.js =====
// ============================================================
// COSMIC JOURNEY: helper bersama untuk Netlify Functions
// Service key hanya dibaca di sini (process.env), tidak pernah masuk bundle front-end.
// ============================================================
import { createHash, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto'

export const CATEGORY_IDS = ['feedback', 'suggestion', 'developer', 'wish']
export const MESSAGE_MIN = 5
export const MESSAGE_MAX = 280
export const NAME_MAX = 30

// ── Respons ──
export function json(body, status = 200, extraHeaders = {}) {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
        status,
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8', ...extraHeaders },
    })
}

// Bentuk error selalu: { error: { code, message } }, code = validation | rate_limit | server
export function fail(code, message, status) {
    return json({ error: { code, message } }, status, { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' })
}

// ── Akses Supabase (PostgREST) dengan service key ──
export function db(path, { method = 'GET', body, headers = {} } = {}) {
    const url = process.env.SUPABASE_URL
    const key = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY
    if (!url || !key) throw new Error('Missing SUPABASE_URL or SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY')

    const h = { apikey: key, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...headers }
    // Key lama (service_role) berupa JWT dan dikirim juga sebagai Bearer; key baru ("sb_secret_...") hanya lewat apikey.
    if (key.startsWith('eyJ')) h.Authorization = `Bearer ${key}`

    return fetch(`${url.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/rest/v1/${path}`, {
        method,
        headers: h,
        body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000),
    })
}

// Content-Range: "0-11/35" atau "*/0" → 35 / 0
export function totalFromRange(res) {
    const n = parseInt((res.headers.get('content-range') || '').split('/')[1], 10)
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0
}

// ── Pembersihan teks (sama dengan di front-end, plus karakter tak terlihat) ──
const INVISIBLE = /[\u200B-\u200F\u202A-\u202E\u2060-\u2069\uFEFF]/g

export const cleanName = (s) =>
    String(s ?? '')
        .replace(INVISIBLE, '')
        .replace(/[\u0000-\u001F\u007F-\u009F]/g, ' ')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        .trim()

export const cleanMessage = (s) =>
    String(s ?? '')
        .replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n')
        .replace(INVISIBLE, '')
        .replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F\u007F-\u009F]/g, '')
        .replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n')
        .trim()

// ── Filter kata kasar dasar (Inggris + Indonesia). Sengaja pendek; tambah sendiri bila perlu. ──
const BAD_WORDS = [
    'fuck', 'shit', 'bitch', 'cunt', 'nigger', 'faggot', 'whore',
    'anjing', 'bangsat', 'bajingan', 'kontol', 'memek', 'ngentot', 'jancok', 'tolol',
]
const LEET = { 0: 'o', 1: 'i', 3: 'e', 4: 'a', 5: 's', 7: 't', '@': 'a', $: 's' }

export function hasBadWords(text) {
    const t = String(text)
        .toLowerCase()
        .normalize('NFKD')
        .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
        .replace(/[013457@$]/g, (c) => LEET[c])
    return BAD_WORDS.some((w) => t.includes(w))
}

// Email / link / nomor telepon → tidak boleh auto-publish (tetap pending untuk dicek manual)
export function needsReview(text) {
    const t = String(text)
    return (
        /[\w.+-]+@[\w-]+\.[\w.-]+/.test(t) ||
        /https?:\/\/|www\./i.test(t) ||
        /\+?\d[\d\s().-]{8,}\d/.test(t)
    )
}

// ── Callsign cadangan bila nama kosong ──
const CALLSIGN_CHARS = '23456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ'
export function makeCallsign() {
    const bytes = randomBytes(3)
    return `Astronaut-${Array.from(bytes, (b) => CALLSIGN_CHARS[b % CALLSIGN_CHARS.length]).join('')}`
}

// ── Hash IP bergaram rahasia (IP mentah tidak pernah disimpan) ──
export function hashIp(ip) {
    if (!ip) return null
    const salt = process.env.IP_HASH_SALT || 'cosmic-journey'
    return createHash('sha256').update(`${salt}|${ip}`).digest('hex').slice(0, 32)
}
===== END FILE =====

import React, { useState, useCallback, useEffect, useRef, Suspense } from 'react'

// Lazy loading sections (kode three.js tidak akan diload di awal)
const Intro = React.lazy(() => import('./sections/Intro.jsx'))
const SolarSystem = React.lazy(() => import('./sections/SolarSystem.jsx'))

const SecretEnding = React.lazy(() => import('./sections/SecretEnding.jsx'))
const Gallery = React.lazy(() => import('./sections/Gallery.jsx'))
// HomeWarp memuat three.js, jadi lazy; dipanggil dari section mana pun lewat handleReturnHome
const HomeWarp = React.lazy(() => import('./components/HomeWarp.jsx'))

import { useAmbientAudio } from './hooks/useAudio.js'
import { sfx } from './hooks/useSfx.js'
import { UNIVERSE_MESSAGES } from './data/index.js'
import { resetSessionCallsign } from './services/wishes.js'

// Cadangan bila chunk gerbang gagal diunduh (mis. offline): lanjut tanpa gerbang dan tanpa suara,
// supaya pengunjung tidak terjebak di layar kosong.
function GateUnavailable({ onEnter, onExited }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    onEnter(false)
    onExited()
  }, [onEnter, onExited])
  return null
}

// Gerbang masuk dimuat lazy; hanya tampil sekali per kunjungan
const EntryGate = React.lazy(() =>
  import('./components/EntryGate.jsx').catch(() => ({ default: GateUnavailable }))
)

// Penutup polos selama chunk gerbang diunduh (mencegah Intro berkedip di bawahnya)
const GATE_FALLBACK = { position: 'fixed', inset: 0, zIndex: 200, background: 'var(--void)' }

// Petunjuk kecil bila suara tertahan (AudioContext suspended / interupsi / mode senyap iOS).
// Fase 12: diturunkan sedikit supaya tidak menimpa pill MusicPlayer mini di kanan atas.
const HINT_WRAP = {
  position: 'fixed',
  top: 'calc(max(0.75rem, env(safe-area-inset-top)) + 3.5rem)',
  left: 0,
  right: 0,
  zIndex: 150,
  display: 'flex',
  justifyContent: 'center',
  padding: '0 1rem',
  pointerEvents: 'none',
}
const HINT_BTN = {
  pointerEvents: 'auto',
  minHeight: '2.75rem',
  padding: '0.6rem 1.1rem',
  borderRadius: '1.25rem',
  border: '1px solid rgba(120, 160, 255, 0.5)',
  background: 'rgba(8, 8, 36, 0.92)',
  color: 'var(--text-primary)',
  fontSize: 'var(--fs-label)',
  fontWeight: 400,
  letterSpacing: '0.02em',
  lineHeight: 1.45,
  textAlign: 'center',
  boxShadow: '0 0 24px rgba(68, 102, 255, 0.25)',
  touchAction: 'manipulation',
}

// 'secret' (Secret Ending) SENGAJA tidak masuk daftar ini: bukan bagian navigasi bebas,
// hanya terbuka lewat submit wish yang sukses. NavDots/ProgressBar memakai 'wishing' saat di 'secret'.
const SECTION_IDS = ['intro', 'solar', 'phenomena', 'deepspace', 'ending', 'wishing', 'gallery']

// Kedalaman starfield per section (0 = terang penuh, 1 = paling redup).
// Semua id section, termasuk 'secret'. Id yang tidak ada di peta jatuh ke 0.
const SECTION_DEPTH = {
  intro: 0,
  solar: 0.2,
  phenomena: 0.4,
  wishing: 0.45,
  ending: 0.55,
  deepspace: 0.7,
  gallery: 0.8,
  secret: 0.8,
}

// Mode Comfort: geser satu tingkat lebih redup
const COMFORT_DEPTH_STEP = 0.15

// Section tersembunyi yang memakai musik section lain (tanpa crossfade putus).
// Juga dipakai untuk label MusicPlayer, supaya 'secret' tidak jatuh ke track default.
const MUSIC_ALIAS = { secret: 'wishing' }

// Dari section ini ke depan, chunk section akhir + HomeWarp diunduh di latar belakang
const PRELOAD_FROM = new Set(['deepspace', 'ending', 'wishing', 'secret', 'gallery'])

// State pengunjung (in-memory saja, sengaja TIDAK disimpan di localStorage/server)
// Diisi Ending saat reveal, dibaca Wishing, Secret Ending & Gallery. Direset setelah pulang ke Bumi.
const INITIAL_VISITOR = {
  name: '',                // nama penerima konstelasi
  sender: '',              // pengirim (hanya mode "For Someone Else")
  constellationPoints: [],
  finalMessage: '',
  coords: '',
  wishSubmitted: false,
  mySubmission: null,      // data kiriman wish pengunjung ini (untuk highlight "Your wish")
  skipped: false,          // true = Wishing di-skip langsung ke Gallery (tidak membuka Secret Ending)
  secretSeen: false,       // true = sinematik Secret Ending sudah selesai/di-skip (kunjungan ulang tampil langsung)
}

const HOME_END_PAD = 800 // jeda setelah pindah ke intro sebelum overlay HomeWarp dilepas
// (HomeWarp.jsx: T_LEAVE bergantung pada angka ini, lihat catatan di sana)

// Perjalanan Bumi (intro > solar): Bumi menjauh & mengecil ke posisinya di tata surya
const TRAVEL_MS = 2800 // dari tombol penuh sampai section berganti
const ARRIVE_MS = 1000 // jeda sampai UI global muncul lagi

// Transisi gelap-terang untuk perpindahan section selain intro > solar
const FADE_OUT = 700
const FADE_IN = 800

export default function App() {
  const [fact, setFact] = useState(null)
  const [message, setMessage] = useState(null)

  // Gerbang masuk: 'show' > 'leaving' (memudar) > 'done'. Tidak pernah kembali ke 'show'
  // dalam satu kunjungan, jadi HomeWarp / "Return home" tidak memunculkannya lagi.
  const [gate, setGate] = useState('show')
  const mainRef = useRef(null)

  const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState('intro')
  const [renderedSection, setRenderedSection] = useState('intro')
  const [fadeState, setFadeState] = useState('visible')
  const navTimer = useRef(0) // timer pergantian section (dibatalkan jika ada perpindahan baru)

  // Perjalanan intro > solar: 'idle' > 'warp' > 'idle'
  const [warp, setWarp] = useState({ phase: 'idle', startTime: 0 })
  const warpingRef = useRef(false)

  const [fadePhase, setFadePhase] = useState('idle')

  const [visitor, setVisitor] = useState(INITIAL_VISITOR)
  const updateVisitor = useCallback((patch) => setVisitor((v) => ({ ...v, ...patch })), [])
  // true = Ending langsung membuka pesan konstelasi (dari Secret Ending: "Stay with my constellation")
  const [resumeMessage, setResumeMessage] = useState(false)

  const [homeWarping, setHomeWarping] = useState(false)
  const homeTimers = useRef([])
  // true = HomeWarp sudah memberi tahu bahwa Bumi mendekat: bintang kembali terang (depth 0)
  const [homeBright, setHomeBright] = useState(false)
  const brightenStars = useCallback(() => setHomeBright(true), [])

  // Mode Comfort (Fase 12): tombolnya ada di MusicPlayer. State hidup di sini, TANPA localStorage
  // (kembali mati saat halaman dimuat ulang; tetap menyala setelah "Return home").
  // Efek: starfield satu tingkat lebih redup dan lebih tenang (calm), body.comfort-mode
  // (kontras teks naik, animasi CSS dihentikan), FactPopup tampil lebih lama, teks langsung tampil.
  const [comfort, setComfort] = useState(false)
  const toggleComfort = useCallback(() => setComfort((c) => !c), [])

  const {
    playing,
    toggleMusic,
    volume,
    setVolume,
    changeSection,
    getAudioData,
    audioProgress,
    startAudio,
    blocked: audioBlocked,
    resumeAudio,
  } = useAmbientAudio()

  // Ketukan gerbang. SEMUA yang butuh gestur (AudioContext sfx, musik) harus dipanggil
  // sinkron di sini, sebelum await / setTimeout apa pun, supaya iOS mengizinkannya.
  const handleGateEnter = useCallback((withSound) => {
    sfx.setMuted(!withSound)
    if (withSound) sfx.unlock()
    startAudio(withSound)
    setGate((g) => (g === 'show' ? 'leaving' : g))
  }, [startAudio])

  const handleGateExited = useCallback(() => setGate('done'), [])

  const handleNavigate = useCallback((id) => {
    setFact(null)
    setMessage(null)
    if (id === activeSection) return
    if (id !== 'ending') setResumeMessage(false)
    setActiveSection(id)
    setFadeState('hidden')
    changeSection(MUSIC_ALIAS[id] ?? id)

    // Perpindahan cepat beruntun: hanya tujuan terakhir yang dirender (tanpa kilatan section perantara)
    clearTimeout(navTimer.current)
    navTimer.current = setTimeout(() => {
      setRenderedSection(id)
      setFadeState('visible')
    }, 400)
  }, [activeSection, changeSection])

  // NavDots: diabaikan selama transisi / HomeWarp (tombol tetap bisa dijangkau keyboard walau tersembunyi)
  const handleNavDot = useCallback((id) => {
    if (warpingRef.current) return
    handleNavigate(id)
  }, [handleNavigate])

  // Dipanggil Intro saat tombol tahan penuh: Intro menganimasikan Bumi, App mengganti section
  const handleWarpNavigate = useCallback((id) => {
    if (warpingRef.current || id === activeSection) return
    warpingRef.current = true
    setFact(null)
    setMessage(null)
    setWarp({ phase: 'warp', startTime: Date.now() })

    setTimeout(() => {
      handleNavigate(id)
      setTimeout(() => {
        setWarp({ phase: 'idle', startTime: 0 })
        warpingRef.current = false
      }, ARRIVE_MS)
    }, TRAVEL_MS)
  }, [activeSection, handleNavigate])

  const handleFadeNavigate = useCallback((id) => {
    if (warpingRef.current || id === activeSection) return
    warpingRef.current = true
    setFact(null)
    setMessage(null)
    setFadePhase('dark')

    setTimeout(() => {
      handleNavigate(id)
      setFadePhase('light')

      setTimeout(() => {
        setFadePhase('idle')
        warpingRef.current = false
      }, FADE_IN)
    }, FADE_OUT)
  }, [activeSection, handleNavigate])

  // Pulang ke Bumi (HomeWarp): bisa dipanggil dari section mana pun (mis. Secret Ending).
  // Tidak menyentuh state gerbang, jadi gerbang tidak muncul lagi.
  const handleReturnHome = useCallback(async () => {
    if (warpingRef.current) return
    warpingRef.current = true
    setFact(null)
    setMessage(null)
    setHomeBright(false)
    try {
      const { HOME_MS } = await import('./components/HomeWarp.jsx')
      setHomeWarping(true)
      const t1 = setTimeout(() => {
        handleNavigate('intro')
        const t2 = setTimeout(() => {
          setHomeWarping(false)
          setHomeBright(false)        // Intro sudah depth 0, jadi tidak ada lompatan visual
          setVisitor(INITIAL_VISITOR) // perjalanan baru = pengunjung baru
          resetSessionCallsign()      // callsign anonim baru untuk pengunjung berikutnya
          warpingRef.current = false
        }, HOME_END_PAD)
        homeTimers.current.push(t2)
      }, HOME_MS)
      homeTimers.current.push(t1)
    } catch (_) {
      warpingRef.current = false
    }
  }, [handleNavigate])

  // Secret Ending > "Stay with my constellation"
  const handleBackToConstellation = useCallback(() => {
    setResumeMessage(true)
    handleFadeNavigate('ending')
  }, [handleFadeNavigate])

  // Wishing: wish berhasil terkirim dan animasi bintang naik selesai > Secret Ending
  const handleWishSubmitted = useCallback(() => {
    handleFadeNavigate('secret')
  }, [handleFadeNavigate])

  useEffect(() => () => {
    homeTimers.current.forEach(clearTimeout)
    clearTimeout(navTimer.current)
  }, [])

  // Selama gerbang tampil, konten di belakangnya tidak bisa difokus keyboard / pembaca layar
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = mainRef.current
    if (!el) return
    if (gate === 'show') el.setAttribute('inert', '')
    else el.removeAttribute('inert')
  }, [gate])

  const transitioning = warp.phase !== 'idle' || fadePhase !== 'idle' || homeWarping
  const hideUi = transitioning || gate !== 'done'
  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.classList.toggle('hide-global-ui', hideUi)
    return () => document.body.classList.remove('hide-global-ui')
  }, [hideUi])

  // Mode Comfort: kontras teks naik + gerak berkurang lewat body.comfort-mode (index.css).
  // FactPopup, MessageBox, dan Phenomena membaca class ini langsung.
  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.classList.toggle('comfort-mode', comfort)
    return () => document.body.classList.remove('comfort-mode')
  }, [comfort])

  // Preload Solar System supaya perpindahan dari intro tidak tersendat
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => import('./sections/SolarSystem.jsx'), 1500)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [])

  // Preload section akhir + HomeWarp begitu pengunjung mencapai Deep Space,
  // supaya layar tidak kosong saat pindah ke Ending / Wishing / Secret / Gallery / pulang.
  const preloaded = useRef(false)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (preloaded.current || !PRELOAD_FROM.has(activeSection)) return
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      preloaded.current = true
      const quiet = () => { }
      import('./sections/Ending.jsx').catch(quiet)
      import('./sections/Wishing.jsx').catch(quiet)
      import('./sections/SecretEnding.jsx').catch(quiet)
      import('./sections/Gallery.jsx').catch(quiet)
      import('./components/HomeWarp.jsx').catch(quiet)
    }, 1200)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [activeSection])

  const showFact = useCallback((d) => setFact(d), [])
  const closeFact = useCallback(() => setFact(null), [])

  const showMessage = useCallback(() => {
    setMessage(UNIVERSE_MESSAGES[Math.floor(Math.random() * UNIVERSE_MESSAGES.length)])
  }, [])
  const closeMessage = useCallback(() => setMessage(null), [])

  // Secret Ending tidak punya titik sendiri di NavDots/ProgressBar, jadi ikut 'wishing'
  const navSection = activeSection === 'secret' ? 'wishing' : activeSection
  const currentIdx = SECTION_IDS.indexOf(navSection)
  const progress = Math.max(0, currentIdx / (SECTION_IDS.length - 1))

  // Depth starfield mengikuti activeSection (berubah saat navigasi dimulai; StarField
  // meng-easing selama ~2 dtk sehingga tumpang tindih dengan fade section, tanpa lompatan).
  const baseDepth = SECTION_DEPTH[activeSection] ?? 0
  const starDepth = homeBright
    ? 0
    : Math.min(1, baseDepth + (comfort ? COMFORT_DEPTH_STEP : 0))

  return (
    <>
      <StarField count={500} mouseParallax depth={starDepth} calm={comfort} />
      <ProgressBar progress={progress} />
      <NavDots active={navSection} onNavigate={handleNavDot} />

      <MusicPlayer
        playing={playing}
        onToggle={toggleMusic}
        volume={volume}
        onVolumeChange={setVolume}
        activeSection={MUSIC_ALIAS[activeSection] ?? activeSection}
        getAudioData={getAudioData}
        audioProgress={audioProgress}
        comfort={comfort}
        onToggleComfort={toggleComfort}
      />

      <div
        className={`fade-overlay ${fadePhase === 'dark' ? 'fade-dark' : ''} ${fadePhase === 'light' ? 'fade-light' : ''}`}
        style={{ '--fade-out': `${FADE_OUT}ms`, '--fade-in': `${FADE_IN}ms` }}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />

      <main ref={mainRef} className={`section-wrapper ${fadeState}`}>
        <Suspense fallback={null}>
          {renderedSection === 'intro' && (
            <Intro
              onNext={() => handleWarpNavigate('solar')}
              isWarping={warp.phase !== 'idle'}
              warpStartTime={warp.startTime}
              travelMs={TRAVEL_MS}
            />
          )}
          {renderedSection === 'solar' && (
            <SolarSystem onFact={showFact} onNext={() => handleFadeNavigate('phenomena')} />
          )}
          {renderedSection === 'phenomena' && (
            <Phenomena onFact={showFact} onNext={() => handleFadeNavigate('deepspace')} />
          )}
          {renderedSection === 'deepspace' && (
            <DeepSpace onFact={showFact} onNext={() => handleFadeNavigate('ending')} />
          )}
          {renderedSection === 'ending' && (
            <Ending
              visitor={visitor}
              onReveal={updateVisitor}
              onNext={() => handleFadeNavigate('wishing')}
              onBackToChoices={() => handleFadeNavigate('secret')}
              resumeMessage={resumeMessage}
            />
          )}
          {renderedSection === 'wishing' && (
            <Wishing
              visitor={visitor}
              onVisitorChange={updateVisitor}
              onGallery={() => handleFadeNavigate('gallery')}
              onSubmitted={handleWishSubmitted}
              onBackToConstellation={handleBackToConstellation}
              onReturnHome={handleReturnHome}
            />
          )}
          {renderedSection === 'secret' && (
            <SecretEnding
              visitor={visitor}
              onVisitorChange={updateVisitor}
              onStay={handleBackToConstellation}
              onGallery={() => handleFadeNavigate('gallery')}
              onReturnHome={handleReturnHome}
            />
          )}
          {renderedSection === 'gallery' && (
            <Gallery
              visitor={visitor}
              onBackToConstellation={handleBackToConstellation}
              onReturnHome={handleReturnHome}
            />
          )}
        </Suspense>
      </main>

      {homeWarping && (
        <Suspense fallback={null}>
          <HomeWarp
            active
            name={visitor.name}
            points={visitor.constellationPoints}
            onBrighten={brightenStars}
          />
        </Suspense>
      )}

      <FactPopup fact={fact} onClose={closeFact} />
      <MessageBox message={message} onClose={closeMessage} />

      {audioBlocked && gate === 'done' && (
        <div style={HINT_WRAP} role="status">
          <button type="button" style={HINT_BTN} onClick={resumeAudio}>
            Sound is paused. Tap to turn it on.
            <br />
            On iPhone, also check the silent switch.
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {gate !== 'done' && (
        <Suspense fallback={<div style={GATE_FALLBACK} aria-hidden="true" />}>
          <EntryGate
            onEnter={handleGateEnter}
            leaving={gate === 'leaving'}
            onExited={handleGateExited}
          />
        </Suspense>
      )}
    </>
  )
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/components/EntryGate.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react'
import styles from './EntryGate.module.css'

const STAR_COUNT = 36
const EXIT_MS = 700
const EXIT_MS_REDUCED = 150

const prefersReduced = () =>
    typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
    window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches

// Bintang gerbang dibuat sekali per mount (murni CSS, tanpa canvas)
function makeStars() {
    return Array.from({ length: STAR_COUNT }, () => ({
        x: Math.random() * 100,
        y: Math.random() * 100,
        size: 1 + Math.random() * 2,
        dur: 2.4 + Math.random() * 3.2,
        delay: -Math.random() * 5,
        o: 0.45 + Math.random() * 0.55,
    }))
}

// onEnter(true)  = masuk dengan suara (HARUS dipanggil sinkron di handler ketukan)
// onEnter(false) = masuk tanpa suara
// leaving        = App sudah menerima ketukan, gerbang memudar lalu memanggil onExited
export default function EntryGate({ onEnter, leaving = false, onExited }) {
    const stars = useMemo(makeStars, [])
    const rootRef = useRef(null)
    const boardRef = useRef(null)
    const exitedRef = useRef(onExited)
    exitedRef.current = onExited

    useEffect(() => {
        boardRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true })
    }, [])

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!leaving) return
        const t = setTimeout(
            () => exitedRef.current?.(),
            prefersReduced() ? EXIT_MS_REDUCED : EXIT_MS
        )
        return () => clearTimeout(t)
    }, [leaving])

    // Fokus dikunci di dalam gerbang (dua tombol saja)
    const onKeyDown = (e) => {
        if (e.key !== 'Tab') return
        const items = rootRef.current?.querySelectorAll('button')
        if (!items || items.length === 0) return
        const first = items[0]
        const last = items[items.length - 1]
        if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
            e.preventDefault()
            last.focus()
        } else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
            e.preventDefault()
            first.focus()
        }
    }

    const choose = (withSound) => {
        if (leaving) return
        onEnter(withSound)
    }

    return (
        <div
            ref={rootRef}
            className={`${styles.gate} ${leaving ? styles.leaving : ''}`}
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-labelledby="gate-title"
            aria-describedby="gate-note"
            onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
        >
            <div className={styles.sky} aria-hidden="true">
                {stars.map((s, i) => (
                    <span
                        key={i}
                        className={styles.star}
                        style={{
                            left: `${s.x}%`,
                            top: `${s.y}%`,
                            width: `${s.size}px`,
                            height: `${s.size}px`,
                            '--d': `${s.dur}s`,
                            '--delay': `${s.delay}s`,
                            '--o': s.o,
                        }}
                    />
                ))}
            </div>

            <div className={styles.content}>
                <div className={styles.core} aria-hidden="true">
                    <span className={styles.coreRing} />
                    <span className={styles.coreDot} />
                </div>

                <h2 id="gate-title" className={styles.kicker}>Cosmic Journey</h2>

                <button
                    ref={boardRef}
                    type="button"
                    className={styles.boardBtn}
                    onClick={() => choose(true)}
                >
                    Board the ship
                </button>

                <p id="gate-note" className={styles.note}>Best with sound on</p>

                <button
                    type="button"
                    className={styles.quiet}
                    onClick={() => choose(false)}
                >
                    Continue without sound
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/components/EntryGate.module.css =====
.gate {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    z-index: 200;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    padding:
        max(1.5rem, env(safe-area-inset-top)) 1.25rem max(1.5rem, env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
    background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 55%, #0a0a2e 0%, var(--void) 70%);
    overflow: hidden;
    text-align: center;
    transition: opacity 0.7s ease-in-out;
}

.leaving {
    opacity: 0;
}

.sky {
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
}

.star {
    position: absolute;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: var(--text-primary);
    opacity: var(--o);
    animation: twinkle var(--d) ease-in-out var(--delay) infinite alternate;
}

    from {
        opacity: calc(var(--o) * 0.25);
        transform: scale(0.8);
    }

    to {
        opacity: var(--o);
        transform: scale(1.15);
    }
}

    z-index: 1;
    display: flex;

}

}

    min-height: 3.25rem;
    padding: 0.9rem 2.6rem;
    border: 1px solid rgba(120, 160, 255, 0.6);
    border-radius: 50px;
    background: rgba(8, 8, 36, 0.6);
    color: var(--accent-blue-text);
    font-family: var(--font-body);
    font-size: 1rem;
    font-weight: var(--fw-regular);
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    box-shadow: 0 0 28px rgba(68, 102, 255, 0.25);
    transition: border-color 0.3s, box-shadow 0.3s, transform 0.15s;
    touch-action: manipulation;
    -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
}

    border-color: rgba(160, 190, 255, 0.85);
    box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(68, 102, 255, 0.4);
}

    transform: scale(0.97);
}

.quiet:focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid #aaccff;
    outline-offset: 4px;
}

.note {
    margin: 0;
    font-family: var(--font-body);
    font-size: var(--fs-label);
    font-weight: var(--fw-regular);
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    color: var(--text-muted);
}

.quiet {
    min-height: 2.75rem;
    padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
    font-family: var(--font-body);
    font-size: var(--fs-label);
    font-weight: var(--fw-regular);
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    color: var(--text-muted);
    text-decoration: underline;
    text-underline-offset: 4px;
    touch-action: manipulation;
    -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {

    .star,
    .coreDot,
    .coreRing {
        animation: none;
    }

    .coreRing {
        display: none;
    }

    .gate {
        transition-duration: 0.15s;
    }
}

===== FILE: src/components/FactPopup.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react'
import styles from './FactPopup.module.css'

// Durasi tampil menyesuaikan panjang teks (±60 ms per karakter), batas 6 sampai 22 detik.
// Mode Comfort (body.comfort-mode) memberi waktu 1,5× lebih lama.
const BASE_MS = 3000
const PER_CHAR_MS = 60
const MIN_MS = 6000
const MAX_MS = 22000
// Setelah tertahan (hover / sentuh / fokus) lalu dilepas, sisa waktu minimal segini
const MIN_RESUME_MS = 3000

function readDuration(fact) {
  const len = [fact.title, fact.subtitle, fact.text, fact.extra].reduce(
    (n, s) => n + (typeof s === 'string' ? s.length : 0),
    0
  )
  const comfort = document.body.classList.contains('comfort-mode')
  const ms = Math.min(MAX_MS, Math.max(MIN_MS, BASE_MS + len * PER_CHAR_MS))
  return comfort ? ms * 1.5 : ms
}

export default function FactPopup({ fact, onClose }) {
  const timerRef = useRef(0)
  const remainRef = useRef(0)   // sisa waktu (ms)
  const startedRef = useRef(0)  // kapan timer terakhir dijalankan
  const holdRef = useRef({ pointer: false, focus: false })
  const onCloseRef = useRef(onClose)

  useEffect(() => {
    onCloseRef.current = onClose
  }, [onClose])

  const isHeld = () => holdRef.current.pointer || holdRef.current.focus

  const schedule = useCallback(() => {
    clearTimeout(timerRef.current)
    startedRef.current = Date.now()
    timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => onCloseRef.current(), remainRef.current)
  }, [])

  // Fakta baru: hitung durasi dan mulai. Jika pointer sedang menahan popup, timer ditunda
  // sampai dilepas. Saat popup tertutup, status tahan direset (pointer events mati saat tersembunyi).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!fact) {
      holdRef.current = { pointer: false, focus: false }
      return
    }
    remainRef.current = readDuration(fact)
    if (!isHeld()) schedule()
    return () => clearTimeout(timerRef.current)
  }, [fact, schedule])

  const hold = (key) => {
    if (!fact) return
    const wasHeld = isHeld()
    holdRef.current[key] = true
    if (!wasHeld) {
      clearTimeout(timerRef.current)
      remainRef.current -= Date.now() - startedRef.current
    }
  }

  const release = (key) => {
    if (!fact) return
    holdRef.current[key] = false
    if (!isHeld()) {
      remainRef.current = Math.max(remainRef.current, MIN_RESUME_MS)
      schedule()
    }
  }

  return (
    <div
      className={`${styles.popup} ${fact ? styles.show : ''}`}
      role="status"
      onPointerEnter={() => hold('pointer')}
      onPointerLeave={() => release('pointer')}
      onPointerCancel={() => release('pointer')}
      onFocus={() => hold('focus')}
      onBlur={() => release('focus')}
    >
      {fact && (
        <>
          <div className={styles.header}>
            <span className={styles.title}>{fact.title}</span>
            {fact.subtitle && <span className={styles.subtitle}>{fact.subtitle}</span>}
            <button className={styles.close} onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">×</button>
          </div>
          <p className={styles.text}>{fact.text}</p>
          {fact.extra && <p className={styles.extra}>{fact.extra}</p>}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/components/HomeWarp.jsx =====
import { useRef, useState, useEffect, useMemo, memo } from 'react'
import { Canvas, useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber'
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { sfx } from '../hooks/useSfx.js'
import styles from './HomeWarp.module.css'

// Timeline (ms)
const T_LAUNCH = 1800    // roket mulai naik, warp mulai
const T_COUNT = 10000    // hitung mundur "3" (lalu 2, 1 tiap 900 ms)
const T_FLASH = 12700    // kilatan menuju Bumi
export const HOME_MS = 13200 // dipakai App sebelum pindah ke Intro
// Overlay mulai memudar saat Intro tampil di belakangnya.
// App melepas overlay di HOME_MS + HOME_END_PAD (800 ms): fade 400 ms ini harus selesai sebelum itu.
const T_LEAVE = HOME_MS + 400

const COUNT = 360
const COUNT_REDUCED = 160
const clamp01 = (v) => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, v))

// Hanya starfield (garis bintang saat warp)
function Scene({ t0, reduce }) {
    const count = reduce ? COUNT_REDUCED : COUNT
    const stars = useRef()
    const mat = useRef()
    const dummy = useMemo(() => new THREE.Object3D(), [])
    const ps = useMemo(() => Array.from({ length: count }, () => ({
        x: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40,
        y: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60,
        z: Math.random() * 13 - 14,
    })), [count])

    useFrame((state, delta) => {
        if (!t0.current || !stars.current) return
        const t = performance.now() - t0.current
        const k = clamp01((t - T_LAUNCH) / 6000)
        const s = k * k * (3 - 2 * k)

        // Bintang makin terang mengikuti hitung mundur, puncaknya saat kilatan (0,8 → 1)
        if (mat.current) {
            const b = clamp01((t - T_COUNT) / (T_FLASH - T_COUNT))
            mat.current.opacity = 0.8 + 0.2 * b * b
        }

        // Reduced motion: tanpa getaran kamera, garis bintang pendek dan lambat
        if (!reduce) {
            const amp = s * s * 0.14
            state.camera.position.x = (Math.random() - 0.5) * amp
            state.camera.position.y = (Math.random() - 0.5) * amp
        }

        const speed = 0.2 + s * (reduce ? 12 : 60)
        const stretch = 1 + s * (reduce ? 8 : 110)
        ps.forEach((p, i) => {
            p.y -= speed * delta
            if (p.y < -30) { p.y = 30; p.x = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40 }
            dummy.position.set(p.x, p.y, p.z)
            dummy.scale.set(1, stretch, 1)
            dummy.updateMatrix()
            stars.current.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix)
        })
        stars.current.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true
    })

    return (
        <instancedMesh ref={stars} args={[null, null, count]} frustumCulled={false}>
            <boxGeometry args={[0.025, 0.025, 0.025]} />
            <meshBasicMaterial ref={mat} color="#e0f0ff" transparent opacity={0.8} />
        </instancedMesh>
    )
}

// Roket 2D: mengapung & bergoyang → api menyala → berdiri tegak & naik perlahan
function Rocket2D({ t0, reduce }) {
    const body = useRef()
    const flame = useRef()

    useEffect(() => {
        let raf = 0
        const j = reduce ? 0 : 1   // 0 = tanpa goyang, getar, dan kedip api
        const loop = () => {
            const t = performance.now() - t0.current
            const lk = clamp01((t - T_LAUNCH) / (T_FLASH - T_LAUNCH - 800))
            const rise = lk * lk * (3 - 2 * lk)                       // naik 0..1
            const up = clamp01((t - T_LAUNCH) / 1400)
            const upright = up * up * (3 - 2 * up)                    // tegak 0..1
            const ig = clamp01((t - T_LAUNCH) / 900)                  // api menyala 0..1
            const sway = Math.sin(t * 0.0016) * 10 * (1 - upright) * j
            const bob = Math.sin(t * 0.0021) * 10 * (1 - upright) * j
            const shake = ig * (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.6 * j
            const y = bob - rise * window.innerHeight * 0.3
            if (body.current) {
                body.current.style.transform =
                    `translate(${shake}px, ${y}px) rotate(${sway + (1 - upright) * 6 * j}deg)`
            }
            if (flame.current) {
                const len = ig * (0.75 + rise * 0.6) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.16 * ig * j
                flame.current.style.transform = `scale(${1 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1 * ig * j}, ${Math.max(len, 0.001)})`
            }
            raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop)
        }
        raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop)
        return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf)
    }, [t0, reduce])

    return (
        <div className={styles.rocketWrap} aria-hidden="true">
            <div ref={body} className={styles.rocket}>
                <svg viewBox="0 0 80 150" className={styles.rocketSvg}>
                    <g ref={flame} className={styles.flame} style={{ transformOrigin: '40px 108px' }}>
                        <path d="M32 108 C32 128 38 142 40 152 C42 142 48 128 48 108Z" fill="#ff8a30" opacity="0.9" />
                        <path d="M36 108 C36 120 39 130 40 138 C41 130 44 120 44 108Z" fill="#fff2b0" />
                    </g>
                    <path d="M20 74 L4 104 L20 96Z" fill="#ff5a5a" />
                    <path d="M60 74 L76 104 L60 96Z" fill="#ff5a5a" />
                    <path d="M30 98 L50 98 L47 109 L33 109Z" fill="#444b5e" />
                    <path d="M40 4 C58 22 62 50 60 98 L20 98 C18 50 22 22 40 4Z" fill="#e8ecf8" />
                    <path d="M40 4 C50 13 56 25 58 38 L22 38 C24 25 30 13 40 4Z" fill="#ff5a5a" />
                    <circle cx="40" cy="58" r="9" fill="#6ec5ff" stroke="#aab4cc" strokeWidth="3" />
                    <circle cx="37" cy="55" r="2.5" fill="#fff" opacity="0.7" />
                </svg>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}

// Konstelasi nama pengguna terbentuk di langit saat roket naik
function NameConstellation({ name, points }) {
    if (!points?.length) return null
    const xs = points.map((p) => p.x)
    const ys = points.map((p) => p.y)
    const pad = 14
    const minX = Math.min(...xs) - pad
    const minY = Math.min(...ys) - pad
    const w = Math.max(...xs) - Math.min(...xs) + pad * 2
    const h = Math.max(...ys) - Math.min(...ys) + pad * 2
    const d = points.map((p, i) => `${i ? 'L' : 'M'}${p.x} ${p.y}`).join(' ')
    return (
        <div className={styles.sky}>
            <svg viewBox={`${minX} ${minY} ${w} ${h}`} className={styles.skySvg} aria-hidden="true">
                <path d={d} pathLength="1" className={styles.skyLine} />
                {points.map((p, i) => (
                    <g key={i} className={styles.skyStar} style={{ animationDelay: `${0.6 + i * 0.5}s` }}>
                        <circle cx={p.x} cy={p.y} r="6" className={styles.skyHalo} />
                        <circle cx={p.x} cy={p.y} r="2" fill="#fff" />
                    </g>
                ))}
            </svg>
            <p className={styles.skyLabel}>✦ {name}’s star</p>
            <p className={styles.skyNote}>It will keep watch over your home, always.</p>
        </div>
    )
}

// onBrighten: dipanggil sekali saat hitung mundur dimulai (Bumi mendekat).
// App menaikkan StarField ke depth 0; easing ~2 dtk membuatnya terang penuh saat kilatan.
function HomeWarp({ active, name = '', points = [], onBrighten }) {
    const [phase, setPhase] = useState('float') // float | launch | count | flash
    const [count, setCount] = useState(0)
    const [leaving, setLeaving] = useState(false)
    const t0 = useRef(performance.now())
    const overlayRef = useRef(null)
    const brightenRef = useRef(onBrighten)
    brightenRef.current = onBrighten

    const reduce = useMemo(
        () => typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches,
        []
    )

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!active) return
        t0.current = performance.now()
        setPhase('float')
        setCount(0)
        setLeaving(false)
        const ids = [
            setTimeout(() => { setPhase('launch'); sfx.warp((T_FLASH - T_LAUNCH) / 1000 + 0.4) }, T_LAUNCH),
            ...[3, 2, 1].map((n, i) =>
                setTimeout(() => {
                    setPhase('count'); setCount(n); sfx.click()
                    if (i === 0) brightenRef.current?.()
                }, T_COUNT + i * 900)
            ),
            setTimeout(() => { setPhase('flash'); sfx.chime(4) }, T_FLASH),
            setTimeout(() => setLeaving(true), T_LEAVE),
        ]
        return () => ids.forEach(clearTimeout)
    }, [active])

    // Fokus pindah ke overlay: tombol "Return home" di belakangnya tidak lagi menerima Enter/Spasi
    useEffect(() => {
        if (active) overlayRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true })
    }, [active])

    if (!active) return null

    const nearEarth = phase === 'count' || phase === 'flash'

    return (
        <div
            ref={overlayRef}
            className={`${styles.overlay} ${leaving ? styles.overlayOut : ''}`}
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-label="Returning home"
            tabIndex={-1}
            onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Tab') e.preventDefault() }}
        >
            <div className={styles.canvasWrap} aria-hidden="true">
                <Canvas dpr={[1, 1.5]} camera={{ position: [0, 0, 10], fov: 60 }}>
                    <Scene t0={t0} reduce={reduce} />
                </Canvas>
            </div>

            {phase !== 'float' && <NameConstellation name={name} points={points} />}
            <Rocket2D t0={t0} reduce={reduce} />

            <div className={`${styles.earthHorizon} ${nearEarth ? styles.earthHorizonOn : ''}`} aria-hidden="true" />

            <div className={styles.caption} role="status" aria-live="polite">
                {phase === 'float' && <p key="f" className={styles.line}>Adrift between the stars…</p>}
                {phase === 'launch' && <p key="l" className={styles.line}>Engines awake. Taking you home{name ? `, ${name}` : ''}.</p>}
                {phase === 'count' && (
                    <>
                        <p className={styles.small}>Arriving at Earth in</p>
                        <div key={count} className={styles.count}>{count}</div>
                    </>
                )}
            </div>

            <div className={`${styles.flash} ${phase === 'flash' ? styles.flashOn : ''}`} aria-hidden="true" />
        </div>
    )
}

export default memo(HomeWarp)
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/components/MessageBox.jsx =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import styles from './MessageBox.module.css'

// Reduced motion atau mode Comfort: teks langsung tampil utuh, tanpa efek ketik
const instantPref = () =>
  typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
  (window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ||
    document.body.classList.contains('comfort-mode'))

const HINT_STYLE = {
  marginTop: '1.25rem',
  fontFamily: 'var(--font-body)',
  fontSize: 'var(--fs-small)',
  fontWeight: 400,
  letterSpacing: '0.08em',
  color: 'var(--text-muted)',
}

export default function MessageBox({ message, onClose }) {
  const [displayed, setDisplayed] = useState('')
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false)
  const timerRef = useRef(0)
  const overlayRef = useRef(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    clearInterval(timerRef.current)
    if (!message) { setDisplayed(''); setDone(false); return }

    if (instantPref()) {
      setDisplayed(message)
      setDone(true)
      return
    }

    setDisplayed('')
    setDone(false)

    let i = 0
    timerRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      if (i < message.length) {
        setDisplayed(message.slice(0, i + 1))
        i++
      } else {
        setDone(true)
        clearInterval(timerRef.current)
      }
    }, 24)

    return () => clearInterval(timerRef.current)
  }, [message])

  // Fokus ke overlay saat terbuka, supaya Esc dan Enter/Spasi langsung bekerja
  useEffect(() => {
    if (message) overlayRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true })
  }, [message])

  // Tampilkan seluruh teks sekarang (hentikan interval supaya tidak menimpa lagi)
  const reveal = useCallback(() => {
    if (!message) return
    clearInterval(timerRef.current)
    setDisplayed(message)
    setDone(true)
  }, [message])

  const handleBackdrop = (e) => {
    if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose()
  }

  const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      e.preventDefault()
      onClose()
      return
    }
    // Enter/Spasi pada overlay (bukan pada tombol di dalamnya) = tampilkan semua teks
    if ((e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && e.target === e.currentTarget && !done) {
      e.preventDefault()
      reveal()
    }
  }

  if (!message) return null

  return (
    <div
      ref={overlayRef}
      className={styles.overlay}
      onClick={handleBackdrop}
      onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
      tabIndex={-1}
      style={{ outline: 'none' }}
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label="Message from the universe"
    >
      <div
        className={styles.card}
        onClick={() => { if (!done) reveal() }}
        style={done ? undefined : { cursor: 'pointer' }}
      >
        <div className={styles.deco}>✦</div>
        <p className={styles.label}>A message from the universe</p>
        <p className={styles.text}>
          {displayed}
          {!done && <span className={styles.cursor} />}
        </p>
        {!done && (
          <p style={HINT_STYLE} aria-hidden="true">Tap anywhere to show all</p>
        )}
        {done && (
          <button className={styles.closeBtn} onClick={onClose}>
            Return to the cosmos
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/components/MusicPlayer.jsx =====
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import styles from './MusicPlayer.module.css'

const TRACK_INFO = {
  intro: { title: 'Departure', artist: 'Leaving Earth', color: ['#4466ff', '#8844ff'], theme: 'nebula' },
  solar: { title: 'Solar Winds', artist: 'The Solar System', color: ['#ffaa33', '#ff6644'], theme: 'solar' },
  phenomena: { title: 'Event Horizon', artist: 'Cosmic Phenomena', color: ['#ff44aa', '#aa22ff'], theme: 'blackhole' },
  deepspace: { title: 'Into the Void', artist: 'Deep Space', color: ['#4488cc', '#224488'], theme: 'stars' },
  ending: { title: 'Stardust', artist: 'Reflection Zone', color: ['#88aaff', '#cc88ff'], theme: 'clouds' },
  wishing: { title: 'Stardust', artist: 'Make a Wish', color: ['#88aaff', '#cc88ff'], theme: 'stars' },
  gallery: { title: 'Departure', artist: 'Astronaut Gallery', color: ['#4466ff', '#8844ff'], theme: 'nebula' },
  default: { title: 'Departure', artist: 'Leaving Earth', color: ['#4466ff', '#8844ff'], theme: 'nebula' },
}

const prefersReduced = () =>
  typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
  window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches

// HP / layar sempit: player mulai dalam bentuk mini supaya tidak menutupi judul section
const isNarrow = () =>
  typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
  window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)').matches

function getAudioMetrics(data) {
  if (!data || data.length === 0) return { bass: 0, avg: 0, mid: 0 }
  const bassEnd = Math.floor(data.length * 0.06)
  const midEnd = Math.floor(data.length * 0.35)
  let bass = 0, mid = 0, total = 0
  for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    const v = data[i] / 255
    total += v
    if (i < bassEnd) bass += v
    else if (i < midEnd) mid += v
  }
  return { bass: bass / bassEnd, mid: mid / (midEnd - bassEnd), avg: total / data.length }
}

function fmt(sec) {
  if (!sec || isNaN(sec)) return '0:00'
  const m = Math.floor(sec / 60)
  const s = Math.floor(sec % 60).toString().padStart(2, '0')
  return `${m}:${s}`
}

// ── Tombol mode Comfort (dipakai di mini, penuh, dan dock) ──────────
function ComfortButton({ comfort, onToggle }) {
  if (!onToggle) return null
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      className={`${styles.comfortBtn} ${comfort ? styles.comfortOn : ''}`}
      onClick={onToggle}
      aria-pressed={comfort}
      aria-label="Comfort mode"
      title="Comfort mode: dimmer stars, softer text, less motion"
    >
      <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true">
        <path d="M21 12.8A9 9 0 1 1 11.2 3a7 7 0 0 0 9.8 9.8z" />
      </svg>
    </button>
  )
}

// ── Animated artwork canvas per theme (IDLE ANIMATION) ──────────────
// still = true (reduced motion / Comfort): digambar sekali, tanpa loop
function ArtCanvas({ theme, color, still }) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null)
  const rafRef = useRef(null)
  const t0 = useRef(Date.now())
  const particles = useRef([])

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current
    if (!canvas) return
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
    const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height

    const initParticles = () => {
      particles.current = []
      if (theme === 'stars' || theme === 'nebula' || theme === 'clouds') {
        for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
          particles.current.push({
            x: Math.random() * W, y: Math.random() * H,
            r: Math.random() * 1.5 + 0.3,
            speed: Math.random() * 0.3 + 0.05,
            phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
            opacity: Math.random() * 0.8 + 0.2,
          })
        }
      }
    }
    initParticles()

    const draw = () => {
      const t = (Date.now() - t0.current) / 1000
      const c1 = color[0], c2 = color[1]

      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H)

      if (theme === 'nebula') {
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
          const cx = W * (0.3 + i * 0.2) + Math.sin(t * 0.3 + i) * 8
          const cy = H * (0.4 + Math.sin(t * 0.2 + i * 1.2) * 0.2)
          const r = 22 + Math.sin(t + i) * 4
          const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, r)
          g.addColorStop(0, c1 + (i === 1 ? 'cc' : '88'))
          g.addColorStop(0.5, c2 + '44')
          g.addColorStop(1, 'transparent')
          ctx.fillStyle = g
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill()
        }
        particles.current.forEach(p => {
          p.phase += 0.02
          const a = 0.4 + Math.abs(Math.sin(p.phase)) * 0.6
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2)
          ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(200,220,255,${a})`; ctx.fill()
        })
      }

      if (theme === 'solar') {
        const cx = W * 0.5, cy = H * 0.5
        const pulseR = 14
        for (let ring = 3; ring >= 0; ring--) {
          const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, pulseR + ring * 8)
          g.addColorStop(0, ring === 0 ? '#ffffff' : c1 + 'ee')
          g.addColorStop(0.4, c1 + (ring === 0 ? 'cc' : '66'))
          g.addColorStop(1, 'transparent')
          ctx.fillStyle = g
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, pulseR + ring * 8, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill()
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
          const angle = t * 0.4 + i * Math.PI / 3
          const len = 10 + Math.sin(t + i) * 4
          const x2 = cx + Math.cos(angle) * (pulseR + len)
          const y2 = cy + Math.sin(angle) * (pulseR + len)
          ctx.strokeStyle = c1 + '88'
          ctx.lineWidth = 1.5
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx, cy); ctx.lineTo(x2, y2); ctx.stroke()
        }
      }

      if (theme === 'blackhole') {
        const cx = W * 0.5, cy = H * 0.5
        const coreR = 10
        for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) {
          const rx = coreR + 6 + i * 4
          const ry = (coreR + 6 + i * 4) * 0.28
          const alpha = (0.6 - i * 0.08) * 0.7
          ctx.save()
          ctx.translate(cx, cy)
          ctx.rotate(t * 0.5)
          ctx.strokeStyle = i < 2 ? c1 + 'ff' : c2 + Math.floor(alpha * 255).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')
          ctx.lineWidth = i < 2 ? 2 : 1
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 0, rx, ry, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2)
          ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore()
        }
        const cg = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, coreR)
        cg.addColorStop(0, '#000000'); cg.addColorStop(0.6, '#110008'); cg.addColorStop(1, c1 + '44')
        ctx.fillStyle = cg
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, coreR, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill()
      }

      if (theme === 'stars') {
        particles.current.forEach(p => {
          p.x -= p.speed * 0.8
          if (p.x < 0) { p.x = W; p.y = Math.random() * H }
          p.phase += 0.015
          const a = 0.3 + Math.abs(Math.sin(p.phase)) * 0.7
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2)
          ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(180,210,255,${a})`; ctx.fill()
        })
        const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(W * 0.3, H * 0.5, 0, W * 0.3, H * 0.5, W * 0.6)
        g.addColorStop(0, c1 + '22'); g.addColorStop(1, 'transparent')
        ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H)
      }

      if (theme === 'clouds') {
        const cloudDefs = [
          { x: 0.2, y: 0.35, r: 18, speed: 0.20 },
          { x: 0.55, y: 0.55, r: 22, speed: 0.15 },
          { x: 0.8, y: 0.3, r: 14, speed: 0.25 },
        ]
        cloudDefs.forEach((cl, i) => {
          const cx = (cl.x + Math.sin(t * cl.speed + i) * 0.15) * W
          const cy = (cl.y + Math.cos(t * cl.speed * 0.15 + i) * 0.16) * H
          const r = cl.r
          for (let b = 3; b >= 0; b--) {
            const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, r + b * 5)
            g.addColorStop(0, `rgba(200,210,255,${0.18 - b * 0.04})`)
            g.addColorStop(1, 'transparent')
            ctx.fillStyle = g
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r + b * 5, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill()
          }
        })
        particles.current.forEach(p => {
          p.phase += 0.008
          const a = 0.2 + Math.abs(Math.sin(p.phase)) * 0.5
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r * 0.8, 0, Math.PI * 2)
          ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(220,225,255,${a})`; ctx.fill()
        })
      }

      if (!still) rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(draw)
    }

    draw()
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current)
  }, [theme, color, still])

  return <canvas ref={canvasRef} width={56} height={56} className={styles.artCanvas} />
}

// ── Bar visualizer ─────────────────────────────────────────
// still: saat idle gambar garis datar sekali (tanpa gelombang bergerak)
function BarVisualizer({ getAudioData, playing, color, onMetrics, still }) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null)
  const rafRef = useRef(null)
  const t0 = useRef(Date.now())
  const freqRef = useRef(new Uint8Array(128))

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current
    if (!canvas) return
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
    const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height
    const BINS = 28
    const freqData = freqRef.current

    const draw = () => {
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H)
      const t = (Date.now() - t0.current) / 1000

      if (!getAudioData || !playing) {
        ctx.strokeStyle = color[0] + '55'
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.5
        ctx.beginPath()
        for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
          const y = still
            ? H / 2
            : H / 2 + Math.sin(x * 0.18 + t * 1.2) * 3.5 + Math.sin(x * 0.08 + t * 0.7) * 2
          x === 0 ? ctx.moveTo(x, y) : ctx.lineTo(x, y)
        }
        ctx.stroke()
        if (onMetrics) onMetrics(null)
        if (!still) rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(draw)
        return
      }

      getAudioData(freqData)
      if (onMetrics) onMetrics(freqData)

      const step = Math.floor(freqData.length / BINS)
      const barW = (W / BINS) - 1

      const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, H, 0, 0)
      grad.addColorStop(0, color[0] + 'dd')
      grad.addColorStop(0.6, color[1] + 'aa')
      grad.addColorStop(1, '#ffffff44')

      for (let i = 0; i < BINS; i++) {
        let sum = 0
        for (let j = 0; j < step; j++) sum += (freqData[i * step + j] || 0)
        const v = (sum / step) / 255
        const barH = Math.max(2, v * H * 1.15)
        const x = i * (barW + 1)
        ctx.fillStyle = grad
        ctx.beginPath()
        if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(x, H - barH, barW, barH, 2)
        else ctx.rect(x, H - barH, barW, barH)
        ctx.fill()
      }
      rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(draw)
    }

    draw()
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current)
  }, [getAudioData, playing, color, onMetrics, still])

  return <canvas ref={canvasRef} width={196} height={32} className={styles.barCanvas} />
}

// ── Main component ─────────────────────────────────────────
// mode: 'mini' (default di HP: putar + volume), 'full' (panel lengkap), 'hidden' (hanya tombol ♫)
export default function MusicPlayer({
  playing, onToggle, volume, onVolumeChange,
  activeSection, getAudioData, audioProgress,
  comfort = false, onToggleComfort,
}) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState(() => (isNarrow() ? 'mini' : 'full'))
  const [track, setTrack] = useState(TRACK_INFO.intro)
  const [transitioning, setTransitioning] = useState(false)
  const [beatFlash, setBeatFlash] = useState(false)
  const playerRef = useRef(null)
  const beatCooldown = useRef(false)
  const prevBass = useRef(0)
  const beatTimers = useRef([])
  const reduce = useRef(prefersReduced()).current
  const still = reduce || comfort

  // Track change animation.
  // Dibandingkan lewat OBJEK track (bukan title): "intro" dan "gallery" sama-sama "Departure"
  // tetapi artisnya berbeda, jadi label harus tetap berganti.
  useEffect(() => {
    const next = TRACK_INFO[activeSection] ?? TRACK_INFO.default
    if (next === track) {
      setTransitioning(false) // kembali ke track semula di tengah animasi: jangan tertinggal pudar
      return
    }
    setTransitioning(true)
    const t = setTimeout(() => { setTrack(next); setTransitioning(false) }, 280)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [activeSection, track])

  useEffect(() => () => beatTimers.current.forEach(clearTimeout), [])

  // Beat detection + direct DOM glow update (tanpa trigger React re-render per frame).
  // Hanya dipanggil oleh BarVisualizer, jadi hanya aktif di mode penuh.
  const handleMetrics = useRef()
  handleMetrics.current = (data) => {
    const el = playerRef.current
    if (!data || !playing) {
      if (el) el.style.setProperty('--glow-int', '0')
      return
    }
    const { bass, avg } = getAudioMetrics(data)
    if (el) {
      // Comfort: glow tetap (tidak berdenyut mengikuti musik)
      el.style.setProperty('--glow-int', comfort ? '0.25' : (avg * 1.4).toFixed(3))
    }
    // Kedip beat (skala + glow tajam) dilewati saat reduced motion / Comfort
    if (!reduce && !comfort && bass > 0.52 && bass > prevBass.current * 1.22 && !beatCooldown.current) {
      setBeatFlash(true)
      beatCooldown.current = true
      beatTimers.current.push(setTimeout(() => setBeatFlash(false), 100))
      beatTimers.current.push(setTimeout(() => { beatCooldown.current = false }, 260))
    }
    prevBass.current = bass
  }

  const onMetricsCallback = useRef((data) => handleMetrics.current(data)).current

  const prog = audioProgress?.progress ?? 0
  const curTime = audioProgress?.currentTime ?? 0
  const dur = audioProgress?.duration ?? 0

  const playIcon = (size) => playing
    ? <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width={size} height={size} fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="5" y="3" width="4" height="18" rx="1.5" /><rect x="15" y="3" width="4" height="18" rx="1.5" /></svg>
    : <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width={size} height={size} fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><polygon points="5,3 20,12 5,21" /></svg>

  const volIcon = (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="13" height="13" fill="currentColor" opacity="0.55" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M3 9v6h4l5 5V4L7 9H3zm13.5 3c0-1.77-1.02-3.29-2.5-4.03v8.05c1.48-.73 2.5-2.25 2.5-4.02z" />
    </svg>
  )

  // ── Tersembunyi: tombol ♫ + tombol Comfort ──
  if (mode === 'hidden') return (
    <div className={styles.playerDock}>
      <button
        type="button"
        className={styles.showBtn}
        onClick={() => setMode(isNarrow() ? 'mini' : 'full')}
        title="Show player"
        aria-label="Show music player"
      >
        ♫
      </button>
      <ComfortButton comfort={comfort} onToggle={onToggleComfort} />
    </div>
  )

  // ── Mini: putar + volume + buka penuh + Comfort ──
  if (mode === 'mini') return (
    <div
      className={`${styles.playerMini} ${playing ? styles.miniOn : ''}`}
      role="group"
      aria-label="Music controls"
      style={{ '--glow': track.color[0] }}
    >
      <button
        type="button"
        className={styles.miniPlay}
        style={{ '--btn-col': track.color[0] }}
        onClick={onToggle}
        aria-label={playing ? 'Pause music' : 'Play music'}
      >
        {playIcon(18)}
      </button>

      <div className={styles.miniVol}>
        {volIcon}
        <input
          type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.02"
          value={volume}
          onChange={e => onVolumeChange(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
          className={styles.miniSlider}
          style={{ '--vol': `${volume * 100}%`, '--c1': track.color[0] }}
          aria-label="Volume"
        />
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        className={styles.miniBtn}
        onClick={() => setMode('full')}
        aria-label="Open full music player"
        aria-expanded="false"
        title="Open player"
      >
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6" />
        </svg>
      </button>

      <ComfortButton comfort={comfort} onToggle={onToggleComfort} />
    </div>
  )

  // ── Penuh ──
  return (
    <div
      ref={playerRef}
      className={`${styles.player} ${playing ? styles.playing : ''} ${beatFlash ? styles.beat : ''}`}
      style={{ '--glow': track.color[0], '--glow-int': '0' }}
    >
      {/* Minimize (kembali ke mini) */}
      <button
        type="button"
        className={styles.collapseBtn}
        onClick={() => setMode('mini')}
        title="Minimize player"
        aria-label="Minimize music player"
        aria-expanded="true"
      >
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="12" height="12" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.6" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="M6 15l6-6 6 6" />
        </svg>
      </button>

      {/* Close button (sembunyikan sepenuhnya) */}
      <button
        type="button"
        className={styles.closeBtn}
        onClick={() => setMode('hidden')}
        title="Hide player"
        aria-label="Hide music player"
      >×</button>

      {/* Top row: art + track info */}
      <div className={styles.topRow}>
        <div className={styles.artWrap} onClick={onToggle}>
          <ArtCanvas theme={track.theme} color={track.color} still={still} />
          <div className={styles.artOverlay}>
            {playing
              ? <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" fill="white"><rect x="5" y="3" width="4" height="18" rx="1.5" /><rect x="15" y="3" width="4" height="18" rx="1.5" /></svg>
              : <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" fill="white"><polygon points="6,3 21,12 6,21" /></svg>
            }
          </div>
          {beatFlash && <div className={styles.beatRing} style={{ borderColor: track.color[0] }} />}
        </div>

        <div className={styles.trackMeta}>
          <div className={styles.trackNameWrap}>
            <span className={`${styles.trackName} ${transitioning ? styles.fadeOut : styles.fadeIn}`}>
              {track.title}
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className={styles.trackArtist}>{track.artist}</span>
          <span className={styles.lofiBadge} style={{ color: track.color[0], borderColor: track.color[0] + '55' }}>
            ✦ lofi space
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Bar visualizer */}
      <div className={styles.vizWrap} aria-hidden="true">
        <BarVisualizer
          getAudioData={getAudioData}
          playing={playing}
          color={track.color}
          onMetrics={onMetricsCallback}
          still={still}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Progress bar — real duration */}
      <div className={styles.progressArea}>
        <span className={styles.timeLabel}>{fmt(curTime)}</span>
        <div className={styles.progressTrack}>
          <div
            className={styles.progressFill}
            style={{ width: `${prog * 100}%`, background: `linear-gradient(90deg, ${track.color[0]}, ${track.color[1]})` }}
          />
        </div>
        <span className={styles.timeLabel}>{fmt(dur)}</span>
      </div>

      {/* Controls row */}
      <div className={styles.controlsRow}>
        <button className={styles.playBtn} onClick={onToggle}
          style={{ '--btn-col': track.color[0] }}
          aria-label={playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play'}
        >
          {playIcon(18)}
        </button>

        <div className={styles.volRow}>
          {volIcon}
          <input
            type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.02"
            value={volume}
            onChange={e => onVolumeChange(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
            className={styles.volSlider}
            style={{ '--vol': `${volume * 100}%`, '--c1': track.color[0] }}
            aria-label="Volume"
          />
          <span className={styles.volNum}>{Math.round(volume * 100)}</span>
        </div>

        <ComfortButton comfort={comfort} onToggle={onToggleComfort} />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/components/NavDots.jsx =====
import { memo } from 'react'
import styles from './NavDots.module.css'

const SECTIONS = [
  { id: 'intro', label: 'Departure' },
  { id: 'solar', label: 'Solar System' },
  { id: 'phenomena', label: 'Phenomena' },
  { id: 'deepspace', label: 'Deep Space' },
  { id: 'ending', label: 'Reflection' },
  { id: 'wishing', label: 'Wishing Star' },
  { id: 'gallery', label: 'Astronaut Gallery' },
]

function NavDots({ active, onNavigate }) {
  return (
    <nav className={styles.nav} aria-label="Section navigation">
      {SECTIONS.map(({ id, label }) => (
        <button
          key={id}
          type="button"
          className={`${styles.dot} ${active === id ? styles.active : ''}`}
          onClick={() => onNavigate(id)}
          aria-label={`Go to ${label}`}
          aria-current={active === id ? 'step' : undefined}
        >
          <span className={styles.tooltip} aria-hidden="true">{label}</span>
        </button>
      ))}
    </nav>
  )
}

export default memo(NavDots)
===== END FILE =====
