===== FILE: src/components/ProgressBar.jsx =====
import { memo } from 'react'

function ProgressBar({ progress }) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: 'fixed',
        top: 0, left: 0,
        width: `${progress * 100}%`,
        height: '2px',
        background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, #4466ff, #8844ff, #ff44aa)',
        zIndex: 200,
        transition: 'width 0.1s linear',
        pointerEvents: 'none',
        boxShadow: '0 0 8px rgba(136,68,255,0.6)',
      }}
      role="progressbar"
      aria-valuenow={Math.round(progress * 100)}
      aria-valuemin={0}
      aria-valuemax={100}
    />
  )
}

export default memo(ProgressBar)
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/components/StarField.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useRef, memo } from 'react'

// Waktu-konstan easing depth (detik). 3 × TAU ≈ 1,6 dtk → ~95% tercapai, ~2 dtk hampir tuntas.
const DEPTH_TAU = 0.55
const clamp01 = (v) => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, v))

// Mode tenang (Comfort), nilai pada calm penuh (di-easing 0..1, tanpa lompatan):
const CALM_SLOW = 0.3     // kedip dan drift tinggal 30%
const CALM_DIM = 0.45     // kecerahan canvas dikurangi 45% lagi (di atas redup dari depth)
const CALM_KEEP = 0.2     // ambang rank turun: bintang yang digambar makin sedikit
const CALM_HALO = 0.5     // halo bintang terang mengecil

// depth 0..1: makin tinggi → canvas lebih redup, bintang yang digambar berkurang
// (sisa kebanyakan yang redup), halo mengecil, kedip melambat. Dibaca lewat ref di loop gambar.
// calm (boolean): mode Comfort, juga dibaca lewat ref (tidak memicu inisialisasi ulang).
function StarField({ count = 500, mouseParallax = true, depth = 0, calm = false }) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null)
  const starsRef = useRef([])
  const shootersRef = useRef([])
  const mouseRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 })
  const animRef = useRef(null)

  // Depth dan calm: target (dari prop) dan nilai berjalan (di-easing di loop, tanpa state per frame)
  const depthTargetRef = useRef(clamp01(depth))
  const depthCurRef = useRef(clamp01(depth))
  const calmTargetRef = useRef(calm ? 1 : 0)
  const calmCurRef = useRef(calm ? 1 : 0)
  const kickRef = useRef(null) // membangunkan loop (penting saat reduced motion: loop berhenti bila sudah stabil)

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current
    if (!canvas) return
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
    const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches

    let lastW = 0
    let lastH = 0
    let lastT = 0

    // Layar sempit (HP) cukup dengan ±55% bintang: tampilan sama, beban jauh lebih ringan
    const targetCount = () => (window.innerWidth < 768 ? Math.round(count * 0.55) : count)

    // ── Init stars ────────────────────────────────────────
    const initStars = () => {
      starsRef.current = Array.from({ length: targetCount() }, () => {
        const tier = Math.random()
        // Radius disesuaikan agar bintang terlihat jelas
        const r = tier > 0.92 ? Math.random() * 1.8 + 1.4   // Bintang terang
          : tier > 0.75 ? Math.random() * 1.0 + 0.8   // Bintang sedang
            : Math.random() * 0.6 + 0.4                 // Bintang redup
        return {
          x: Math.random() * canvas.width,
          y: Math.random() * canvas.height,
          r,
          // rank 0..1 tetap per bintang: bintang besar cenderung berrank tinggi (dibuang duluan saat depth naik)
          rank: 0.5 * ((r - 0.4) / 2.8) + 0.5 * Math.random(),
          alpha: Math.random(),
          speed: reduce ? 0 : Math.random() * 0.005 + 0.001,
          phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
          drift: reduce ? 0 : (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.02,
          layer: Math.floor(Math.random() * 3),
          color: (() => {
            const c = Math.random()
            if (c > 0.90) return { r: 255, g: 220, b: 120 }
            if (c > 0.80) return { r: 200, g: 180, b: 255 }
            if (c > 0.70) return { r: 140, g: 200, b: 255 }
            if (c > 0.60) return { r: 255, g: 180, b: 180 }
            return { r: 210, g: 225, b: 255 }
          })(),
        }
      })
    }

    // ── Resize ────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Address bar HP naik-turun hanya mengubah TINGGI: bintang tidak dibuat ulang,
    // posisi Y cukup diskalakan. Bintang baru dibuat hanya jika LEBAR berubah.
    const resize = () => {
      const w = window.innerWidth
      const h = window.innerHeight
      const widthChanged = w !== lastW
      const oldH = lastH
      lastW = w
      lastH = h
      canvas.width = w
      canvas.height = h

      if (!starsRef.current.length || widthChanged) {
        initStars()
      } else if (oldH && h !== oldH) {
        const k = h / oldH
        starsRef.current.forEach((s) => { s.y *= k })
      }
      if (reduce) draw() // loop tidak berjalan terus di mode reduced: gambar ulang manual
    }

    // ── Shooting stars ────────────────────────────────────
    const spawnShooter = () => ({
      x: Math.random() * canvas.width * 0.7,
      y: Math.random() * canvas.height * 0.4,
      len: Math.random() * 180 + 80,
      speed: Math.random() * 14 + 8,
      angle: Math.PI / 4 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3,
      opacity: 1,
      width: Math.random() * 1.5 + 0.5,
      trail: [],
    })

    // Spawn shooting star setiap 3–7 detik (dimatikan saat reduced motion; dilewati saat Comfort)
    const shooterInterval = reduce ? 0 : setInterval(() => {
      if (!document.hidden && calmTargetRef.current === 0 && shootersRef.current.length < 3) {
        shootersRef.current.push(spawnShooter())
      }
    }, 3000 + Math.random() * 4000)

    // ── Draw ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    function draw() {
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)

      // c: 0 = normal, 1 = Comfort penuh (di-easing, jadi transisinya mulus)
      const c = calmCurRef.current
      const calmK = 1 - (1 - CALM_SLOW) * c
      const mx = mouseRef.current.x * (1 - c)
      const my = mouseRef.current.y * (1 - c)

      // Parameter turunan depth + calm (dihitung sekali per frame)
      const d = depthCurRef.current
      const dim = (1 - 0.5 * d) * (1 - CALM_DIM * c)   // kecerahan canvas: 1 → 0,5 (depth), lalu × 0,55 (Comfort)
      const keep = 1.1 - 0.65 * d - CALM_KEEP * c      // ambang rank: makin kecil, makin sedikit bintang
      const slow = 1 - 0.65 * d                        // kecepatan kedip: 100% → 35%
      const haloK = (2.2 - 0.9 * d) * (1 - CALM_HALO * c * 0.5) // radius halo
      const haloA = 0.22 * (1 - 0.5 * d) * (1 - CALM_HALO * c)

      ctx.globalAlpha = dim

      // Draw stars
      starsRef.current.forEach((s) => {
        s.phase += s.speed * slow * calmK
        s.x += s.drift * calmK
        if (s.x < -2) s.x = canvas.width + 2
        if (s.x > canvas.width + 2) s.x = -2

        // Memudar halus di sekitar ambang (tidak "pop" saat bintang hilang)
        const vis = clamp01((keep - s.rank) / 0.1)
        if (vis <= 0.01) return

        const parallaxStr = [0.003, 0.009, 0.020][s.layer]
        const px = s.x + mx * parallaxStr
        const py = s.y + my * parallaxStr

        // Twinkle: bintang besar lebih dramatis (reduced: kecerahan tetap)
        const twinkle = reduce ? 0.8 : 0.3 + Math.abs(Math.sin(s.phase)) * 0.7
        const { r, g, b } = s.color
        const alpha = twinkle * (s.r > 1.2 ? 1 : 0.85) * vis

        ctx.beginPath()
        ctx.arc(px, py, s.r, 0, Math.PI * 2)
        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${alpha})`
        ctx.fill()

        // Luminous halo untuk bintang terang (jauh lebih efisien dari shadowBlur CPU filter)
        if (s.r > 1.0) {
          ctx.beginPath()
          ctx.arc(px, py, s.r * haloK, 0, Math.PI * 2)
          ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${alpha * haloA})`
          ctx.fill()
        }
      })

      // Draw shooting stars (ikut dim lewat globalAlpha). Yang sudah terlanjur muncul dibiarkan selesai.
      shootersRef.current = shootersRef.current.filter((s) => {
        s.x += Math.cos(s.angle) * s.speed
        s.y += Math.sin(s.angle) * s.speed
        s.opacity -= 0.012

        if (s.opacity <= 0 || s.x > canvas.width + 50 || s.y > canvas.height + 50) return false

        const tailX = s.x - Math.cos(s.angle) * s.len
        const tailY = s.y - Math.sin(s.angle) * s.len

        const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(tailX, tailY, s.x, s.y)
        grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255,255,255,0)`)
        grad.addColorStop(0.7, `rgba(200,220,255,${s.opacity * 0.4})`)
        grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(255,255,255,${s.opacity})`)

        ctx.beginPath()
        ctx.moveTo(tailX, tailY)
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y)
        ctx.strokeStyle = grad
        ctx.lineWidth = s.width
        ctx.shadowBlur = 8
        ctx.shadowColor = `rgba(180,210,255,${s.opacity})`
        ctx.stroke()
        ctx.shadowBlur = 0

        return true
      })

      ctx.globalAlpha = 1
    }

    // ── Loop ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Normal: berjalan terus (dijeda saat tab tersembunyi).
    // Reduced motion: hanya berjalan selama depth / calm masih berpindah, lalu berhenti sendiri.
    const ease = (cur, target, dt) => {
      const diff = target - cur
      return Math.abs(diff) < 0.0005 ? target : cur + diff * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / DEPTH_TAU))
    }

    const loop = (now) => {
      const dt = lastT ? Math.min((now - lastT) / 1000, 0.1) : 0.016
      lastT = now

      depthCurRef.current = ease(depthCurRef.current, depthTargetRef.current, dt)
      calmCurRef.current = ease(calmCurRef.current, calmTargetRef.current, dt)

      draw()

      if (
        reduce &&
        depthCurRef.current === depthTargetRef.current &&
        calmCurRef.current === calmTargetRef.current
      ) {
        animRef.current = null
        lastT = 0
        return
      }
      animRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(loop)
    }
    const startLoop = () => {
      if (animRef.current || document.hidden) return
      lastT = 0
      animRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(loop)
    }
    const stopLoop = () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(animRef.current)
      animRef.current = null
    }
    const onVisibility = () => (document.hidden ? stopLoop() : startLoop())

    const onMouseMove = (e) => {
      mouseRef.current = {
        x: e.clientX - window.innerWidth / 2,
        y: e.clientY - window.innerHeight / 2,
      }
    }

    kickRef.current = startLoop

    resize()
    window.addEventListener('resize', resize)
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility)
    // Parallax mouse dimatikan saat reduced motion
    if (mouseParallax && !reduce) window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove, { passive: true })
    startLoop()

    return () => {
      kickRef.current = null
      window.removeEventListener('resize', resize)
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove)
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility)
      stopLoop()
      if (shooterInterval) clearInterval(shooterInterval)
      shootersRef.current = []
    }
  }, [count, mouseParallax])

  // Perubahan depth hanya memperbarui target (ref) dan membangunkan loop; efek utama tidak dijalankan ulang
  useEffect(() => {
    depthTargetRef.current = clamp01(depth)
    kickRef.current?.()
  }, [depth])

  // Perubahan calm: target di-easing di loop (mulus), loop dibangunkan bila sedang berhenti
  useEffect(() => {
    calmTargetRef.current = calm ? 1 : 0
    kickRef.current?.()
  }, [calm])

  return (
    <canvas
      ref={canvasRef}
      aria-hidden="true"
      style={{
        position: 'fixed',
        top: 0, left: 0,
        width: '100%', height: '100%',
        pointerEvents: 'none',
        zIndex: 0,
      }}
    />
  )
}

export default memo(StarField)
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/components/UniverseScale.jsx =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { UNIVERSE_LEVELS as LEVELS } from '../data/index.js'
import { sfx } from '../hooks/useSfx.js'
import { SCENES, RHO, CX, CY, EARTH_ANCHOR, sunAt, diskTransform } from './universeScenes.jsx'
import styles from './UniverseScale.module.css'

const LAST = LEVELS.length - 1
const clamp = (n, a, b) => Math.min(Math.max(n, a), b)
const sstep = (x) => { const t = clamp(x, 0, 1); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t) }
const OMEGA = 0.14 // rad/detik, putaran galaksi (≈45 detik per putaran)

/**
 * Kamera zoom antar scene. Scene i-1 "hidup" di dalam scene i pada titik A[i]
 * (Bumi di orbitnya, Tata Surya di sekitar Matahari, dst) dan tampil dengan
 * skala RHO[i]. Pada transisi t∈[0,1]:
 *  - scene i-1 (anak) mengecil dari 1 → RHO[i] menuju titik A[i]
 *  - scene i (induk) membesar dari 1/RHO[i] → 1, dengan A[i] berpindah dari pusat
 * Keduanya memakai titik b(t) yang sama, jadi selalu konsisten.
 * Hasil: transform layar = s·p + (x, y) dalam koordinat viewBox 400×250.
 */
function layerState(i, v, A) {
  if (i >= 1 && v < i) {                       // masuk sebagai induk
    const t = v - (i - 1)
    if (t <= 0) return null
    const a = A[i], s = Math.pow(RHO[i], t - 1)
    const bx = CX + (a[0] - CX) * t, by = CY + (a[1] - CY) * t
    return { s, x: bx - s * a[0], y: by - s * a[1], op: sstep((t - 0.5) / 0.4) }
  }
  if (i < LAST) {                              // keluar sebagai anak
    const t = v - i
    if (t >= 1) return null
    const a = A[i + 1], s = Math.pow(RHO[i + 1], t)
    const bx = CX + (a[0] - CX) * t, by = CY + (a[1] - CY) * t
    return { s, x: bx - s * CX, y: by - s * CY, op: 1 - sstep((t - 0.65) / 0.3) }
  }
  return { s: 1, x: 0, y: 0, op: 1 }           // scene terakhir
}

// 2 angka signifikan: 704.500 → "700,000"
const sig2 = (n) => {
  const p = Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log10(n)) - 1)
  return (Math.round(n / p) * p).toLocaleString('en-US')
}

export default function UniverseScale() {
  const [v, setV] = useState(0)          // posisi slider (float, 0..5)
  const vRef = useRef(0)
  const target = useRef(0)
  const dragging = useRef(false)
  const raf = useRef(0)
  const trackRef = useRef(null)
  const reduce = useRef(false)
  const firstRun = useRef(true)
  const figRef = useRef(null)
  const layers = useRef([])
  const diskRef = useRef(null)
  const sunRef = useRef(null)
  const theta = useRef(0.6)
  const inView = useRef(true)
  // Titik tujuan tiap scene di dalam scene berikutnya (index = scene induk)
  const anchors = useRef([null, EARTH_ANCHOR, [CX, CY], sunAt(0.6), [CX, CY], [CX, CY]])

  const apply = useCallback((val) => {
    layers.current.forEach((el, i) => {
      if (!el) return
      const st = layerState(i, val, anchors.current)
      if (!st || st.op < 0.01) {
        el.style.visibility = 'hidden'
        el.style.opacity = 0
        el.classList.add(styles.idle)
        return
      }
      el.classList.remove(styles.idle)
      el.style.visibility = 'visible'
      el.style.opacity = st.op
      el.style.transform = `translate(${(st.x / 400) * 100}%, ${(st.y / 250) * 100}%) scale(${st.s})`
    })
  }, [])

  const spinGalaxy = useCallback((th) => {
    diskRef.current?.setAttribute('transform', diskTransform(th))
    const [sx, sy] = sunAt(th)
    sunRef.current?.setAttribute('transform', `translate(${sx} ${sy})`)
    anchors.current[3] = [sx, sy]
  }, [])

  useLayoutEffect(() => { spinGalaxy(theta.current); apply(v) }, [v, apply, spinGalaxy])

  // Putaran galaksi: hanya jalan saat scene Milky Way terlihat dan komponen di layar
  useEffect(() => {
    const io = new IntersectionObserver(([en]) => { inView.current = en.isIntersecting }, { threshold: 0.05 })
    if (figRef.current) io.observe(figRef.current)
    let id = 0, last = performance.now()
    const loop = (now) => {
      id = requestAnimationFrame(loop)
      const dt = Math.min((now - last) / 1000, 0.1)
      last = now
      const val = vRef.current
      if (reduce.current || !inView.current || val <= 2 || val >= 5) return
      theta.current -= dt * OMEGA   // negatif: lengan spiral "tertinggal" (trailing)
      spinGalaxy(theta.current)
      apply(val)
    }
    id = requestAnimationFrame(loop)
    return () => { cancelAnimationFrame(id); io.disconnect() }
  }, [apply, spinGalaxy])

  useEffect(() => {
    reduce.current = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf.current)
  }, [])

  const level = Math.round(v)

  // Bunyi saat level berganti (termasuk saat menyapu slider)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (firstRun.current) { firstRun.current = false; return }
    sfx.chime(level)
  }, [level])

  const animate = useCallback(() => {
    let last = performance.now()
    const step = (now) => {
      if (dragging.current) { raf.current = 0; return }
      const dt = Math.min((now - last) / 1000, 0.1)
      last = now
      const d = target.current - vRef.current
      if (Math.abs(d) < 0.002) {
        vRef.current = target.current
        setV(target.current)
        raf.current = 0
        return
      }
      vRef.current += d * (1 - Math.exp(-dt * 4.5))
      setV(vRef.current)
      raf.current = requestAnimationFrame(step)
    }
    raf.current = requestAnimationFrame(step)
  }, [])

  const goTo = useCallback((i) => {
    target.current = clamp(i, 0, LAST)
    if (reduce.current) { vRef.current = target.current; setV(target.current); return }
    if (!raf.current) animate()
  }, [animate])

  const fromPointer = (e) => {
    const r = trackRef.current.getBoundingClientRect()
    const f = clamp((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width, 0, 1)
    vRef.current = f * LAST
    setV(vRef.current)
  }
  const onDown = (e) => {
    e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId)
    dragging.current = true
    cancelAnimationFrame(raf.current); raf.current = 0
    fromPointer(e)
  }
  const onMove = (e) => { if (dragging.current) fromPointer(e) }
  const onUp = () => {
    if (!dragging.current) return
    dragging.current = false
    goTo(Math.round(vRef.current))
  }
  const onKey = (e) => {
    const k = e.key
    if (k === 'ArrowRight' || k === 'ArrowUp') { e.preventDefault(); goTo(level + 1) }
    else if (k === 'ArrowLeft' || k === 'ArrowDown') { e.preventDefault(); goTo(level - 1) }
    else if (k === 'Home') { e.preventDefault(); goTo(0) }
    else if (k === 'End') { e.preventDefault(); goTo(LAST) }
  }

  const cur = LEVELS[level]
  const factor = level > 0 ? sig2(cur.ly / LEVELS[level - 1].ly) : null

  return (
    <figure ref={figRef} className={styles.figure}>
      <figcaption className={styles.caption}>
        <span className="t-label">Scale of the Universe</span>
        <span className={styles.hint}>Drag the slider to zoom out</span>
      </figcaption>

      <div className={styles.stage}>
        {SCENES.map((Scene, i) => (
          <div key={i} ref={(el) => { layers.current[i] = el }} className={styles.scene} aria-hidden="true">
            {i === 3 ? <Scene diskRef={diskRef} sunRef={sunRef} /> : <Scene />}
          </div>
        ))}
        <div className={styles.level}>LEVEL {String(level + 1).padStart(2, '0')} / {String(LEVELS.length).padStart(2, '0')}</div>
        {factor && <div className={styles.factor}>×{factor} wider than the previous view</div>}
      </div>

      <div className={styles.info} aria-live="polite">
        <div key={cur.id} className={styles.infoInner}>
          <h3 className={styles.name}>{cur.name}</h3>
          <p className={styles.tagline}>{cur.tagline}</p>
          <dl className={styles.stats}>
            <div><dt>Diameter</dt><dd>{cur.size}</dd></div>
            <div><dt>{cur.light[0]}</dt><dd>{cur.light[1]}</dd></div>
            <div><dt>Contains</dt><dd>{cur.holds}</dd></div>
          </dl>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.controls}>
        <button className={styles.step} onClick={() => goTo(level - 1)} disabled={level === 0} aria-label="Zoom in">−</button>

        <div className={styles.trackPad}>
          <div
            ref={trackRef}
            className={styles.track}
            onPointerDown={onDown}
            onPointerMove={onMove}
            onPointerUp={onUp}
            onPointerCancel={onUp}
          >
            <div className={styles.rail} />
            <div className={styles.fill} style={{ width: `${(v / LAST) * 100}%` }} />
            {LEVELS.map((l, i) => (
              <span key={l.id} className={`${styles.tick} ${i <= v + 0.01 ? styles.tickOn : ''}`} style={{ left: `${(i / LAST) * 100}%` }} />
            ))}
            <div
              className={styles.thumb}
              style={{ left: `${(v / LAST) * 100}%` }}
              role="slider"
              tabIndex={0}
              aria-label="Zoom level"
              aria-valuemin={0}
              aria-valuemax={LAST}
              aria-valuenow={level}
              aria-valuetext={cur.name}
              onKeyDown={onKey}
            />
          </div>
          <div className={styles.labels}>
            {LEVELS.map((l, i) => (
              <button
                key={l.id}
                className={`${styles.lab} ${i === level ? styles.labOn : ''}`}
                style={{ left: `${(i / LAST) * 100}%` }}
                onClick={() => goTo(i)}
              >
                {l.short}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <button className={styles.step} onClick={() => goTo(level + 1)} disabled={level === LAST} aria-label="Zoom out">+</button>
      </div>
      <p className={styles.note}>Schematic illustrations, not to scale.</p>
    </figure>
  )
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/components/UniverseScale.module.css =====
.figure {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2;
  width: min(760px, 100%);
  margin: 0;
}

.caption {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: baseline;
  gap: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 0.8rem;
  padding: 0 0.25rem;
}

.hint {
  font-family: var(--font-body);
  font-size: var(--fs-small);
  font-weight: var(--fw-regular);
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  color: var(--text-muted);
}

/* ── Panggung ── */
.stage {
  position: relative;
  aspect-ratio: 16 / 10;
  border: 1px solid rgba(68, 102, 255, 0.3);
  border-radius: 14px;
  background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 50%, rgba(20, 35, 80, 0.35), rgba(0, 0, 6, 0.6) 72%);
  box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(68, 102, 255, 0.08), inset 0 0 60px rgba(0, 0, 10, 0.6);
  overflow: hidden;
}

.scene {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  transform-origin: 0 0;
  opacity: 0;
  visibility: hidden;
  will-change: transform, opacity;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.scene svg {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  display: block;
}

/* Scene yang tidak terlihat: sembunyikan dan hentikan animasinya */
.idle {
  visibility: hidden;
}

.idle * {
  animation-play-state: paused !important;
}

.level,
.factor {
  position: absolute;
  font-family: var(--font-body);
  font-size: var(--fs-small);
  font-weight: var(--fw-regular);
  letter-spacing: var(--ls-label);
  color: var(--text-muted);
  text-shadow: var(--text-shadow-soft);
  pointer-events: none;
}

.level {
  top: 12px;
  left: 16px;
}

.factor {
  top: 12px;
  right: 16px;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  color: var(--text-secondary);
}

/* ── Label teks di dalam SVG scene ──
   Halo gelap (stroke di belakang isian) menggantikan text-shadow dan menjaga
   keterbacaan di atas titik-titik bintang. Warna ikut token. */
.svgText,

===== FILE: src/components/universeScenes.jsx =====
import styles from './UniverseScale.module.css'

// Ilustrasi skematis (bukan skala sebenarnya). Semua memakai viewBox 400×250,
// pusat (200,125). Penanda cyan "You" selalu di pusat, kecuali di Galaxy.
// Label teks memakai class svgText/svgSoft/svgHalo (warna dari token + halo gelap).
export const CX = 200, CY = 125
const FONT = { fontFamily: 'Outfit, system-ui, sans-serif' }

function mulberry32(seed) {
  return () => {
    seed |= 0; seed = (seed + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0
    let t = Math.imul(seed ^ (seed >>> 15), 1 | seed)
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296
  }
}
const gauss = (r) => (r() + r() + r() + r() - 2) / 0.58
// Kumpulan titik sebagai satu <path>: jauh lebih ringan daripada ribuan <circle>
const dots = (pts) => pts.map(([x, y]) => `M${x.toFixed(1)} ${y.toFixed(1)}h0`).join('')

function You({ x = CX, y = CY, label, anchor = 'start', dx = 12, dy = -12 }) {
  const lx = x + dx, ly = y + dy
  return (
    <g>
      <circle cx={x} cy={y} r="3" className={styles.pulse} fill="none" stroke="#00d0ff" strokeWidth="1" />
      <circle cx={x} cy={y} r="2.2" fill="#00d0ff" />
      {label && (
        <>
          <path d={`M${x + (dx > 0 ? 3 : -3)} ${y + (dy > 0 ? 3 : -3)}L${lx - (dx > 0 ? 2 : -2)} ${ly + (dy < 0 ? 3 : -3)}`}
            stroke="rgba(0,208,255,.5)" strokeWidth=".7" />
          <text x={lx} y={ly} textAnchor={anchor} className={styles.svgHalo} fill="#7fe3ff" fontSize="9" letterSpacing="1.4" style={FONT}>
            {label}
          </text>
        </>
      )}
    </g>
  )
}

// ── 1. Bumi ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const LAND = [
  'M150 90q20-16 42-6q14 10 6 26q-10 14-28 10q-24-4-20-30z',
  'M210 120q24-10 40 6q8 22-8 40q-20 10-30-8q-10-18-2-38z',
  'M165 150q16-6 22 8q-2 16-18 14q-12-6-4-22z',
  'M240 82q14-6 22 6q2 12-12 14q-14-2-10-20z',
]
export function EarthScene() {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 400 250" role="presentation">
      <defs>
        <radialGradient id="us-ocean" cx="38%" cy="35%" r="80%">
          <stop offset="0" stopColor="#3b86e0" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#0a2a5e" />
        </radialGradient>
        <radialGradient id="us-shade" cx="35%" cy="32%" r="75%">
          <stop offset="0.45" stopColor="#000010" stopOpacity="0" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#000010" stopOpacity="0.75" />
        </radialGradient>
        <radialGradient id="us-moon" cx="35%" cy="35%" r="75%">
          <stop offset="0" stopColor="#dcdcdc" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#6c6c72" />
        </radialGradient>
        <clipPath id="us-earth-clip"><circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r="74" /></clipPath>
      </defs>
      <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r="84" fill="none" stroke="rgba(90,170,255,.22)" strokeWidth="8" />
      <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r="74" fill="url(#us-ocean)" />
      <g clipPath="url(#us-earth-clip)">
        <g className={styles.earthSpin} fill="#3f9c58" opacity=".9">
          {LAND.map((d, i) => <path key={i} d={d} />)}
          <g transform="translate(288 0)">{LAND.map((d, i) => <path key={i} d={d} />)}</g>
        </g>
      </g>
      <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r="74" fill="url(#us-shade)" />
      <circle cx="318" cy="72" r="10" fill="url(#us-moon)" />
      <text x="318" y="96" textAnchor="middle" className={styles.svgSoft} fontSize="9" letterSpacing="1.4" style={FONT}>MOON</text>
    </svg>
  )
}

// ── 2. Tata Surya ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Bumi (fixed) tidak mengorbit di tampilan ini: posisinya jadi titik tujuan
// saat scene Bumi mengecil. Ukurannya (2.6) harus = RHO[1] × radius Bumi (74).
const EARTH_ANGLE = 40
export const EARTH_ANCHOR = [
  CX + 28 * Math.cos((EARTH_ANGLE * Math.PI) / 180),
  CY + 28 * Math.sin((EARTH_ANGLE * Math.PI) / 180),
]
const PLANETS = [
  { r: 13, s: 1.6, c: '#b5a99a', t: 6 }, { r: 20, s: 2.4, c: '#e8c27a', t: 10 },
  { r: 28, s: 2.6, c: '#4aa3ff', t: 14, fixed: true }, { r: 36, s: 2.0, c: '#d46b45', t: 20 },
  { r: 56, s: 5.5, c: '#d9b38c', t: 40 }, { r: 74, s: 4.5, c: '#e6d3a0', t: 70, ring: true },
  { r: 93, s: 3.2, c: '#8fe3e8', t: 100 }, { r: 112, s: 3.2, c: '#5b7bff', t: 140 },
]
export function SolarScene() {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 400 250" role="presentation">
      <defs>
        <radialGradient id="us-sun"><stop offset="0" stopColor="#fff6d0" /><stop offset=".4" stopColor="#ffc75a" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#ff8a2a" stopOpacity="0" /></radialGradient>
      </defs>
      <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r="45" fill="none" stroke="rgba(143,171,198,.25)" strokeDasharray="1 3" strokeWidth="5" opacity=".5" />
      {PLANETS.map((p, i) => (
        <circle key={i} cx={CX} cy={CY} r={p.r} fill="none" stroke="rgba(120,160,230,.22)" strokeWidth=".7" />
      ))}
      <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r="15" fill="url(#us-sun)" />
      <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r="5.5" fill="#ffd36a" />
      <circle cx={EARTH_ANCHOR[0]} cy={EARTH_ANCHOR[1]} r="2.6" fill="#4aa3ff" />
      {PLANETS.filter((p) => !p.fixed).map((p, i) => (
        <g key={i} className={styles.orbit}
          style={{ animationDuration: `${p.t}s`, animationDelay: `${-i * 3.7}s`, transformOrigin: `${CX}px ${CY}px` }}>
          {p.ring && <ellipse cx={CX + p.r} cy={CY} rx={p.s * 2} ry={p.s * 0.6} fill="none" stroke="rgba(230,211,160,.7)" strokeWidth=".8" />}
          <circle cx={CX + p.r} cy={CY} r={p.s} fill={p.c} />
        </g>
      ))}
      <text x={CX} y="244" textAnchor="middle" className={styles.svgText} fontSize="9" letterSpacing="1.4" style={FONT}>ORBIT SPACING COMPRESSED</text>
    </svg>
  )
}

// ── 3. Bintang tetangga ────────────────────────────────────────────────
const K = 5.4 // px per light-year
const NEAR = [
  ['Proxima Centauri', 4.24, 205, '#ff8f70', 2.0, true], ['α Centauri', 4.37, 218, '#fff0c0', 2.6],
  ["Barnard's Star", 5.96, 80, '#ff9a7a', 1.8, true], ['Wolf 359', 7.86, 150, '#ff8f70', 1.6],
  ['Lalande 21185', 8.31, 40, '#ff9a7a', 1.7], ['Sirius', 8.6, 295, '#cfe4ff', 4.2, true],
  ['Ross 154', 9.7, 248, '#ff9a7a', 1.6], ['ε Eridani', 10.5, 15, '#ffc890', 2.2],
  ['Procyon', 11.4, 118, '#fff6e0', 3.4, true], ['61 Cygni', 11.4, 338, '#ffc890', 2.2],
  ['Tau Ceti', 11.9, 268, '#ffe2a8', 2.4, true],
]
export function NeighborsScene() {
  const rnd = mulberry32(7)
  const filler = Array.from({ length: 70 }, () => {
    const d = 20 * Math.sqrt(rnd()), a = rnd() * Math.PI * 2
    return [CX + Math.cos(a) * d * K, CY - Math.sin(a) * d * K]
  })
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 400 250" role="presentation">
      {[5, 10, 15, 20].map((ly) => (
        <g key={ly}>
          <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r={ly * K} fill="none" stroke="rgba(120,160,230,.26)" strokeDasharray="2 5" strokeWidth=".8" />
          <text x={CX + 3} y={CY - ly * K - 2} className={styles.svgText} fontSize="8.5" style={FONT}>{ly} ly</text>
        </g>
      ))}
      <path d={dots(filler)} stroke="rgba(170,200,255,.45)" strokeWidth="1.2" strokeLinecap="round" />
      {NEAR.map(([name, d, ang, col, size, label], i) => {
        const a = (ang * Math.PI) / 180
        const x = CX + Math.cos(a) * d * K, y = CY - Math.sin(a) * d * K
        const right = x >= CX
        return (
          <g key={i}>
            <circle cx={x} cy={y} r={size * 2.4} fill={col} opacity=".14" />
            <circle cx={x} cy={y} r={size} fill={col} />
            {label && (
              <text x={x + (right ? 7 : -7)} y={y + 3} textAnchor={right ? 'start' : 'end'} className={styles.svgSoft} fontSize="9" letterSpacing=".5" style={FONT}>
                {name} · {d} ly
              </text>
            )}
          </g>
        )
      })}
      <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r="5" fill="rgba(255,211,106,.12)" stroke="rgba(120,160,230,.55)" strokeWidth=".7" />
      <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r="2.2" fill="#ffd36a" />
      <You label="YOU (SUN)" dx={12} dy={16} />
    </svg>
  )
}

// ── 4. Bima Sakti (berputar) ───────────────────────────────────────────
// Bintang disimpan di koordinat bidang galaksi (belum dimiringkan). Rotasi
// dilakukan dari luar (UniverseScale) lewat ref supaya posisi Matahari, yang
// jadi titik tujuan transisi, selalu sinkron dengan putaran.
const R0 = 0.08, PITCH = Math.tan((15 * Math.PI) / 180), TILT = 0.62, GR = 168
const armAngle = (k, r) => Math.log(r / R0) / PITCH + (k * Math.PI) / 2
const GALAXY = (() => {
  const rnd = mulberry32(42)
  const px = (x, y) => [x * GR, y * GR]
  const warm = [], blue = [], pink = []
  for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++) {
    const r = Math.abs(gauss(rnd)) * 0.075, a = rnd() * Math.PI * 2
    const x = Math.cos(a) * r * 1.7, y = Math.sin(a) * r
    const c = Math.cos(0.45), s = Math.sin(0.45)
    warm.push(px(x * c - y * s, x * s + y * c))
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < 1500; i++) {
    const k = Math.floor(rnd() * 4), rf = R0 + (1 - R0) * Math.pow(rnd(), 1.4)
    const a = armAngle(k, rf) + gauss(rnd) * (0.045 + 0.1 * rf)
      ; (rnd() < 0.08 ? pink : blue).push(px(rf * Math.cos(a), rf * Math.sin(a)))
  }
  return { warm, blue, pink }
})()
const SUN_A = armAngle(1, 0.52)
const SUN_G = [0.52 * Math.cos(SUN_A), 0.52 * Math.sin(SUN_A)]
export const GALAXY_TILT = TILT

/** Posisi Matahari di layar (koordinat viewBox) untuk sudut putar theta (radian). */
export function sunAt(theta) {
  const c = Math.cos(theta), s = Math.sin(theta)
  return [CX + (SUN_G[0] * c - SUN_G[1] * s) * GR, CY + (SUN_G[0] * s + SUN_G[1] * c) * GR * TILT]
}
export const diskTransform = (theta) =>
  `translate(${CX} ${CY}) scale(1 ${TILT}) rotate(${(theta * 180) / Math.PI})`

export function GalaxyScene({ diskRef, sunRef }) {
  const [sx, sy] = sunAt(0)
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 400 250" role="presentation">
      <defs>
        <radialGradient id="us-gdisc"><stop offset="0" stopColor="#ffd6a0" stopOpacity=".35" /><stop offset=".25" stopColor="#ffc896" stopOpacity=".1" /><stop offset=".7" stopColor="#6e96e6" stopOpacity=".05" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#3c5ac8" stopOpacity="0" /></radialGradient>
        <radialGradient id="us-gcore"><stop offset="0" stopColor="#fff0d2" stopOpacity=".9" /><stop offset=".4" stopColor="#ffc88c" stopOpacity=".25" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#ffb478" stopOpacity="0" /></radialGradient>
      </defs>
      <ellipse cx={CX} cy={CY} rx={GR} ry={GR * TILT} fill="url(#us-gdisc)" />
      <g ref={diskRef} transform={diskTransform(0)}>
        <path d={dots(GALAXY.blue)} stroke="rgba(175,208,255,.8)" strokeWidth="1.5" strokeLinecap="round" />
        <path d={dots(GALAXY.pink)} stroke="rgba(255,160,205,.85)" strokeWidth="1.6" strokeLinecap="round" />
        <path d={dots(GALAXY.warm)} stroke="rgba(255,222,176,.85)" strokeWidth="1.4" strokeLinecap="round" />
      </g>
      <ellipse cx={CX} cy={CY} rx={GR * 0.12} ry={GR * 0.12 * TILT} fill="url(#us-gcore)" className={styles.breathe} />
      <g ref={sunRef} transform={`translate(${sx} ${sy})`}>
        {/* cincin kecil = lingkungan bintang tetangga dari level sebelumnya */}
        <circle r="5" fill="rgba(255,211,106,.1)" stroke="rgba(120,160,230,.55)" strokeWidth=".7" />
        <You x={0} y={0} label="YOU ARE HERE" dx={14} dy={-16} />
      </g>
      <text x="14" y="238" className={styles.svgText} fontSize="9" letterSpacing="1" style={FONT}>26,000 LY FROM THE CENTER</text>
    </svg>
  )
}

// ── 5. Local Group ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Skala: 24 px = 1 juta ly. Galaksi digambar membesar supaya terlihat.
const LG = (() => {
  const rnd = mulberry32(11)
  const around = (cx, cy, rad, n) => Array.from({ length: n }, () => {
    const d = rad * Math.sqrt(rnd()), a = rnd() * Math.PI * 2
    return [cx + Math.cos(a) * d, cy + Math.sin(a) * d]
  })
  const m31 = [CX + 52, CY - 30]
  const far = Array.from({ length: 14 }, () => {
    const d = 112 * Math.sqrt(rnd()), a = rnd() * Math.PI * 2
    return [CX + Math.cos(a) * d, CY + Math.sin(a) * d]
  })
  return { m31, m33: [m31[0] - 12, m31[1] - 15], sats: [...around(CX, CY, 22, 14), ...around(m31[0], m31[1], 20, 12), ...far] }
})()
export function LocalGroupScene() {
  const [ax, ay] = LG.m31
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 400 250" role="presentation">
      <defs>
        <radialGradient id="us-lgg"><stop offset="0" stopColor="#e8e0ff" stopOpacity=".9" /><stop offset=".4" stopColor="#a8a0ff" stopOpacity=".35" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#7878ff" stopOpacity="0" /></radialGradient>
      </defs>
      <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r="118" fill="none" stroke="rgba(143,171,198,.35)" strokeDasharray="3 5" strokeWidth=".8" />
      <path d={dots(LG.sats)} stroke="rgba(190,205,255,.75)" strokeWidth="1.8" strokeLinecap="round" />
      <path d={dots([[CX - 6, CY + 6], [CX - 10, CY + 9]])} stroke="#d6e4ff" strokeWidth="2.2" strokeLinecap="round" />
      <ellipse cx={CX} cy={CY} rx="14" ry="8.7" fill="url(#us-lgg)" />
      <ellipse cx={ax} cy={ay} rx="20" ry="7" fill="url(#us-lgg)" transform={`rotate(-35 ${ax} ${ay})`} />
      <ellipse cx={LG.m33[0]} cy={LG.m33[1]} rx="8" ry="6" fill="url(#us-lgg)" />
      <text x={ax + 16} y={ay + 14} className={styles.svgHalo} fill="#c9c3ff" fontSize="9" letterSpacing="1.2" style={FONT}>ANDROMEDA</text>
      <text x={LG.m33[0] - 10} y={LG.m33[1] - 9} textAnchor="end" className={styles.svgHalo} fill="#bcc7f0" fontSize="8.5" letterSpacing="1" style={FONT}>TRIANGULUM</text>
      <You label="YOU ARE IN HERE" dx={-14} dy={20} anchor="end" />
      <path d="M14 232v-4h24v4" fill="none" stroke="rgba(159,176,212,.7)" strokeWidth=".8" transform="translate(0 4)" />
      <text x="44" y="238" className={styles.svgText} fontSize="9" letterSpacing="1" style={FONT}>1 MILLION LY</text>
    </svg>
  )
}

// ── 6. Alam semesta teramati (jaring kosmik) ───────────────────────────
const COSMOS = (() => {
  const rnd = mulberry32(5)
  const inCircle = (rad) => { for (; ;) { const x = (rnd() * 2 - 1) * rad, y = (rnd() * 2 - 1) * rad; if (x * x + y * y <= rad * rad) return [x, y] } }
  const nodes = Array.from({ length: 48 }, () => inCircle(108))
  const edges = new Set()
  nodes.forEach((n, i) => {
    nodes.map((m, j) => [j, Math.hypot(n[0] - m[0], n[1] - m[1])])
      .filter(([j, d]) => j !== i && d < 58).sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]).slice(0, 2)
      .forEach(([j]) => edges.add(i < j ? `${i}-${j}` : `${j}-${i}`))
  })
  const lines = [], a = [], b = [], c = []
  const put = (x, y) => (rnd() < 0.5 ? a : rnd() < 0.7 ? b : c).push([CX + x, CY + y])
  edges.forEach((e) => {
    const [i, j] = e.split('-').map(Number), p = nodes[i], q = nodes[j]
    lines.push(`M${CX + p[0]} ${CY + p[1]}L${CX + q[0]} ${CY + q[1]}`)
    for (let k = 0; k < 9; k++) { const t = rnd(); put(p[0] + (q[0] - p[0]) * t + gauss(rnd) * 2.4, p[1] + (q[1] - p[1]) * t + gauss(rnd) * 2.4) }
  })
  nodes.forEach((n) => { for (let k = 0; k < 12; k++) put(n[0] + gauss(rnd) * 4, n[1] + gauss(rnd) * 4) })
  for (let k = 0; k < 120; k++) { const [x, y] = inCircle(116); (rnd() < 0.5 ? a : b).push([CX + x, CY + y]) }
  return { lines: lines.join(''), a, b, c }
})()
export function CosmosScene() {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 400 250" role="presentation">
      <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r="118" fill="rgba(40,60,140,.07)" stroke="rgba(143,171,198,.4)" strokeDasharray="3 5" strokeWidth=".8" />
      <path d={COSMOS.lines} stroke="rgba(120,150,255,.16)" strokeWidth=".6" fill="none" />
      <path d={dots(COSMOS.a)} stroke="rgba(180,205,255,.8)" strokeWidth="1.3" strokeLinecap="round" />
      <path d={dots(COSMOS.b)} stroke="rgba(255,240,215,.75)" strokeWidth="1.3" strokeLinecap="round" />
      <path d={dots(COSMOS.c)} stroke="rgba(255,175,205,.8)" strokeWidth="1.4" strokeLinecap="round" />
      <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r="4" fill="rgba(143,171,198,.18)" stroke="rgba(143,171,198,.6)" strokeDasharray="1.5 1.5" strokeWidth=".6" />
      <You label="YOU (A POINT HERE)" dx={14} dy={-16} />
      <text x={CX} y="247" textAnchor="middle" className={styles.svgText} fontSize="8.5" letterSpacing="1.4" style={FONT}>EDGE OF THE OBSERVABLE UNIVERSE</text>
    </svg>
  )
}

// RHO[i] = seberapa kecil scene i-1 tampil di dalam scene i (rasio ukuran).
// Dipilih agar ukurannya sama dengan objek penanda di scene i:
//  1: Bumi 74px → planet r=2.6   2: orbit Neptunus 112px → cincin r=5
//  3: lingkungan 108px → cincin r=5   4: spiral 168px → elips rx=14
//  5: Local Group 118px → lingkaran r=4
export const RHO = [null, 2.6 / 74, 5 / 112, 5 / 108, 14 / 168, 4 / 118]

export const SCENES = [EarthScene, SolarScene, NeighborsScene, GalaxyScene, LocalGroupScene, CosmosScene]
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/components/WarpOverlay.jsx =====
import { useRef, memo } from 'react'
import { Canvas, useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber'
import * as THREE from 'three'
import styles from './WarpOverlay.module.css'

// ── Parameter efek warp (sudah di-tone down dari versi awal) ──────────
// Versi awal: 300 bintang, kecepatan x8, garis memanjang hingga 9x, opacity 0.6
export const WARP_RAMP_MS = 1400   // lama akselerasi sampai kecepatan maksimum
const COUNT = 160
const MAX_SPEED = 3.5
const STRETCH = 0.45
const OPACITY = 0.45

function FallingStars({ startTime }) {
  const meshRef = useRef()
  const dummy = useRef(new THREE.Object3D())

  const particles = useRef(
    Array.from({ length: COUNT }).map(() => ({
      x: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20,
      y: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20,
      z: Math.random() * -10,
      speed: Math.random() * 0.5 + 0.5,
    }))
  )

  useFrame((_, delta) => {
    if (!meshRef.current) return

    const elapsed = Date.now() - startTime
    const speedMult = Math.min(elapsed / WARP_RAMP_MS, 1) * MAX_SPEED

    particles.current.forEach((p, i) => {
      p.z += p.speed * speedMult * delta * 60
      if (p.z > 5) {
        p.z = -15
        p.x = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20
        p.y = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20
      }
      dummy.current.position.set(p.x, p.y, p.z)
      dummy.current.scale.set(1, 1, 1 + speedMult * STRETCH)
      dummy.current.updateMatrix()
      meshRef.current.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.current.matrix)
    })
    meshRef.current.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true
  })

  return (
    <instancedMesh ref={meshRef} args={[null, null, COUNT]}>
      <boxGeometry args={[0.012, 0.4, 0.012]} />
      <meshBasicMaterial color="#cceeff" transparent opacity={OPACITY} />
    </instancedMesh>
  )
}

/**
 * Lapisan bintang warp, dipakai oleh semua tombol perpindahan section.
 * Berada di level App (bukan di dalam section) supaya tidak terputus
 * saat section diganti, lalu memudar lewat kelas `.arrive`.
 */
function WarpOverlay({ startTime, arriving }) {
  return (
    <div className={`${styles.overlay} ${arriving ? styles.arrive : ''}`}>
      <Canvas dpr={[1, 1.5]} camera={{ position: [0, 0, 10], fov: 45 }}>
        <FallingStars startTime={startTime} />
      </Canvas>
    </div>
  )
}

export default memo(WarpOverlay)
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/components/WarpOverlay.module.css =====
.overlay {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 5;
  pointer-events: none; /* tombol dan elemen lain tetap aman */
  opacity: 0;
  animation: warpIn 0.7s ease forwards;
}

.overlay canvas {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
}

/* Setelah section baru tampil: bintang memudar pelan */
.arrive {
  animation: warpOut 0.9s ease forwards;
}

@keyframes warpIn {
  from { opacity: 0; }
  to   { opacity: 1; }
}

@keyframes warpOut {
  from { opacity: 1; }
  to   { opacity: 0; }
}

===== FILE: src/components/WishForm.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'
import { sfx } from '../hooks/useSfx.js'
import {
    CATEGORIES,
    MESSAGE_MAX,
    MESSAGE_MIN,
    NAME_MAX,
    getSessionCallsign,
    submitWish,
} from '../services/wishes.js'
import styles from './WishForm.module.css'

const RISE_MS = 2800
const RISE_MS_REDUCED = 1500

const RATING_WORDS = ['', 'A faint glimmer', 'Some starlight', 'A good voyage', 'A bright journey', 'Pure supernova']

const DEFAULT_DRAFT = {
    name: '',
    anonymous: false,
    category: 'wish',
    message: '',
    rating: 0,
    isPrivate: false,
    consent: false,
}

/**
 * Props:
 *  - initialDraft: isi form yang tersimpan saat form ditutup (opsional)
 *  - onClose(draft): form ditutup tanpa kirim
 *  - onSaved(record): kiriman sukses (App menyimpannya sebagai mySubmission)
 *  - onDone(): animasi bintang naik selesai, lanjut ke Secret Ending
 */
export default function WishForm({ initialDraft, onClose, onSaved, onDone }) {
    const uid = useId()
    const [draft, setDraft] = useState({ ...DEFAULT_DRAFT, ...initialDraft })
    const [hoverRating, setHoverRating] = useState(0)
    const [attempted, setAttempted] = useState(false)
    const [status, setStatus] = useState('idle') // idle | sending | rise
    const [serverError, setServerError] = useState('')
    const [honeypot, setHoneypot] = useState('')
    const [viewport, setViewport] = useState(null) // { h, top } dari visualViewport (keyboard virtual)

    const dialogRef = useRef(null)
    const closeRef = useRef(null)
    const submitRef = useRef(null)
    const messageRef = useRef(null)
    const ratingRef = useRef(null)
    const consentRef = useRef(null)
    const timerRef = useRef(0)
    const mountedRef = useRef(false)
    const draftRef = useRef(draft)
    draftRef.current = draft
    const statusRef = useRef(status)
    statusRef.current = status
    const cbRef = useRef({ onClose, onSaved, onDone })
    cbRef.current = { onClose, onSaved, onDone }

    const reduce = useRef(
        typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches
    ).current

    const callsign = getSessionCallsign()
    const set = (patch) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, ...patch }))

    // ── Validasi ──
    const msgLen = draft.message.trim().length
    const errors = {}
    if (msgLen === 0) errors.message = 'Your wish needs a few words, even a tiny one.'
    else if (msgLen < MESSAGE_MIN) errors.message = `A little more, please: at least ${MESSAGE_MIN} characters.`
    if (!draft.rating) errors.rating = 'Please rate your journey. Tap a star.'
    if (!draft.isPrivate && !draft.consent) {
        errors.consent = 'Tick the box to show your message in the Gallery, or send it privately.'
    }
    const show = attempted ? errors : {}

    // ── Tutup ──
    const handleClose = () => {
        if (statusRef.current !== 'idle') return
        sfx.close()
        cbRef.current.onClose?.(draftRef.current)
    }
    const closeRefFn = useRef(handleClose)
    closeRefFn.current = handleClose

    // ── Mount: fokus ke dialog, Esc, visualViewport, cleanup ──
    useEffect(() => {
        mountedRef.current = true
        dialogRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true })

        const onKey = (e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                e.preventDefault()
                closeRefFn.current()
            }
        }
        document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)

        // Keyboard virtual: ikuti visual viewport agar tombol kirim tidak tertutup
        const vv = window.visualViewport
        const sync = () => setViewport({ h: vv.height, top: vv.offsetTop })
        if (vv) {
            sync()
            vv.addEventListener('resize', sync)
            vv.addEventListener('scroll', sync)
        }

        return () => {
            mountedRef.current = false
            clearTimeout(timerRef.current)
            document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
            if (vv) {
                vv.removeEventListener('resize', sync)
                vv.removeEventListener('scroll', sync)
            }
        }
    }, [])

    // Fokus terjebak di dalam dialog (portal ada di akhir body)
    const onKeyDown = (e) => {
        if (e.key !== 'Tab') return
        const active = document.activeElement
        if (e.shiftKey && (active === closeRef.current || active === dialogRef.current)) {
            e.preventDefault()
            submitRef.current?.focus()
        } else if (!e.shiftKey && active === submitRef.current) {
            e.preventDefault()
            closeRef.current?.focus()
        }
    }

    // ── Submit ──
    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (status !== 'idle') return
        setAttempted(true)

        if (errors.message) return messageRef.current?.focus()
        if (errors.rating) return ratingRef.current?.focus()
        if (errors.consent) return consentRef.current?.focus()

        setStatus('sending')
        setServerError('')

        const anonymous = draft.anonymous || !draft.name.trim()
        const payload = {
            displayName: anonymous ? callsign : draft.name.trim(),
            isAnonymous: anonymous,
            category: draft.category,
            message: draft.message.trim(),
            rating: draft.rating,
            isPrivate: draft.isPrivate,
            consent: draft.consent,
            website: honeypot, // honeypot: harus kosong
        }

        try {
            const record = await submitWish(payload)
            if (!mountedRef.current) return
            sfx.reveal()
            cbRef.current.onSaved?.(record)
            setStatus('rise')
            timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => cbRef.current.onDone?.(), reduce ? RISE_MS_REDUCED : RISE_MS)
        } catch (err) {
            if (!mountedRef.current) return
            setStatus('idle')
            setServerError(
                err?.code === 'validation' || err?.code === 'rate_limit'
                    ? err.message
                    : 'Your wish got lost in the dark for a moment. Please try again.'
            )
        }
    }

    const busy = status !== 'idle'
    const rising = status === 'rise'

    const overlayStyle = viewport
        ? { height: `${viewport.h}px`, top: `${viewport.top}px` }
        : undefined

    const content = (
        <div className={`${styles.overlay} ${rising ? styles.overlayRise : ''}`} style={overlayStyle}>
            <div
                ref={dialogRef}
                className={`${styles.box} ${rising ? styles.boxOut : ''}`}
                role="dialog"
                aria-modal="true"
                aria-labelledby={`${uid}-title`}
                aria-describedby={`${uid}-notice`}
                tabIndex={-1}
                onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
            >
                <header className={styles.header}>
                    <div>
                        <p className={styles.eyebrow}>Wishing Star</p>
                        <h3 id={`${uid}-title`} className={styles.title}>
                            Whisper <em>your wish</em>
                        </h3>
                    </div>
                    <button
                        ref={closeRef}
                        type="button"
                        className={styles.close}
                        onClick={handleClose}
                        disabled={busy}
                        aria-label="Close wish form"
                    >
                        ✕
                    </button>
                </header>

                <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
                    <div className={styles.body}>
                        <p id={`${uid}-notice`} className={styles.notice}>
                            {draft.isPrivate
                                ? 'Only the developer will read this message. Still, please don’t share personal information.'
                                : 'Your message will be displayed in the Gallery of Astronauts for other travelers to see. Please don’t include personal information such as email, phone number or address.'}
                        </p>

                        {/* Nama + anonim */}
                        <div className={styles.field}>
                            <label htmlFor={`${uid}-name`} className={styles.label}>
                                Your name <span className={styles.opt}>optional</span>
                            </label>
                            <input
                                id={`${uid}-name`}
                                type="text"
                                className={styles.input}
                                value={draft.anonymous ? callsign : draft.name}
                                onChange={(e) => set({ name: e.target.value })}
                                disabled={draft.anonymous}
                                maxLength={NAME_MAX}
                                placeholder="How should we call you?"
                                autoComplete="off"
                            />
                            <label className={styles.switchRow}>
                                <input
                                    type="checkbox"
                                    role="switch"
                                    className={styles.hiddenInput}
                                    checked={draft.anonymous}
                                    onChange={(e) => set({ anonymous: e.target.checked })}
                                />
                                <span className={styles.switchTrack} aria-hidden="true">
                                    <span className={styles.switchThumb} />
                                </span>
                                <span>Stay anonymous</span>
                            </label>
                            <p className={styles.hintSmall}>
                                {draft.anonymous || !draft.name.trim()
                                    ? `You will fly as ${callsign}.`
                                    : 'Your name will appear on your astronaut card.'}
                            </p>
                        </div>

                        {/* Kategori */}
                        <fieldset className={styles.fieldset}>
                            <legend className={styles.label}>What is this about?</legend>
                            <div className={styles.chips}>
                                {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
                                    <label key={c.id} className={styles.chip}>
                                        <input
                                            type="radio"
                                            name={`${uid}-cat`}
                                            value={c.id}
                                            className={styles.hiddenInput}
                                            checked={draft.category === c.id}
                                            onChange={() => set({ category: c.id })}
                                        />
                                        <span className={styles.chipText}>{c.label}</span>
                                    </label>
                                ))}
                            </div>
                        </fieldset>

                        {/* Pesan */}
                        <div className={styles.field}>
                            <label htmlFor={`${uid}-msg`} className={styles.label}>Your message</label>
                            <textarea
                                id={`${uid}-msg`}
                                ref={messageRef}
                                className={`${styles.input} ${styles.textarea} ${show.message ? styles.invalid : ''}`}
                                rows={4}
                                maxLength={MESSAGE_MAX}
                                value={draft.message}
                                onChange={(e) => set({ message: e.target.value })}
                                placeholder="Write a wish, or tell us what you thought."
                                aria-invalid={Boolean(show.message)}
                                aria-describedby={`${uid}-count${show.message ? ` ${uid}-msg-err` : ''}`}
                            />
                            <div className={styles.metaRow}>
                                <span
                                    id={`${uid}-count`}
                                    className={`${styles.counter} ${draft.message.length >= MESSAGE_MAX - 20 ? styles.counterNear : ''}`}
                                >
                                    {draft.message.length}/{MESSAGE_MAX}
                                </span>
                            </div>
                            {show.message && <p id={`${uid}-msg-err`} className={styles.error}>{show.message}</p>}
                        </div>

                        {/* Rating */}
                        <fieldset
                            ref={ratingRef}
                            tabIndex={-1}
                            className={styles.fieldset}
                            aria-describedby={show.rating ? `${uid}-rate-err` : undefined}
                        >
                            <legend className={styles.label}>How was your journey?</legend>
                            <div className={styles.stars} onPointerLeave={() => setHoverRating(0)}>
                                {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => {
                                    const on = n <= (hoverRating || draft.rating)
                                    return (
                                        <label
                                            key={n}
                                            className={`${styles.star} ${on ? styles.starOn : ''}`}
                                            onPointerEnter={(e) => e.pointerType === 'mouse' && setHoverRating(n)}
                                        >
                                            <input
                                                type="radio"
                                                name={`${uid}-rating`}
                                                value={n}
                                                className={styles.hiddenInput}
                                                checked={draft.rating === n}
                                                onChange={() => {
                                                    set({ rating: n })
                                                    sfx.chime(n)
                                                }}
                                            />
                                            <span aria-hidden="true">★</span>
                                            <span className={styles.srOnly}>{n} {n === 1 ? 'star' : 'stars'}</span>
                                        </label>
                                    )
                                })}
                                <span className={styles.ratingWord} aria-hidden="true">
                                    {RATING_WORDS[hoverRating || draft.rating]}
                                </span>
                            </div>
                            {show.rating && <p id={`${uid}-rate-err`} className={styles.error}>{show.rating}</p>}
                        </fieldset>

                        {/* Private */}
                        <label className={styles.check}>
                            <input
                                type="checkbox"
                                className={styles.hiddenInput}
                                checked={draft.isPrivate}
                                onChange={(e) => set({ isPrivate: e.target.checked })}
                            />
                            <span className={styles.checkBox} aria-hidden="true">✓</span>
                            <span>
                                Send privately to the developer
                                <small className={styles.checkSmall}>It won’t appear in the Gallery.</small>
                            </span>
                        </label>

                        {/* Persetujuan tampil publik (tidak perlu untuk mode private) */}
                        {!draft.isPrivate && (
                            <div className={styles.field}>
                                <label className={styles.check}>
                                    <input
                                        ref={consentRef}
                                        type="checkbox"
                                        className={styles.hiddenInput}
                                        checked={draft.consent}
                                        onChange={(e) => set({ consent: e.target.checked })}
                                        aria-invalid={Boolean(show.consent)}
                                        aria-describedby={show.consent ? `${uid}-consent-err` : undefined}
                                    />
                                    <span className={`${styles.checkBox} ${show.consent ? styles.checkBoxInvalid : ''}`} aria-hidden="true">✓</span>
                                    <span>I agree that my message may be shown publicly in the Gallery of Astronauts.</span>
                                </label>
                                {show.consent && <p id={`${uid}-consent-err`} className={styles.error}>{show.consent}</p>}
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {/* Honeypot (disiapkan untuk fase backend): manusia tidak melihatnya */}
                        <div className={styles.hp} aria-hidden="true">
                            <label>
                                Website
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    name="website"
                                    tabIndex={-1}
                                    autoComplete="off"
                                    value={honeypot}
                                    onChange={(e) => setHoneypot(e.target.value)}
                                />
                            </label>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Footer selalu terlihat (di luar area scroll) */}
                    <div className={styles.footer}>
                        {serverError && <p role="alert" className={`${styles.error} ${styles.serverError}`}>{serverError}</p>}
                        <button ref={submitRef} type="submit" className={styles.submit} disabled={status === 'sending'} aria-busy={status === 'sending'}>
                            {status === 'sending' ? 'Sending to the stars…' : draft.isPrivate ? 'Send privately ✦' : 'Send my wish ✦'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>

            {/* Bintang permintaan naik */}
            {rising && (
                <div className={styles.rise} aria-hidden="true">
                    <span className={styles.riseStar}>✦</span>
                    <p className={styles.riseText}>Your wish is rising to the stars…</p>
                </div>
            )}
            <div role="status" className={styles.srOnly}>
                {rising ? 'Your wish is rising to the stars.' : ''}
            </div>
        </div>
    )

    return createPortal(content, document.body)
}

===== FILE: src/components/WishForm.module.css =====
/* Modal form permintaan. Di-portal ke <body> agar di atas NavDots & MusicPlayer.
   Tinggi mengikuti visual viewport (keyboard virtual) lewat inline style dari JS. */
.overlay {
    position: fixed;
    left: 0;
    top: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100vh;
    height: 100dvh;
    z-index: 400;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: max(0.75rem, env(safe-area-inset-top, 0px)) max(0.75rem, env(safe-area-inset-right, 0px)) max(0.75rem, env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px)) max(0.75rem, env(safe-area-inset-left, 0px));
    background: rgba(2, 2, 10, 0.72);
    backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
    animation: ovIn 0.5s ease;
    transition: background 0.9s ease, backdrop-filter 0.9s ease;
}

.overlayRise {
    background: rgba(2, 2, 10, 0);
    backdrop-filter: blur(0);
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(0);
}

@keyframes ovIn {
