===== FILE: src/hooks/useAudio.js =====
import { useState, useRef, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react'

const SECTION_MUSIC = {
  intro: '/audio/intro.mp3',
  solar: '/audio/solar.mp3',
  phenomena: '/audio/phenomena.mp3',
  deepspace: '/audio/deepspace.mp3',
  ending: '/audio/ending.mp3',
  wishing: '/audio/ending.mp3', // lanjut dari Ending tanpa putus (src sama: tidak crossfade)
  gallery: '/audio/intro.mp3', // hangat & ramah, seperti kembali ke titik berangkat
  default: '/audio/intro.mp3',
}

const FADE_DURATION = 1500
const PRELOAD_DELAY = 1200 // beri waktu render + tekstur Bumi dulu, baru musik Intro disiapkan

// iOS: volume elemen audio DIABAIKAN, jadi fade dan volume lewat GainNode.
// Rantai per track: elemen -> MediaElementSource -> trackGain (fade) -> analyser -> master (volume) -> speaker.

const iosAudioSession = () => {
  // Safari 16.4+: jalur 'playback' tetap bunyi walau tombol senyap menyala
  try { if (navigator.audioSession) navigator.audioSession.type = 'playback' } catch (_) { }
}

// Hemat data seluler: 'none' = tidak mengunduh apa pun sebelum diketuk
function preloadMode() {
  const c = navigator.connection || navigator.mozConnection || navigator.webkitConnection
  if (!c) return 'auto' // iOS Safari tidak punya API ini (dan memang mengabaikan preload)
  if (c.saveData) return 'none'
  if (/(^|-)2g$/.test(c.effectiveType || '')) return 'none'
  if (c.effectiveType === '3g' || c.type === 'cellular') return 'metadata'
  return 'auto'
}

// resume() di Safari lama bisa mengembalikan undefined
const softResume = (ctx) => {
  try {
    const r = ctx.resume()
    r?.catch?.(() => { })
  } catch (_) { }
}

export function useAmbientAudio() {
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false)
  const [volume, setVolumeState] = useState(0.5)
  const [audioProgress, setAudioProgress] = useState({ currentTime: 0, duration: 0, progress: 0 })
  // true = pengunjung ingin suara tetapi AudioContext belum running (iOS senyap/interupsi, atau play gagal)
  const [blocked, setBlocked] = useState(false)

  const audioRef = useRef(null)
  const preparedRef = useRef(null) // elemen musik Intro yang sudah disiapkan sebelum gerbang diketuk
  const currentSrc = useRef(null)
  const volumeRef = useRef(0.5)

  const isPlaying = useRef(false)
  const manualPause = useRef(false) // pengguna sengaja pause / memilih tanpa suara
  const wantSound = useRef(false)   // pengguna pernah memilih suara (gerbang atau tombol play)
  const started = useRef(false)     // gerbang sudah diketuk

  const ctxRef = useRef(null)
  const analyserRef = useRef(null)
  const gainNodeRef = useRef(null)

  const syncBlocked = useCallback(() => {
    const ctx = ctxRef.current
    const want = wantSound.current && !manualPause.current
    setBlocked(!!(want && ctx && ctx.state !== 'running'))
  }, [])

  const getCtx = useCallback(() => {
    if (!ctxRef.current) {
      const AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext
      if (!AC) return null
      const ctx = new AC()
      const analyser = ctx.createAnalyser()
      analyser.fftSize = 256
      analyser.smoothingTimeConstant = 0.8
      const master = ctx.createGain()
      master.gain.value = volumeRef.current
      analyser.connect(master)
      master.connect(ctx.destination)
      ctx.onstatechange = syncBlocked
      ctxRef.current = ctx
      analyserRef.current = analyser
      gainNodeRef.current = master
    }
    return ctxRef.current
  }, [syncBlocked])

  // Sambungkan elemen ke grafik audio SEBELUM play(), dengan gain 0 (tidak ada letupan di iOS).
  // Idempotent. Mengembalikan true bila WebAudio dipakai.
  const connectAudio = useCallback((audio) => {
    if (audioRef.current !== audio) return false
    try {
      const ctx = getCtx()
      if (!ctx) {
        try { audio.volume = 0 } catch (_) { }
        return false
      }
      if (ctx.state !== 'running') softResume(ctx)
      if (!audio._node) {
        audio._node = ctx.createMediaElementSource(audio)
        audio._gain = ctx.createGain()
        audio._gain.gain.value = 0
        audio._node.connect(audio._gain)
        audio._gain.connect(analyserRef.current)
      }
      return true
    } catch (_) {
      try { audio.volume = 0 } catch (__) { }
      return false
    }
  }, [getCtx])

  const getAudioData = useCallback((targetArray) => {
    if (analyserRef.current && targetArray) {
      analyserRef.current.getByteFrequencyData(targetArray)
      return true
    }
    return false
  }, [])

  // Fade level track (0..1). Satu timer selesai per elemen: fade baru membatalkan yang lama.
  // Cadangan tanpa WebAudio: volume elemen diubah langsung (tanpa fade halus).
  const rampTo = useCallback((audio, level, ms = FADE_DURATION, onDone) => {
    if (!audio) return
    clearTimeout(audio._fadeT)
    const ctx = ctxRef.current
    if (audio._gain && ctx) {
      const p = audio._gain.gain
      const t = ctx.currentTime
      p.cancelScheduledValues(t)
      p.setValueAtTime(p.value, t)
      p.linearRampToValueAtTime(level, t + ms / 1000)
    } else {
      try { audio.volume = level * volumeRef.current } catch (_) { }
    }
    if (onDone) audio._fadeT = setTimeout(onDone, ms + 40)
  }, [])

  // Pensiunkan elemen audio: fade-out, lalu bebaskan (tanpa src='' agar tidak memicu error)
  const retire = useCallback((audio) => {
    if (!audio || audio._retired) return
    audio._retired = true
    rampTo(audio, 0, FADE_DURATION, () => {
      audio.pause()
      audio.onerror = null
      audio.ontimeupdate = null
      audio.removeAttribute('src')
      try { audio.load() } catch (_) { }
      try { audio._node?.disconnect() } catch (_) { }
      try { audio._gain?.disconnect() } catch (_) { }
    })
  }, [rampTo])

  const createTrack = useCallback((src, preload = 'auto') => {
    const audio = new Audio()
    audio.crossOrigin = 'anonymous'
    audio.loop = true
    audio.preload = preload
    audio._key = src

    audio.onerror = () => {
      if (audioRef.current === audio) {
        console.warn(`[useAudio] Gagal load: ${src}. Pastikan file ada di public/audio/`)
      }
    }

    // Update progres paling sering 1x per detik (UI hanya menampilkan detik)
    let lastSec = -1
    audio.ontimeupdate = () => {
      if (audioRef.current !== audio) return
      const sec = Math.floor(audio.currentTime)
      if (sec === lastSec) return
      lastSec = sec
      setAudioProgress({
        currentTime: audio.currentTime,
        duration: audio.duration || 0,
        progress: audio.duration ? (audio.currentTime / audio.duration) : 0,
      })
    }

    audio.src = src
    return audio
  }, [])

  // Mengembalikan Promise (menolak jika browser memblokir atau play dibatalkan).
  // Untuk iOS, pemanggilan pertama harus sinkron di dalam handler gestur.
  const playSrc = useCallback((src) => {
    const cur = audioRef.current

    // Track yang sama sudah ada: lanjutkan (membatalkan fade-out yang belum selesai)
    if (cur && cur._key === src && !cur._retired) {
      connectAudio(cur)
      const p = cur.paused ? cur.play() : Promise.resolve()
      return p.then(() => {
        if (audioRef.current !== cur) return
        rampTo(cur, 1)
      })
    }

    retire(cur)
    setAudioProgress({ currentTime: 0, duration: 0, progress: 0 })

    let audio
    if (preparedRef.current && preparedRef.current._key === src) {
      audio = preparedRef.current
      preparedRef.current = null
    } else {
      audio = createTrack(src)
    }

    audioRef.current = audio
    currentSrc.current = src

    connectAudio(audio) // gain 0 sebelum play
    return audio.play().then(() => {
      if (audioRef.current !== audio) return // sudah digantikan selagi menunggu
      rampTo(audio, 1)
    })
  }, [connectAudio, createTrack, retire, rampTo])

  // Dipanggil SEKALI dari ketukan gerbang. withSound=false: tanpa musik (tombol play tetap bisa dipakai).
  const startAudio = useCallback((withSound) => {
    if (started.current) return
    started.current = true
    if (!withSound) {
      manualPause.current = true
      return
    }
    wantSound.current = true
    manualPause.current = false
    iosAudioSession()
    playSrc(currentSrc.current || SECTION_MUSIC.intro)
      .then(() => {
        isPlaying.current = true
        setPlaying(true)
        setTimeout(syncBlocked, 500)
      })
      .catch(() => setBlocked(true))
  }, [playSrc, syncBlocked])

  // Elemen yang dijeda sistem (iOS saat interupsi) dinyalakan lagi bila pengunjung masih ingin musik
  const kick = useCallback(() => {
    const a = audioRef.current
    if (a && a.paused && isPlaying.current && !manualPause.current) a.play().catch(() => { })
  }, [])

  // Dipanggil dari petunjuk "Sound is paused" (ketukan pengunjung = gestur sah)
  const resumeAudio = useCallback(() => {
    if (!wantSound.current) return
    manualPause.current = false
    iosAudioSession()
    const ctx = ctxRef.current
    if (ctx) softResume(ctx)
    if (!isPlaying.current) {
      playSrc(currentSrc.current || SECTION_MUSIC.intro)
        .then(() => {
          isPlaying.current = true
          setPlaying(true)
        })
        .catch(() => { })
    } else {
      kick()
    }
    setTimeout(syncBlocked, 500)
  }, [playSrc, kick, syncBlocked])

  const toggleMusic = useCallback(() => {
    if (!isPlaying.current) {
      manualPause.current = false
      wantSound.current = true
      started.current = true
      iosAudioSession()
      playSrc(currentSrc.current || SECTION_MUSIC.intro)
        .then(() => {
          isPlaying.current = true
          setPlaying(true)
          setTimeout(syncBlocked, 500)
        })
        .catch(() => { })
    } else {
      manualPause.current = true
      const audio = audioRef.current
      if (audio) rampTo(audio, 0, FADE_DURATION, () => audio.pause())
      isPlaying.current = false
      setPlaying(false)
      setBlocked(false)
    }
  }, [playSrc, rampTo, syncBlocked])

  const changeSection = useCallback((sectionId) => {
    const src = SECTION_MUSIC[sectionId] ?? SECTION_MUSIC.default
    if (src === currentSrc.current) return

    if (isPlaying.current) {
      playSrc(src).catch(() => { })
    } else {
      currentSrc.current = src
    }
  }, [playSrc])

  const setVolume = useCallback((val) => {
    const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, val))
    setVolumeState(v)
    volumeRef.current = v
    const ctx = ctxRef.current
    if (gainNodeRef.current && ctx) {
      gainNodeRef.current.gain.setTargetAtTime(v, ctx.currentTime, 0.02)
    } else {
      const a = audioRef.current
      if (a && !a.paused) { try { a.volume = v } catch (_) { } }
    }
  }, [])

  // Kembali ke tab / aplikasi: coba hidupkan lagi AudioContext (iOS menandainya 'interrupted')
  useEffect(() => {
    const onVis = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState !== 'visible') return
      const ctx = ctxRef.current
      if (ctx && wantSound.current && !manualPause.current) {
        if (ctx.state !== 'running') softResume(ctx)
        kick()
        setTimeout(syncBlocked, 500)
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVis)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVis)
  }, [kick, syncBlocked])

  // Siapkan musik Intro lebih awal, tanpa memblokir render dan hemat data seluler
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false
    let idleId = 0
    const run = () => {
      if (cancelled || preparedRef.current || audioRef.current) return
      preparedRef.current = createTrack(SECTION_MUSIC.intro, preloadMode())
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (window.requestIdleCallback) idleId = window.requestIdleCallback(run, { timeout: 2000 })
      else run()
    }, PRELOAD_DELAY)
    return () => {
      cancelled = true
      clearTimeout(timer)
      if (idleId && window.cancelIdleCallback) window.cancelIdleCallback(idleId)
      const p = preparedRef.current
      preparedRef.current = null
      if (p) {
        p.onerror = null
        p.ontimeupdate = null
        p.removeAttribute('src')
        try { p.load() } catch (_) { }
      }
    }
  }, [createTrack])

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      started.current = false // StrictMode: mount ulang mulai dari keadaan bersih
      const a = audioRef.current
      if (a) {
        clearTimeout(a._fadeT)
        a.pause()
        a.onerror = null
        a.ontimeupdate = null
        a.removeAttribute('src')
        try { a.load() } catch (_) { }
      }
      audioRef.current = null
      currentSrc.current = null
      isPlaying.current = false
      try { if (ctxRef.current) ctxRef.current.onstatechange = null } catch (_) { }
      try { ctxRef.current?.close() } catch (_) { }
      ctxRef.current = null
      analyserRef.current = null
      gainNodeRef.current = null
    }
  }, [])

  return {
    playing,
    toggleMusic,
    volume,
    setVolume,
    changeSection,
    getAudioData,
    audioProgress,
    startAudio,
    blocked,
    resumeAudio,
  }
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/hooks/useScroll.js =====
import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react'

// Track scroll progress (0-1) and current active section
export function useScrollProgress() {
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0)
  const [scrollY, setScrollY] = useState(0)

  useEffect(() => {
    const onScroll = () => {
      const total = document.body.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight
      const current = window.scrollY
      setProgress(total > 0 ? current / total : 0)
      setScrollY(current)
    }
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true })
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll)
  }, [])

  return { progress, scrollY }
}

// Track which section is currently in view.
// Aktif = section yang sedang dilewati garis tengah layar. Tidak bergantung pada
// tinggi section, jadi section yang lebih tinggi dari layar (Phenomena, Ending)
// tetap terdeteksi dengan benar.
export function useActiveSection(sectionIds) {
  const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState(sectionIds[0])

  useEffect(() => {
    const observers = []
    sectionIds.forEach((id) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id)
      if (!el) return
      const obs = new IntersectionObserver(
        ([entry]) => { if (entry.isIntersecting) setActiveSection(id) },
        { rootMargin: '-50% 0px -50% 0px', threshold: 0 }
      )
      obs.observe(el)
      observers.push(obs)
    })
    return () => observers.forEach((o) => o.disconnect())
  }, [sectionIds])

  return activeSection
}

// Intersection observer for reveal animations
export function useInView(options = {}) {
  const ref = useRef(null)
  const [inView, setInView] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ref.current) return
    const obs = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => { if (entry.isIntersecting) setInView(true) },
      { threshold: 0.15, ...options }
    )
    obs.observe(ref.current)
    return () => obs.disconnect()
  }, [])

  return [ref, inView]
}

// Mouse parallax effect
export function useMouseParallax(strength = 0.02) {
  const [offset, setOffset] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 })

  useEffect(() => {
    const onMove = (e) => {
      const cx = window.innerWidth / 2
      const cy = window.innerHeight / 2
      setOffset({
        x: (e.clientX - cx) * strength,
        y: (e.clientY - cy) * strength,
      })
    }
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove, { passive: true })
    return () => window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
  }, [strength])

  return offset
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/hooks/useSfx.js =====
// ============================================================
// COSMIC JOURNEY: Synthesized sound effects (no audio files)
// Usage: import { sfx } from '../hooks/useSfx.js'  ->  sfx.chime(2)
// Must be called from a user gesture (click/keypress) the first time.
// Fase 10: sfx.unlock() dipanggil dari ketukan gerbang masuk.
// ============================================================

let ctx = null
let master = null
let noiseBuf = null
let muted = false
try { muted = localStorage.getItem('cj-sfx-muted') === '1' } catch (_) { }

const SCALE = [523.25, 587.33, 659.25, 783.99, 880.0, 987.77, 1174.66, 1318.51] // C major pentatonic-ish

function getCtx() {
  if (muted) return null
  if (!ctx) {
    const AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext
    if (!AC) return null
    ctx = new AC()
    master = ctx.createGain()
    master.gain.value = 0.35
    master.connect(ctx.destination)
  }
  if (ctx.state === 'suspended') ctx.resume()
  return ctx
}

function getNoise(c) {
  if (!noiseBuf) {
    noiseBuf = c.createBuffer(1, c.sampleRate * 2, c.sampleRate)
    const d = noiseBuf.getChannelData(0)
    for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) d[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1
  }
  return noiseBuf
}

// Mengembalikan gain node agar suara bisa dihentikan lebih awal (lihat sfx.open)
function tone({ freq, to, type = 'sine', dur = 0.4, vol = 0.2, delay = 0, attack = 0.01 }) {
  const c = getCtx()
  if (!c) return null
  const t = c.currentTime + delay
  const o = c.createOscillator()
  const g = c.createGain()
  o.type = type
  o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t)
  if (to) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(to, t + dur)
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t)
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol, t + Math.min(attack, dur * 0.5))
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur)
  o.connect(g)
  g.connect(master)
  o.start(t)
  o.stop(t + dur + 0.05)
  return g
}

function noise({ dur = 1, vol = 0.2, from = 300, to = 3000, q = 1, delay = 0, swell = 0.5 }) {
  const c = getCtx()
  if (!c) return null
  const t = c.currentTime + delay
  const src = c.createBufferSource()
  src.buffer = getNoise(c)
  src.loop = true
  const f = c.createBiquadFilter()
  f.type = 'bandpass'
  f.Q.value = q
  f.frequency.setValueAtTime(from, t)
  f.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(to, t + dur)
  const g = c.createGain()
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t)
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol, t + dur * swell)
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur)
  src.connect(f)
  f.connect(g)
  g.connect(master)
  src.start(t)
  src.stop(t + dur + 0.05)
  return g
}

export const sfx = {
  /** Buka AudioContext sfx dari gestur pengguna (iOS): resume + buffer senyap 1 sampel. */
  unlock() {
    try { if (navigator.audioSession) navigator.audioSession.type = 'playback' } catch (_) { }
    const c = getCtx()
    if (!c) return
    try {
      const b = c.createBuffer(1, 1, 22050)
      const s = c.createBufferSource()
      s.buffer = b
      s.connect(c.destination)
      s.start(0)
    } catch (_) { }
  },

  /** Tiny tick for hovering cards */
  hover() { tone({ freq: 1400, to: 1800, dur: 0.06, vol: 0.035 }) },

  /** Generic soft click */
  click() { tone({ freq: 660, to: 440, dur: 0.09, vol: 0.12, type: 'triangle' }) },

  /** Bell-like chime. Pass an index (e.g. planet number) to vary the pitch. */
  chime(i = 0) {
    const f = SCALE[i % SCALE.length]
    tone({ freq: f, dur: 1.5, vol: 0.16 })
    tone({ freq: f * 2, dur: 0.9, vol: 0.05, delay: 0.01 })
    tone({ freq: f * 1.5, dur: 1.1, vol: 0.04, delay: 0.02 })
  },

  /** Deep swelling drone. Mengembalikan fungsi stop() yang memudarkan suara dengan cepat. */
  open() {
    const nodes = [
      tone({ freq: 55, to: 82, dur: 2.4, vol: 0.3, attack: 0.7 }),
      tone({ freq: 110, to: 165, dur: 2.4, vol: 0.1, attack: 0.9, type: 'triangle' }),
      noise({ dur: 2.4, vol: 0.07, from: 80, to: 420, q: 0.7, swell: 0.6 }),
    ].filter(Boolean)
    return () => {
      if (!ctx) return
      const t = ctx.currentTime
      nodes.forEach((g) => {
        try {
          const v = Math.max(g.gain.value, 0.0001)
          g.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t)
          g.gain.setValueAtTime(v, t)
          g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.25)
        } catch (_) { }
      })
    }
  },

  /** Descending blip: closing / going back */
  close() { tone({ freq: 520, to: 180, dur: 0.35, vol: 0.14 }) },

  /** Hyperspace whoosh: warp jumps (tidak lagi dipakai intro) */
  warp(dur = 3) {
    noise({ dur, vol: 0.32, from: 200, to: 5200, q: 0.8, swell: 0.85 })
    tone({ freq: 80, to: 900, dur, vol: 0.08, type: 'sawtooth', attack: dur * 0.6 })
  },

  /** Ascending sparkle arpeggio: message revealed */
  reveal() {
    ;[523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5, 1318.51].forEach((f, i) =>
      tone({ freq: f, dur: 1.6, vol: 0.13, delay: i * 0.12 })
    )
  },

  setMuted(m) {
    muted = !!m
    try { localStorage.setItem('cj-sfx-muted', muted ? '1' : '0') } catch (_) { }
  },
  isMuted() { return muted },
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/sections/DeepSpace.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, memo } from 'react'
import { DEEP_SPACE_LINES } from '../data/index.js'
import UniverseScale from '../components/UniverseScale.jsx'
import styles from './DeepSpace.module.css'

function LocalSupernovas() {
  const [novas, setNovas] = useState([])

  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setNovas((prev) => {
        const newNova = {
          id: Date.now() + Math.random(),
          x: Math.random() * 100,
          y: Math.random() * 100,
          scale: Math.random() * 3 + 2,
          color: ['#ffbb88', '#88ccff', '#ee88ff'][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)]
        }
        return [...prev.slice(-3), newNova]
      })
    }, 2800)
    return () => clearInterval(interval)
  }, [])

  return (
    <div className={styles.supernovaContainer}>
      {novas.map((nova) => (
        <div key={nova.id} className={styles.supernova}
          style={{
            left: `${nova.x}%`, top: `${nova.y}%`,
            '--nova-scale': nova.scale, '--nova-color': nova.color,
          }}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

function DeepSpace({ onFact, onNext }) {
  const [visibleLines, setVisibleLines] = useState([])

  // Dijalankan langsung saat komponen di-mount.
  // IntersectionObserver tidak lagi dibutuhkan karena komponen ini di Lazy-Load (hanya merender saat aktif).
  useEffect(() => {
    const timeouts = DEEP_SPACE_LINES.map((_, i) => {
      return setTimeout(() => {
        setVisibleLines((prev) => [...prev, i])
      }, (i * 700) + 300) // delay sedikit agar sinkron dengan fade CSS 400ms
    })

    return () => timeouts.forEach(clearTimeout)
  }, [])

  const allVisible = visibleLines.length === DEEP_SPACE_LINES.length

  return (
    <section id="deepspace" className={`cosmic-section ${styles.deepspace}`}>
      <LocalSupernovas />

      <div className="noise-overlay" />

      <div className={styles.infinityWrap}>
        <div className={styles.infinity}>∞</div>
        <div className={styles.infinityRing} />
        <div className={styles.infinityRing2} />
      </div>

      <div className={styles.content}>
        <p className="t-label" style={{ marginBottom: '1.5rem' }}>
          Stage III: 10,000 light-years from home
        </p>

        <h2 className={styles.title}>Beyond the Known</h2>

        <div className={styles.lines}>
          {DEEP_SPACE_LINES.map((line, i) => (
            <p
              key={i}
              className={`${styles.line} ${visibleLines.includes(i) ? styles.lineVisible : ''}`}
              style={{ transitionDelay: `${i * 0.1}s` }}
            >
              {line}
            </p>
          ))}
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Zoom-out dari Bumi sampai alam semesta teramati */}
      {allVisible && <UniverseScale />}

      <div className={`${styles.nextNav} ${allVisible ? styles.nextNavVisible : ''}`}>
        <button className={styles.nextBtn} onClick={onNext}>
          Reflect on the Journey <span className={styles.arrow}>↓</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.floatingObjects}>
        {[...Array(6)].map((_, i) => (
          <div
            key={i}
            className={styles.floatObj}
            style={{
              '--x': `${15 + i * 15}%`,
              '--y': `${20 + (i % 3) * 25}%`,
              '--delay': `${i * 1.4}s`,
              '--size': `${1 + (i % 3) * 0.5}px`,
            }}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

export default memo(DeepSpace)
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/sections/Ending.jsx =====
import { useState, useEffect, useMemo, memo } from 'react'
import { sfx } from '../hooks/useSfx.js'
import styles from './Ending.module.css'

// ── DATA PESAN SEMESTA ───────────────────────────────────────────────
const COSMIC_MESSAGES = [
  "Much of you was forged inside stars. Carry that with you.",
  "Stars burn out, but their light keeps travelling long after. Leave some of yours behind.",
  "Out of every way things could have gone, you happened. Be gentle with that.",
  "Space is mostly dark and quiet. That is why even a small light is easy to see.",
  "What you do today reaches further than you can see. Someone will feel it."
]

const ALPHABET_POINTS = {
  A: [[20, 90], [50, 10], [80, 90], [65, 50], [35, 50]],
  B: [[20, 10], [20, 90], [20, 50], [60, 50], [70, 70], [60, 90], [20, 90], [20, 10], [60, 10], [70, 30], [60, 50]],
  C: [[80, 20], [60, 10], [30, 20], [20, 50], [30, 80], [60, 90], [80, 80]],
  D: [[20, 10], [20, 90], [50, 90], [70, 70], [70, 30], [50, 10], [20, 10]],
  E: [[80, 10], [20, 10], [20, 50], [60, 50], [20, 50], [20, 90], [80, 90]],
  F: [[80, 10], [20, 10], [20, 50], [60, 50], [20, 50], [20, 90]],
  G: [[80, 20], [60, 10], [30, 20], [20, 50], [30, 80], [60, 90], [80, 80], [80, 50], [50, 50]],
  H: [[20, 10], [20, 90], [20, 50], [80, 50], [80, 10], [80, 90]],
  I: [[30, 10], [70, 10], [50, 10], [50, 90], [30, 90], [70, 90]],
  J: [[20, 70], [40, 90], [60, 90], [80, 70], [80, 10]],
  K: [[20, 10], [20, 90], [20, 50], [80, 10], [20, 50], [80, 90]],
  L: [[20, 10], [20, 90], [80, 90]],
  M: [[20, 90], [20, 10], [50, 50], [80, 10], [80, 90]],
  N: [[20, 90], [20, 10], [80, 90], [80, 10]],
  O: [[50, 10], [20, 30], [20, 70], [50, 90], [80, 70], [80, 30], [50, 10]],
  P: [[20, 90], [20, 10], [60, 10], [80, 30], [60, 50], [20, 50]],
  Q: [[50, 10], [20, 30], [20, 70], [50, 90], [80, 70], [80, 30], [50, 10], [70, 70], [90, 90]],
  R: [[20, 90], [20, 10], [60, 10], [80, 30], [60, 50], [20, 50], [50, 50], [80, 90]],
  S: [[80, 20], [50, 10], [20, 30], [50, 50], [80, 70], [50, 90], [20, 80]],
  T: [[10, 10], [90, 10], [50, 10], [50, 90]],
  U: [[20, 10], [20, 70], [40, 90], [60, 90], [80, 70], [80, 10]],
  V: [[20, 10], [50, 90], [80, 10]],
  W: [[10, 10], [30, 90], [50, 50], [70, 90], [90, 10]],
  X: [[20, 10], [80, 90], [50, 50], [80, 10], [20, 90]],
  Y: [[20, 10], [50, 50], [80, 10], [50, 50], [50, 90]],
  Z: [[20, 10], [80, 10], [20, 90], [80, 90]]
}

// Bangun titik rasi bintang dari huruf pertama nama (dipakai tampilan + kartu share)
function makeConstellation(name) {
  let char = name.trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase()
  if (!ALPHABET_POINTS[char]) char = 'O'
  return ALPHABET_POINTS[char].map(([x, y]) => ({
    x: 90 + x * 1.2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 8,
    y: 20 + y * 1.2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 8,
  }))
}

function LetterConstellation({ name, points }) {
  return (
    <div className={styles.constellationWrap}>
      <svg viewBox="0 0 300 160" className={styles.constellationSvg}>
        {points.map((p, i) => {
          const next = points[i + 1]
          return next ? (
            <line key={`line-${i}`} x1={p.x} y1={p.y} x2={next.x} y2={next.y} stroke="rgba(136, 170, 255, 0.4)" strokeWidth="1.5" strokeDasharray="4 4" />
          ) : null
        })}
        {points.map((p, i) => (
          <g key={`star-${i}`}>
            <circle cx={p.x} cy={p.y} r="4" fill="#ffffff" filter="drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(136, 170, 255, 0.8))" />
            <circle cx={p.x} cy={p.y} r="1.5" fill="#fff" />
          </g>
        ))}
      </svg>
      <p className={styles.constellationLabel}>Constellation {name}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

// ── SHARE CARD: render konstelasi + pesan ke PNG 1080×1350 ─────────────
function wrapLines(g, text, maxWidth) {
  const words = text.split(' ')
  const lines = []
  let line = ''
  for (const w of words) {
    const test = line ? `${line} ${w}` : w
    if (g.measureText(test).width > maxWidth && line) {
      lines.push(line)
      line = w
    } else {
      line = test
    }
  }
  if (line) lines.push(line)
  return lines
}

async function drawShareCard({ name, from, message, points, coords }) {
  try {
    await Promise.all([
      document.fonts.load('italic 300 60px "Cormorant Garamond"'),
      document.fonts.load('300 24px "Outfit"'),
      document.fonts.load('400 24px "Outfit"'),
    ])
  } catch (_) { /* font fallback */ }

  const W = 1080, H = 1350
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
  canvas.width = W
  canvas.height = H
  const g = canvas.getContext('2d')
  const serif = '"Cormorant Garamond", Georgia, serif'
  const sans = '"Outfit", system-ui, sans-serif'
  // Warna teks kecil di kartu: lantai sama dengan --text-muted (#9fb0d4)
  const MUTED = '#9fb0d4'
  const setSpacing = (px) => { if ('letterSpacing' in g) g.letterSpacing = `${px}px` }

  // background
  const bg = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H)
  bg.addColorStop(0, '#02021a')
  bg.addColorStop(0.55, '#0b0720')
  bg.addColorStop(1, '#16091f')
  g.fillStyle = bg
  g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H)

  // soft nebula washes
  const wash = (x, y, r, color) => {
    const rg = g.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, r)
    rg.addColorStop(0, color)
    rg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)')
    g.fillStyle = rg
    g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H)
  }
  wash(220, 260, 520, 'rgba(80,110,255,0.16)')
  wash(900, 1050, 560, 'rgba(160,80,255,0.14)')

  // star dust
  for (let i = 0; i < 320; i++) {
    const r = Math.random() * 1.6 + 0.3
    g.fillStyle = `rgba(210,225,255,${Math.random() * 0.7 + 0.15})`
    g.beginPath()
    g.arc(Math.random() * W, Math.random() * H, r, 0, Math.PI * 2)
    g.fill()
  }

  // frame
  g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(136,170,255,0.25)'
  g.lineWidth = 2
  g.beginPath()
  g.roundRect ? g.roundRect(40, 40, W - 80, H - 80, 28) : g.rect(40, 40, W - 80, H - 80)
  g.stroke()

  // constellation (fit into 520×340 area centred at y=330)
  const xs = points.map((p) => p.x), ys = points.map((p) => p.y)
  const minX = Math.min(...xs), maxX = Math.max(...xs)
  const minY = Math.min(...ys), maxY = Math.max(...ys)
  const scale = Math.min(520 / ((maxX - minX) || 1), 340 / ((maxY - minY) || 1))
  const pts = points.map((p) => ({
    x: W / 2 + (p.x - (minX + maxX) / 2) * scale,
    y: 330 + (p.y - (minY + maxY) / 2) * scale,
  }))

  g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(136,170,255,0.55)'
  g.lineWidth = 2.5
  g.setLineDash([10, 10])
  g.beginPath()
  pts.forEach((p, i) => (i ? g.lineTo(p.x, p.y) : g.moveTo(p.x, p.y)))
  g.stroke()
  g.setLineDash([])

  pts.forEach((p) => {
    const halo = g.createRadialGradient(p.x, p.y, 0, p.x, p.y, 30)
    halo.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(160,190,255,0.85)')
    halo.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(160,190,255,0)')
    g.fillStyle = halo
    g.fillRect(p.x - 30, p.y - 30, 60, 60)
    g.fillStyle = '#fff'
    g.beginPath()
    g.arc(p.x, p.y, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2)
    g.fill()
  })

  g.textAlign = 'center'

  // label under constellation
  g.fillStyle = MUTED
  g.font = `400 24px ${sans}`
  setSpacing(5)
  g.fillText(`CONSTELLATION ${name.toUpperCase()}`, W / 2, 560)

  // divider
  g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)'
  g.lineWidth = 1
  g.beginPath()
  g.moveTo(160, 600)
  g.lineTo(W - 160, 600)
  g.stroke()

  // header
  g.fillStyle = MUTED
  g.font = `400 24px ${sans}`
  setSpacing(4)
  g.fillText('DECRYPTED TRANSMISSION', W / 2, 668)
  setSpacing(0)

  // "Dear Name," mengecil sampai muat
  let size = 70
  g.fillStyle = '#aaccff'
  do {
    g.font = `italic 300 ${size}px ${serif}`
    size -= 4
  } while (g.measureText(`Dear ${name},`).width > 880 && size > 30)
  g.fillText(`Dear ${name},`, W / 2, 770)

  // message: font mengecil sampai muat di atas tanda tangan/footer
  g.fillStyle = '#e0e5ff'
  let msgSize = 54
  let lines = []
  while (true) {
    g.font = `italic 300 ${msgSize}px ${serif}`
    lines = wrapLines(g, `“${message}”`, 840)
    if (lines.length * msgSize * 1.45 <= 270 || msgSize <= 32) break
    msgSize -= 2
  }
  const lh = msgSize * 1.45
  lines.forEach((ln, i) => g.fillText(ln, W / 2, 870 + i * lh))

  // signature (untuk "someone else")
  if (from) {
    const sigY = Math.min(870 + (lines.length - 1) * lh + 105, 1185)
    g.fillStyle = MUTED
    g.font = `400 24px ${sans}`
    setSpacing(3)
    g.fillText('WITH COSMIC LOVE,', W / 2, sigY - 44)
    setSpacing(0)
    let sigSize = 56
    g.fillStyle = '#ff99cc'
    do {
      g.font = `italic 300 ${sigSize}px ${serif}`
      sigSize -= 4
    } while (g.measureText(from).width > 700 && sigSize > 28)
    g.fillText(from, W / 2, sigY + 8)
  }

  // footer
  g.fillStyle = MUTED
  g.font = `400 24px ${sans}`
  setSpacing(4)
  g.fillText('COSMIC JOURNEY', W / 2, H - 110)
  g.font = `400 21px ${sans}`
  setSpacing(2)
  g.fillText(`COSMIC COORDS: ${coords}`, W / 2, H - 76)
  setSpacing(0)

  return new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve, 'image/png'))
}

// ── KOMPONEN BINTANG GEMERLAP (BACKGROUND KARTU) ──────────────────────
function ModalStars() {
  const stars = useMemo(() => Array.from({ length: 60 }).map(() => ({
    x: Math.random() * 100,
    y: Math.random() * 100,
    size: Math.random() * 2 + 1,
    delay: Math.random() * 4,
    duration: Math.random() * 2 + 2
  })), [])

  return (
    <div className={styles.modalStarsWrap}>
      {stars.map((s, i) => (
        <div key={i} className={styles.modalStar} style={{
          left: `${s.x}%`, top: `${s.y}%`,
          width: `${s.size}px`, height: `${s.size}px`,
          animationDelay: `${s.delay}s`,
          animationDuration: `${s.duration}s`
        }} />
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

// ── KOMPONEN HEART SUPERNOVA (LEDAKAN HATI RANDOM) ────────────────────
function HeartSupernovas() {
  const [novas, setNovas] = useState([])

  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setNovas((prev) => {
        const newNova = {
          id: Date.now() + Math.random(),
          x: Math.random() * 100,
          y: Math.random() * 100,
          scale: Math.random() * 1.5 + 1.5, // Skala acak
          color: ['#ff99cc', '#ffb3e6', '#e6ccff', '#ffccff', '#ffd700'][Math.floor(Math.random() * 5)]
        }
        return [...prev.slice(-4), newNova] // Maksimal 5 ledakan di DOM
      })
    }, 1800) // Meledak setiap 1.8 detik

    return () => clearInterval(interval)
  }, [])

  return (
    <div className={styles.heartNovaWrap}>
      {novas.map((nova) => (
        <div key={nova.id} className={styles.heartNova} style={{
          left: `${nova.x}%`, top: `${nova.y}%`,
          '--nova-scale': nova.scale,
          '--nova-color': nova.color
        }}>
          {/* SVG Hati */}
          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor">
            <path d="M12 21.35l-1.45-1.32C5.4 15.36 2 12.28 2 8.5 2 5.42 4.42 3 7.5 3c1.74 0 3.41.81 4.5 2.09C13.09 3.81 14.76 3 16.5 3 19.58 3 22 5.42 22 8.5c0 3.78-3.4 6.86-8.55 11.54L12 21.35z" />
          </svg>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

// ── KOMPONEN UTAMA ENDING ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function Ending({ visitor, onReveal, onNext, onBackToChoices, resumeMessage = false }) {
  const { name: recipient, sender, constellationPoints: constellation, finalMessage, coords, wishSubmitted } = visitor
  const isOther = Boolean(sender)
  const hasMessage = Boolean(finalMessage)
  // Wish sudah terkirim (datang dari Secret Ending): tombol utama kembali ke pilihan, bukan mengulang Wishing
  const canGoBackToChoices = Boolean(wishSubmitted && onBackToChoices)

  const [isHovered, setIsHovered] = useState(false)
  // Kembali dari Secret Ending ("Stay with my constellation"): langsung buka pesan lagi
  const [modalState, setModalState] = useState(resumeMessage && hasMessage ? 'message' : 'closed')
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ type: 'self', name: '', from: '', to: '', note: '' })
  const [sharing, setSharing] = useState(false)

  const handleOpenForm = () => {
    sfx.open()
    setModalState('form')
    setFormData({ type: 'self', name: '', from: '', to: '', note: '' })
  }

  const handleReveal = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()

    let name = ''
    let from = ''
    let message = ''
    if (formData.type === 'self') {
      name = formData.name.trim()
      if (!name) return
      message = COSMIC_MESSAGES[Math.floor(Math.random() * COSMIC_MESSAGES.length)]
    } else {
      name = formData.to.trim()
      from = formData.from.trim()
      if (!name || !from) return
      message = formData.note.trim() || COSMIC_MESSAGES[Math.floor(Math.random() * COSMIC_MESSAGES.length)]
    }

    // Data pengunjung disimpan di App (state in-memory, tanpa localStorage)
    onReveal({
      name,
      sender: from,
      finalMessage: message,
      constellationPoints: makeConstellation(name),
      coords: `${Math.random().toFixed(4)} N, ${Math.random().toFixed(4)} W`,
    })
    sfx.reveal()
    setModalState('message')
  }

  // X / Esc pada pesan: hanya menutup pesan (tombol lanjut muncul di halaman)
  const handleCloseMessage = () => {
    sfx.click()
    setModalState('closed')
  }

  const handleShareCard = async () => {
    if (sharing) return
    setSharing(true)
    sfx.click()
    try {
      const blob = await drawShareCard({ name: recipient, from: sender, message: finalMessage, points: constellation, coords })
      if (!blob) return
      const safe = recipient.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-') || 'cosmic'
      const file = new File([blob], `constellation-${safe}.png`, { type: 'image/png' })

      // Mobile: native share sheet. Desktop / tidak didukung: download.
      if (navigator.canShare && navigator.canShare({ files: [file] })) {
        try {
          await navigator.share({ files: [file], title: 'A message from the universe' })
          return
        } catch (err) {
          if (err && err.name === 'AbortError') return
        }
      }
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
      const a = document.createElement('a')
      a.href = url
      a.download = file.name
      document.body.appendChild(a)
      a.click()
      a.remove()
      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000)
    } finally {
      setSharing(false)
    }
  }

  // Esc: tutup form / tutup pesan saja
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e) => {
      if (e.key !== 'Escape') return
      if (modalState === 'form') setModalState('closed')
      else if (modalState === 'message') setModalState('closed')
    }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
  }, [modalState])

  return (
    <section id="ending" className={`cosmic-section ${styles.ending}`}>
      <div className={styles.bgGradient} />
      <div className="noise-overlay" />

      <div className={styles.eclipseContainer}>
        {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((i) => (
          <div
            key={i}
            className={`${styles.recordRing} ${isHovered ? styles.ringActive : ''}`}
            style={{ '--i': i }}
          />
        ))}
        <div className={styles.eclipseCore} />
      </div>

      <div className={styles.content}>
        <p className={`t-label ${styles.eyebrow}`}>
          Final Transmission
        </p>

        <h2 className={styles.title}>
          The Universe<br />
          <em>has a message for you</em>
        </h2>

        <p className={styles.sub}>
          You have traveled through 13.8 billion years of cosmic history.<br />
          Now it is the universe&rsquo;s turn to speak.
        </p>

        <button
          className={styles.messageBtn}
          onClick={handleOpenForm}
          onMouseEnter={() => setIsHovered(true)}
          onMouseLeave={() => setIsHovered(false)}
        >
          <span className={styles.btnStar}>✦</span>
          Receive your message
          <span className={styles.btnStar}>✦</span>
        </button>

        {hasMessage && modalState === 'closed' && (
          canGoBackToChoices ? (
            <button className={styles.nextBtn} onClick={() => { sfx.click(); onBackToChoices() }}>
              Back to choices ✦
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button className={styles.nextBtn} onClick={() => { sfx.click(); onNext?.() }}>
              Next: Make a Wish ✦
            </button>
          )
        )}

        <p className={styles.footer}>
          Every journey through the cosmos begins and ends at home.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className={`${styles.modalOverlay} ${modalState !== 'closed' ? styles.modalOpen : ''}`}>

        {/* Latar belakang khusus saat pesan muncul (gemerlap & supernova) */}
        {modalState === 'message' && (
          <>
            <ModalStars />
            <HeartSupernovas />
          </>
        )}

        {modalState === 'form' && (
          <div className={styles.modalBox}>
            <button className={styles.closeBtn} onClick={() => setModalState('closed')} aria-label="Close form">✕</button>
            <h3 className={styles.modalTitle}>Set Transmission Target</h3>

            <form onSubmit={handleReveal} className={styles.formContainer}>
              <div className={styles.radioGroup}>
                <label className={formData.type === 'self' ? styles.radioActive : ''}>
                  <input type="radio" name="type" checked={formData.type === 'self'} onChange={() => setFormData({ ...formData, type: 'self' })} />
                  For Myself
                </label>
                <label className={formData.type === 'other' ? styles.radioActiveOther : ''}>
                  <input type="radio" name="type" checked={formData.type === 'other'} onChange={() => setFormData({ ...formData, type: 'other' })} />
                  For Someone Else ✨
                </label>
              </div>

              {formData.type === 'self' ? (
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Enter your name..."
                  className={styles.nameInput}
                  value={formData.name}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, name: e.target.value })}
                  required
                  maxLength={30}
                />
              ) : (
                <div className={styles.validationBox}>
                  <p className={styles.validationHint}>Who is this transmission from, and for whom? 💌</p>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="From (your name)..."
                    className={styles.nameInput}
                    value={formData.from}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, from: e.target.value })}
                    required
                    maxLength={30}
                  />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="To (their name)..."
                    className={styles.nameInput}
                    value={formData.to}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, to: e.target.value })}
                    required
                    maxLength={30}
                  />
                  <textarea
                    placeholder="Write your own message, or leave it empty and the universe will write one"
                    className={`${styles.nameInput} ${styles.noteInput}`}
                    value={formData.note}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, note: e.target.value })}
                    rows={4}
                    maxLength={240}
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              <button type="submit" className={styles.submitBtn}>Decode Message →</button>
            </form>
          </div>
        )}

        {modalState === 'message' && (
          <div className={styles.ticketBox}>
            <button className={styles.closeBtn} onClick={handleCloseMessage} aria-label="Close message">✕</button>

            <div className={styles.ticketContent}>
              <LetterConstellation name={recipient} points={constellation} />
              <p className={`t-label ${styles.ticketLabel}`}>DECRYPTED TRANSMISSION</p>
              <h3 className={styles.recipientName}>
                Dear <span className={styles.highlightName}>{recipient}</span>,
              </h3>
              <p className={styles.finalMessageText}>"{finalMessage}"</p>

              {isOther && (
                <div className={styles.signOff}>
                  <p>With cosmic love,</p>
                  <p className={styles.signature}>{sender}</p>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className={styles.ticketFooter}>
                <span>COSMIC COORDS: {coords}</span>
                <span>STATUS: DELIVERED</span>
              </div>
              <button className={styles.shareBtn} onClick={handleShareCard} disabled={sharing}>
                {sharing ? 'Rendering card…' : '✦ Save as card'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

export default memo(Ending)
===== END FILE =====

// src/sections/Gallery.jsx [UBAH: menimpa file lama]
import { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import styles from './Gallery.module.css'
import { getWishes, CATEGORIES, PAGE_SIZE } from '../services/wishes.js'
import { sfx } from '../hooks/useSfx.js'

const CATEGORY_LABEL = Object.fromEntries(CATEGORIES.map((c) => [c.id, c.label]))
const SORTS = [
    { id: 'newest', label: 'Newest' },
    { id: 'rating', label: 'Highest rated' },
]

// Bintang kecil berdenyut di latar (posisi deterministik, dihitung sekali)
const STAR_DOTS = Array.from({ length: 32 }, (_, i) => {
    const r = (n) => {
        const x = Math.sin(i * 12.9898 + n * 78.233) * 43758.5453
        return x - Math.floor(x)
    }
    return { left: r(1) * 100, top: r(2) * 100, size: 1 + r(3) * 2, delay: r(4) * 5, dur: 3 + r(5) * 4 }
})

function timeAgo(iso) {
    const s = Math.max(0, (Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime()) / 1000)
    if (s < 60) return 'just now'
    const m = Math.floor(s / 60)
    if (m < 60) return `${m}m ago`
    const h = Math.floor(m / 60)
    if (h < 24) return `${h}h ago`
    const d = Math.floor(h / 24)
    if (d < 7) return `${d}d ago`
    const w = Math.floor(d / 7)
    if (d < 30) return `${w}w ago`
    const mo = Math.floor(d / 30)
    if (mo < 12) return `${mo}mo ago`
    return `${Math.floor(d / 365)}y ago`
}

function Helmet() {
    return (
        <svg className={styles.helmet} viewBox="0 0 32 32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
            <path d="M16 3C9 3 4.5 8 4.5 14.5V19c0 4 3 7 6.5 7h10c3.5 0 6.500-3 6.500-7v-4.500C27.500 8 23 3 16 3z"
                fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5" />
            <path d="M9 13.500c0-2.800 2.800-4.800 7-4.800s7 2 7 4.800v2.200c0 2.600-2.800 4.300-7 4.300s-7-1.700-7-4.300z"
                fill="rgba(136,170,255,0.18)" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.2" />
            <path d="M11.500 12c1-.9 2.300-1.300 3.800-1.300" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.2" strokeLinecap="round" />
        </svg>
    )
}

function Stars({ value }) {
    return (
        <span className={styles.stars} role="img" aria-label={`Rated ${value} out of 5`}>
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => (
                <span key={n} aria-hidden="true" className={n <= value ? styles.starOn : styles.starOff}>★</span>
            ))}
        </span>
    )
}

function Card({ item, mine }) {
    return (
        <article className={`${styles.card} ${mine ? styles.mine : ''}`}>
            {mine && <span className={styles.yourBadge}>Your wish</span>}
            <header className={styles.cardHead}>
                <Helmet />
                <div className={styles.who}>
                    <span className={styles.name}>{item.displayName}</span>
                    <span className={styles.role}>{item.isAnonymous ? 'Anonymous astronaut' : 'Astronaut'}</span>
                </div>
                <Stars value={item.rating} />
            </header>
            <p className={styles.message}>{item.message}</p>
            <footer className={styles.cardFoot}>
                <span className={styles.cat}>{CATEGORY_LABEL[item.category] ?? item.category}</span>
                <time className={styles.time} dateTime={item.createdAt}>{timeAgo(item.createdAt)}</time>
            </footer>
        </article>
    )
}

function Skeletons() {
    return (
        <div className={styles.grid} role="status" aria-label="Loading messages">
            {Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => (
                <div key={i} className={`${styles.card} ${styles.skel}`} aria-hidden="true">
                    <div className={styles.skelLine} style={{ width: '55%' }} />
                    <div className={styles.skelLine} style={{ width: '95%' }} />
                    <div className={styles.skelLine} style={{ width: '80%' }} />
                    <div className={styles.skelLine} style={{ width: '35%' }} />
                </div>
            ))}
        </div>
    )
}

function Gallery({ visitor, onBackToConstellation, onReturnHome }) {
    const [sort, setSort] = useState('newest')
    const [category, setCategory] = useState('all')
    const [items, setItems] = useState([])
    const [nextCursor, setNextCursor] = useState(null)
    const [aggregate, setAggregate] = useState(null)
    const [status, setStatus] = useState('loading') // loading | ready | error
    const [more, setMore] = useState('idle')        // idle | loading | error
    const [retryKey, setRetryKey] = useState(0)
    const reqRef = useRef(0)

    const mySubmission = visitor?.mySubmission
    const cameFromSecret = Boolean(visitor?.wishSubmitted || mySubmission)
    const showMine = Boolean(
        mySubmission && !mySubmission.isPrivate && (category === 'all' || mySubmission.category === category)
    )

    useEffect(() => {
        const id = ++reqRef.current
        setStatus('loading')
        setMore('idle')
        setItems([])
        setNextCursor(null)
        getWishes({ sort, category, limit: PAGE_SIZE })
            .then((res) => {
                if (id !== reqRef.current) return
                setItems(res.items)
                setNextCursor(res.nextCursor)
                setAggregate(res.aggregate)
                setStatus('ready')
            })
            .catch(() => {
                if (id !== reqRef.current) return
                setStatus('error')
            })
        return () => { reqRef.current++ } // abaikan respons yang datang setelah unmount/ganti filter
    }, [sort, category, retryKey])

    const loadMore = useCallback(() => {
        if (!nextCursor || more === 'loading') return
        sfx.click()
        const id = reqRef.current
        setMore('loading')
        getWishes({ sort, category, cursor: nextCursor, limit: PAGE_SIZE })
            .then((res) => {
                if (id !== reqRef.current) return
                setItems((prev) => {
                    const seen = new Set(prev.map((i) => i.id))
                    return [...prev, ...res.items.filter((i) => !seen.has(i.id))]
                })
                setNextCursor(res.nextCursor)
                setMore('idle')
            })
            .catch(() => {
                if (id !== reqRef.current) return
                setMore('error')
            })
    }, [nextCursor, more, sort, category])

    const pick = (setter, value, current) => {
        if (value === current) return
        sfx.click()
        setter(value)
    }

    const summary = useMemo(() => {
        if (!aggregate) return null
        if (!aggregate.count) return 'Be the first astronaut to leave a message'
        return `${aggregate.average.toFixed(1)} ★ from ${aggregate.count} ${aggregate.count === 1 ? 'astronaut' : 'astronauts'}`
    }, [aggregate])

    // Mode AUTO_APPROVE: wish pengirim sendiri ikut dikembalikan server. Buang dari daftar
    // supaya hanya kartu "Your wish" yang tampil. Cocokkan lewat id; cadangan: nama + pesan + rating
    // yang sama (jaga-jaga bila id lokal berbeda dari id server).
    const visibleItems = useMemo(() => {
        if (!showMine) return items
        const my = mySubmission
        return items.filter(
            (i) =>
                i.id !== my.id &&
                !(i.displayName === my.displayName && i.message === my.message && i.rating === my.rating)
        )
    }, [items, showMine, mySubmission])

    const nothing = status === 'ready' && visibleItems.length === 0 && !showMine

    return (
        <section id="gallery" className={styles.gallery}>
            <div className={styles.sky} aria-hidden="true">
                {STAR_DOTS.map((d, i) => (
                    <span
                        key={i}
                        className={styles.dot}
                        style={{
                            left: `${d.left}%`, top: `${d.top}%`,
                            width: d.size, height: d.size,
                            animationDelay: `${d.delay}s`, animationDuration: `${d.dur}s`,
                        }}
                    />
                ))}
            </div>

            <div className={styles.inner}>
                <div className={styles.topBar}>
                    <button className={styles.btn} onClick={() => { sfx.close(); onBackToConstellation?.() }}>
                        ← Back to the stars
                    </button>
                    {cameFromSecret && (
                        <button className={styles.btn} onClick={() => { sfx.click(); onReturnHome?.() }}>
                            Return home
                        </button>
                    )}
                </div>

                <header className={styles.head}>
                    <p className={styles.label}>Astronaut Gallery</p>
                    <h2 className={styles.title}>
                        Voices <em>among the stars</em>
                    </h2>
                    <p className={styles.summary} aria-live="polite">{summary ?? '\u00A0'}</p>
                </header>

                <div className={styles.controls}>
                    <div className={styles.segment} role="group" aria-label="Sort messages">
                        {SORTS.map((s) => (
                            <button
                                key={s.id}
                                className={`${styles.seg} ${sort === s.id ? styles.segOn : ''}`}
                                aria-pressed={sort === s.id}
                                onClick={() => pick(setSort, s.id, sort)}
                            >
                                {s.label}
                            </button>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                    <div className={styles.chips} role="group" aria-label="Filter by category">
                        {[{ id: 'all', label: 'All' }, ...CATEGORIES].map((c) => (
                            <button
                                key={c.id}
                                className={`${styles.chip} ${category === c.id ? styles.chipOn : ''}`}
                                aria-pressed={category === c.id}
                                onClick={() => pick(setCategory, c.id, category)}
                            >
                                {c.label}
                            </button>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </div>

                {status === 'loading' && !showMine && <Skeletons />}

                {status === 'error' && (
                    <div className={styles.state} role="alert">
                        <p>The signal got lost on its way here.</p>
                        <button className={styles.btn} onClick={() => setRetryKey((k) => k + 1)}>Try again</button>
                    </div>
                )}

                {nothing && (
                    <div className={styles.state}>
                        <p>{category === 'all'
                            ? 'No messages among the stars yet. Yours could be the first.'
                            : 'No messages in this corner of the sky yet.'}</p>
                        {category !== 'all' && (
                            <button className={styles.btn} onClick={() => pick(setCategory, 'all', category)}>Show all</button>
                        )}
                    </div>
                )}

                {(showMine || (status !== 'error' && visibleItems.length > 0)) && (
                    <div className={styles.grid}>
                        {showMine && <Card item={mySubmission} mine />}
                        {visibleItems.map((it) => <Card key={it.id} item={it} />)}
                    </div>
                )}

                {status === 'loading' && showMine && (
                    <p className={styles.loadingNote} role="status">Gathering more voices…</p>
                )}

                {status === 'ready' && nextCursor && (
                    <div className={styles.moreWrap}>
                        <button className={styles.btn} onClick={loadMore} disabled={more === 'loading'}>
                            {more === 'loading' ? 'Loading…' : 'Load more'}
                        </button>
                        {more === 'error' && (
                            <p className={styles.moreErr} role="alert">Couldn't load more. Please try again.</p>
                        )}
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </section>
    )
}

export default memo(Gallery)
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/sections/Intro.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, useCallback, Suspense, memo } from 'react'
import { Canvas, useFrame, useLoader } from '@react-three/fiber'
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { sfx } from '../hooks/useSfx.js'
import styles from './Intro.module.css'

const HOLD_MS = 2500      // lama menahan tombol
const RELEASE_MS = 700    // lama progres kembali tenang saat dilepas
const MOON_KM = 384400
const EARTH_PX = 220      // diameter Bumi (px) di wrapper; dipakai untuk hitung skala transisi
const MOON_R = 3.6
const ISS_R = 2.75

function makeMoonTex() {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas')
  c.width = 128; c.height = 64
  const x = c.getContext('2d')
  x.fillStyle = '#9a9a9a'
  x.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 64)
  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
    x.fillStyle = `rgba(${Math.random() < 0.5 ? '60,60,60' : '210,210,210'},${Math.random() * 0.35})`
    x.beginPath()
    x.arc(Math.random() * 128, Math.random() * 64, Math.random() * 7 + 1, 0, 6.3)
    x.fill()
  }
  return new THREE.CanvasTexture(c)
}

function OrbitLine({ r, color, opacity }) {
  return (
    <mesh rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}>
      <ringGeometry args={[r - 0.006, r + 0.006, 128]} />
      <meshBasicMaterial color={color} transparent opacity={opacity} side={THREE.DoubleSide} depthWrite={false} />
    </mesh>
  )
}

function Earth3D({ isWarping, warpStartTime, travelMs, holdRef }) {
  const root = useRef()
  const earth = useRef()
  const moon = useRef()
  const iss = useRef()
  const colorMap = useLoader(THREE.TextureLoader, '/images/earth.jpg')
  const moonTex = useMemo(makeMoonTex, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (colorMap) {
      colorMap.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping
      colorMap.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping
      colorMap.anisotropy = 16
    }
  }, [colorMap])

  useFrame((state, delta) => {
    const p = holdRef.current
    const t = isWarping ? Math.min((Date.now() - warpStartTime) / travelMs, 1) : 0
    const el = state.clock.elapsedTime

    // Getar halus saat ditahan, mereda begitu perjalanan mulai
    if (root.current) {
      const a = p * p * 0.06 * (1 - Math.min(t * 2, 1))
      root.current.position.set((Math.random() - 0.5) * a, (Math.random() - 0.5) * a, 0)
    }
    if (earth.current) earth.current.rotation.y += (0.09 + p * 1.2 + t * t * 1.5) * delta

    if (moon.current) {
      const a = el * 0.18
      moon.current.position.set(Math.cos(a) * MOON_R, 0, Math.sin(a) * MOON_R)
    }
    if (iss.current) {
      const a = el * 0.9
      iss.current.position.set(Math.cos(a) * ISS_R, 0, Math.sin(a) * ISS_R)
    }
  })

  return (
    <group ref={root}>
      <group ref={earth}>
        <mesh>
          <sphereGeometry args={[2.18, 64, 64]} />
          <meshBasicMaterial color="#020813" />
        </mesh>
        <mesh>
          <sphereGeometry args={[2.2, 64, 64]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial map={colorMap} roughness={0.8} metalness={0.2} alphaTest={0.5} />
        </mesh>
        <mesh>
          <sphereGeometry args={[2.25, 64, 64]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#6ec5ff" transparent opacity={0.15} depthWrite={false} side={THREE.FrontSide} />
        </mesh>
      </group>

      {/* Bulan */}
      <group rotation={[1.1, 0, -0.2]}>
        <OrbitLine r={MOON_R} color="#8fb0ff" opacity={0.15} />
        <mesh ref={moon}>
          <sphereGeometry args={[0.32, 32, 32]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial map={moonTex} roughness={1} />
        </mesh>
      </group>

      {/* ISS (inklinasi ±51,6°, orbit cepat) */}
      <group rotation={[0.9, 0, 0.35]}>
        <OrbitLine r={ISS_R} color="#ffd27a" opacity={0.12} />
        <group ref={iss} scale={1.5}>
          <mesh><boxGeometry args={[0.24, 0.012, 0.012]} /><meshBasicMaterial color="#e6e9f2" /></mesh>
          <mesh><boxGeometry args={[0.05, 0.04, 0.04]} /><meshBasicMaterial color="#cfd4e2" /></mesh>
          <mesh position={[-0.1, 0, 0]}><boxGeometry args={[0.05, 0.004, 0.14]} /><meshBasicMaterial color="#d9a441" /></mesh>
          <mesh position={[0.1, 0, 0]}><boxGeometry args={[0.05, 0.004, 0.14]} /><meshBasicMaterial color="#d9a441" /></mesh>
        </group>
      </group>
    </group>
  )
}

const wib = () =>
  new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { timeZone: 'Asia/Jakarta', hour12: false })

function Clock() {
  const [t, setT] = useState(wib)
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => setT(wib()), 1000)
    return () => clearInterval(id)
  }, [])
  return <div className={styles.clock}>{t}</div>
}

function Intro({ onNext, isWarping = false, warpStartTime = 0, travelMs = 2800 }) {
  const holdRef = useRef(0) // 0..1, dibaca oleh Three.js tanpa re-render
  const wrapRef = useRef(null)
  const btnRef = useRef(null)
  const distRef = useRef(null)
  const st = useRef({ holding: false, last: 0, raf: 0, stop: null })
  const nextRef = useRef(onNext)
  nextRef.current = onNext

  const tick = useCallback((now) => {
    const s = st.current
    const dt = Math.min(now - s.last, 50)
    s.last = now
    const p = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, holdRef.current + (s.holding ? dt / HOLD_MS : -dt / RELEASE_MS)))
    holdRef.current = p

    btnRef.current?.style.setProperty('--p', p)
    if (distRef.current) {
      distRef.current.textContent = `${((MOON_KM * (1 - p)) / 1e5).toFixed(3)} × 10⁵ km from home`
    }

    if (s.holding && p >= 1) {
      // Penuh: berangkat. Suara dibiarkan selesai sendiri.
      s.holding = false
      s.stop = null
      s.raf = 0
      nextRef.current()
      return
    }
    s.raf = p > 0 || s.holding ? requestAnimationFrame(tick) : 0
  }, [])

  const start = () => {
    const s = st.current
    if (s.holding || isWarping || holdRef.current >= 1) return
    s.holding = true
    s.last = performance.now()
    if (!s.stop) s.stop = sfx.open()
    if (!s.raf) s.raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick)
  }

  const end = () => {
    const s = st.current
    if (!s.holding) return
    s.holding = false
    s.stop?.()
    s.stop = null
  }

  useEffect(() => () => {
    cancelAnimationFrame(st.current.raf)
    st.current.stop?.()
  }, [])

  // Saat berangkat: Bumi bergeser & mengecil ke posisi Bumi di section Solar System
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const el = wrapRef.current
    if (!isWarping || !el) return
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect()
    const vw = window.innerWidth
    const vh = window.innerHeight
    const mobile = vw < 768
    const camZ = mobile ? 34 : 26                                   // sama dgn CameraRig overview
    const unit = vh / (2 * camZ * Math.tan(THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(20))) // px per unit scene Solar
    const posX = mobile ? -2.1 : 2.5                                // posisi Earth (index 2) di grid
    const posY = mobile ? 1.6 : 3
    const tx = vw / 2 + posX * unit - (r.left + r.width / 2)
    const ty = vh / 2 - posY * unit - (r.top + r.height / 2)
    const s = ((68 / 65) * 2 * unit) / EARTH_PX                     // radius Earth = 68/65
    el.style.transitionDuration = `${travelMs}ms`
    el.style.transform = `translate(${tx}px, ${ty}px) scale(${s})`
  }, [isWarping, travelMs])

  return (
    <section id="intro" className={`cosmic-section ${styles.intro}`}>
      <div className="noise-overlay" />
      <div className={styles.nebulaBg} />
      <div
        className={`${styles.sunGlow} ${isWarping ? styles.sunGlowOn : ''}`}
        style={{ transitionDuration: `${travelMs}ms` }}
      />

      <div className={`${styles.hud} ${isWarping ? styles.hudHidden : ''}`}>
        <p className={styles.hudLabel}>WIB · GMT+7 · Indonesia Barat</p>
        <Clock />
        <div className={styles.hudIss}>
          <span className={styles.issDot} />
          <span className={styles.hudLabel}>ISS · ±408 km · orbit 92 min</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div ref={wrapRef} className={`${styles.earthWrap} ${isWarping ? styles.earthWrapWarp : ''}`}>
        <div className={styles.earthFloat}>
          <div className={styles.earthGlow} />
          <div className={styles.atmosphereRing} />
          <Canvas className={styles.earthCanvas} dpr={[1, 1.5]} camera={{ position: [0, 0, 15.8], fov: 45 }}>
            <ambientLight intensity={0.05} />
            <directionalLight position={[5, 4, 2]} intensity={2.5} color="#fff4e6" />
            <Suspense fallback={null}>
              <Earth3D isWarping={isWarping} warpStartTime={warpStartTime} travelMs={travelMs} holdRef={holdRef} />
            </Suspense>
          </Canvas>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className={`${styles.content} ${isWarping ? styles.contentHidden : ''}`}>
        <p ref={distRef} className={`t-label ${styles.coords}`}>3.844 × 10⁵ km from home</p>
        <h1 className={styles.heading}>
          Welcome aboard,<br /><em>astronaut.</em>
        </h1>
        <p className={styles.sub}>
          Fasten your seatbelt. Our journey begins.
        </p>
        <p className={styles.local} lang="id">
          Kencangkan sabuk pengamanmu.
        </p>
        <button
          ref={btnRef}
          className={styles.beginBtn}
          onPointerDown={(e) => { e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId); start() }}
          onPointerUp={end}
          onPointerCancel={end}
          onBlur={end}
          onKeyDown={(e) => { if ((e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') && !e.repeat) { e.preventDefault(); start() } }}
          onKeyUp={(e) => { if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') end() }}
          onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
        >
          Begin the Journey <span className={styles.arrow}>↓</span>
        </button>
        <p className={styles.holdHint}>Hold to launch</p>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

export default memo(Intro)
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/sections/Phenomena.jsx =====
import { useState, useRef, useEffect, memo } from 'react'
import { PHENOMENA } from '../data/index.js'
import { sfx } from '../hooks/useSfx.js'
import styles from './Phenomena.module.css'
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'

// Reduced motion atau mode Comfort (body.comfort-mode): tanpa tilt dan tanpa efek ketik
const calmMotion = () =>
  typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
  (window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ||
    document.body.classList.contains('comfort-mode'))

// Potong deskripsi di batas kata (bukan di tengah kata), tambah elipsis bila terpotong
function truncateWords(text, max = 75) {
  if (!text || text.length <= max) return text || ''
  const cut = text.slice(0, max)
  // Jika karakter berikutnya spasi, potongan sudah tepat di batas kata
  const atBoundary = /\s/.test(text.charAt(max))
  const lastSpace = cut.lastIndexOf(' ')
  const base = atBoundary || lastSpace < 30 ? cut : cut.slice(0, lastSpace)
  return base.replace(/[\s,.;:!?-]+$/, '') + '…'
}

// ── KOMPONEN EFEK MESIN KETIK (TERMINAL SCI-FI) ────────────────────────
// instant = true: teks langsung tampil utuh (ketuk untuk melewati, Comfort, reduced motion)
function Typewriter({ text, speed = 25, delay = 0, instant = false, onDone }) {
  const [displayed, setDisplayed] = useState(instant ? text : '')
  const onDoneRef = useRef(onDone)

  useEffect(() => {
    onDoneRef.current = onDone
  }, [onDone])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (instant) {
      setDisplayed(text)
      return
    }
    setDisplayed('')
    let timeout
    let interval

    timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      let i = 0
      interval = setInterval(() => {
        setDisplayed(text.substring(0, i + 1))
        i++
        if (i >= text.length) {
          clearInterval(interval)
          onDoneRef.current?.()
        }
      }, speed)
    }, delay)

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timeout)
      clearInterval(interval)
    }
  }, [text, speed, delay, instant])

  return <span>{displayed}</span>
}

// ── KOMPONEN 2.5D PARALLAX CARD ────────────────────────────────────────
function TiltCard({ p, onClick }) {
  const [tilt, setTilt] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 })
  const cardRef = useRef(null)

  const handleMouseMove = (e) => {
    if (!cardRef.current || calmMotion()) return
    const rect = cardRef.current.getBoundingClientRect()
    const x = e.clientX - rect.left
    const y = e.clientY - rect.top
    const centerX = rect.width / 2
    const centerY = rect.height / 2
    const rotateX = ((y - centerY) / centerY) * -15
    const rotateY = ((x - centerX) / centerX) * 15
    setTilt({ x: rotateX, y: rotateY })
  }

  const handleMouseLeave = () => setTilt({ x: 0, y: 0 })

  return (
    <div className={styles.cardWrapper}>
      <div
        ref={cardRef}
        className={styles.cardHUD}
        style={{
          '--glow': p.glowColor,
          '--border': p.borderColor,
          transform: `perspective(1000px) rotateX(${tilt.x}deg) rotateY(${tilt.y}deg)`,
        }}
        onMouseMove={handleMouseMove}
        onMouseEnter={() => sfx.hover()}
        onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
        onClick={() => onClick(p)}
        role="button"
        tabIndex={0}
        aria-label={`Open ${p.name}`}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
            e.preventDefault()
            onClick(p)
          }
        }}
      >
        <div className={styles.icon3D} style={{ boxShadow: `0 0 30px ${p.glowColor}88` }}>
          {getIcon(p.id)}
        </div>
        <h3 className={styles.cardTitle3D}>{p.name}</h3>
        <p className={styles.cardDesc3D}>{truncateWords(p.description, 75)}</p>

        <div className={styles.cardFooter3D}>
          <span className={styles.discover}>Initialize Scan →</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ── KOMPONEN UTAMA PHENOMENA ───────────────────────────────────────────
function Phenomena({ onFact, onNext }) {
  const [activePhenom, setActivePhenom] = useState(null)
  // revealAll: seluruh teks langsung tampil (ketuk, tombol "Show all text", Comfort, reduced motion)
  const [revealAll, setRevealAll] = useState(false)
  // finished: efek ketik selesai sendiri
  const [finished, setFinished] = useState(false)

  const handleSelect = (p) => {
    setActivePhenom(p)
    setRevealAll(calmMotion())
    setFinished(false)
    sfx.open()
    if (onFact) onFact(null)
  }

  const closeOverlay = () => {
    setActivePhenom(null)
    sfx.close()
  }

  // Ketuk di mana saja pada overlay (selain tombol) = tampilkan seluruh teks
  const handleOverlayClick = (e) => {
    if (!activePhenom || revealAll) return
    if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('button')) return
    setRevealAll(true)
  }

  // Esc closes the cinematic overlay
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activePhenom) return
    const onKey = (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') closeOverlay()
    }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
  }, [activePhenom])

  const typing = !revealAll && !finished

  return (
    <section id="phenomena" className={`cosmic-section ${styles.phenomena}`}>
      <div className="noise-overlay" />
      <div className={styles.bgGradient} />

      <div className={styles.header}>
        <p className="t-label">Stage II: Beyond the Solar System</p>
        <h2 className={styles.title}>Cosmic Phenomena</h2>
        <p className={styles.subtitle}>The most extreme things in the universe</p>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.scatterGrid}>
        {PHENOMENA.map((p) => (
          <TiltCard key={p.id} p={p} onClick={handleSelect} />
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className={styles.nextNav}>
        <button className={styles.nextBtn} onClick={onNext}>
          Enter Deep Space <span className={styles.arrow}>↓</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {createPortal(
        <div
          className={`${styles.cinematicOverlay} ${activePhenom ? styles.open : ''} ${activePhenom && typing ? styles.tappable : ''}`}
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          aria-hidden={!activePhenom}
          onClick={handleOverlayClick}
        >
          {activePhenom && (
            <>
              <button className={styles.closeBtn} onClick={closeOverlay} autoFocus>
                [ ESC ] ABORT
              </button>
              <div className={styles.cinematicContent}>
                <div className={styles.cinematicIconWrap} style={{ '--glow': activePhenom.glowColor }}>
                  {getIcon(activePhenom.id)}
                </div>
                <div className={styles.cinematicTextData}>
                  <p className="t-label" style={{ color: activePhenom.glowColor, marginBottom: '10px' }}>
                    DATA FRAGMENT ACQUIRED
                  </p>
                  <h2 className={styles.cinematicTitle} style={{ '--glow': activePhenom.glowColor }}>
                    {activePhenom.name}
                  </h2>
                  <div className={styles.cinematicDesc}>
                    <Typewriter text={activePhenom.description} speed={25} instant={revealAll} />
                  </div>
                  <div className={styles.terminalExtra} style={{ '--glow': activePhenom.glowColor }}>
                    <strong>&gt; ANALYSIS: </strong>
                    <Typewriter
                      text={activePhenom.fact2}
                      speed={30}
                      delay={activePhenom.description.length * 25 + 500}
                      instant={revealAll}
                      onDone={() => setFinished(true)}
                    />
                    <span className={styles.cursorBlock}>█</span>
                  </div>
                  {typing && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className={styles.skipBtn}
                      onClick={() => setRevealAll(true)}
                    >
                      Show all text
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>,
        document.body
      )}
    </section>
  )
}

export default memo(Phenomena)

// ── ANIMATED SVG ICONS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Animation classes live in Phenomena.module.css (ico*). All rotate/scale
// around the viewBox centre (30,30) and respect prefers-reduced-motion.
function getIcon(id) {
  switch (id) {
    case 'blackhole': return <BlackHoleIcon />
    case 'supernova': return <SupernovaIcon />
    case 'nebula': return <NebulaIcon />
    case 'pulsar': return <PulsarIcon />
    case 'quasar': return <QuasarIcon />
    case 'magnetar': return <MagnetarIcon />
    case 'wormhole': return <WormholeIcon />
    case 'darkmatter': return <DarkMatterIcon />
    default: return null
  }
}

const dur = (s) => ({ animationDuration: `${s}s` })
const delay = (s) => ({ animationDelay: `${s}s` })

// Matter streaming around the event horizon
function BlackHoleIcon() {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 60 60" width="60%" height="60%">
      <defs>
        <radialGradient id="bhGrad" cx="50%" cy="50%">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#000" />
          <stop offset="60%" stopColor="#1a0008" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#330015" />
        </radialGradient>
      </defs>
      <circle cx="30" cy="30" r="28" fill="url(#bhGrad)" />
      <g transform="rotate(-18 30 30)">
        <ellipse cx="30" cy="30" rx="24" ry="6" fill="none" stroke="#ff55bb" strokeWidth="1.2" opacity="0.85"
          strokeDasharray="14 6" className={styles.icoDash} style={dur(2.4)} />
        <ellipse cx="30" cy="30" rx="20" ry="4" fill="none" stroke="#ff99dd" strokeWidth="0.9" opacity="0.6"
          strokeDasharray="8 12" className={`${styles.icoDash} ${styles.icoDashRev}`} style={dur(3.2)} />
        <ellipse cx="30" cy="30" rx="15" ry="3" fill="none" stroke="#ffd0ee" strokeWidth="0.7" opacity="0.5"
          strokeDasharray="4 6" className={styles.icoDash} style={dur(1.8)} />
      </g>
      <circle cx="30" cy="30" r="10" fill="#000" className={styles.icoPulse} />
      <circle cx="30" cy="30" r="11.5" fill="none" stroke="#ff55bb" strokeWidth="0.6" opacity="0.5" className={styles.icoPulse} />
    </svg>
  )
}

// Spinning rays, expanding shockwave, throbbing core
function SupernovaIcon() {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 60 60" width="60%" height="60%">
      <circle cx="30" cy="30" r="10" fill="none" stroke="#ffdd55" strokeWidth="1" className={styles.icoShock} />
      <circle cx="30" cy="30" r="10" fill="none" stroke="#ffee88" strokeWidth="0.6" className={styles.icoShock} style={delay(1.3)} />
      <g className={styles.icoSpinSlow}>
        <g className={styles.icoPulse} style={dur(2.2)}>
          {[0, 45, 90, 135, 180, 225, 270, 315].map((angle, i) => (
            <line key={i} x1="30" y1="30"
              x2={30 + 24 * Math.cos((angle * Math.PI) / 180)}
              y2={30 + 24 * Math.sin((angle * Math.PI) / 180)}
              stroke="#ffdd55" strokeWidth={i % 2 === 0 ? 1.5 : 0.8} opacity={i % 2 === 0 ? 0.9 : 0.5} />
          ))}
        </g>
      </g>
      <circle cx="30" cy="30" r="9" fill="#ffee88" opacity="0.9" className={styles.icoPulse} style={dur(1.6)} />
      <circle cx="30" cy="30" r="5" fill="#ffffff" opacity="0.95" />
    </svg>
  )
}

@keyframes cinematicPulse {

  0%,
