===== FILE: src/sections/SecretEnding.jsx =====
import { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { sfx } from '../hooks/useSfx.js'
import styles from './SecretEnding.module.css'

const LINE_COUNT = 4

const prefersReduced = () =>
    typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
    window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches

function SecretEnding({ visitor, onVisitorChange, onStay, onGallery, onReturnHome }) {
    const sub = visitor?.mySubmission
    const callsign = sub?.displayName || visitor?.name || 'Traveler'
    const isPrivate = Boolean(sub?.isPrivate)

    // Sudah pernah melihat urutan ini (mis. kembali dari "Stay with my constellation")
    // maka langsung tampil lengkap, tanpa mengulang sinematiknya.
    const seen = useRef(Boolean(visitor?.secretSeen)).current
    const reduce = useMemo(() => prefersReduced(), [])

    const lines = useMemo(() => [
        { id: 'a', big: true, lead: 'Your wish is now a star.' },
        {
            id: 'b',
            lead: 'Thank you, ',
            name: callsign,
            tail: ', for spending a little time here.',
        },
        {
            id: 'c',
            lead: isPrivate
                ? 'Your words went only to the person who built this site.'
                : 'Your words will sit among the other astronauts, for anyone who passes by.',
        },
        {
            id: 'd',
            lead: 'Every light you saw here travelled a very long way to reach you. Thank you for looking up.',
        },
    ], [callsign, isPrivate])

    const [shown, setShown] = useState(seen ? LINE_COUNT : 0)
    const [ready, setReady] = useState(seen)
    const timers = useRef([])
    const sectionRef = useRef(null)
    const primaryRef = useRef(null)

    // Bintang kecil pelengkap langit (posisi acak, dibuat sekali)
    const dots = useMemo(() => Array.from({ length: reduce ? 14 : 22 }, () => ({
        x: Math.random() * 100,
        y: Math.random() * 100,
        size: 1.5 + Math.random() * 1.8,
        delay: 0.8 + Math.random() * 5,
        dur: 2.6 + Math.random() * 2.4,
    })), [reduce])

    const clearTimers = () => {
        timers.current.forEach(clearTimeout)
        timers.current = []
    }

    useEffect(() => {
        if (seen) return
        const start = reduce ? 900 : 2400
        const gap = reduce ? 3200 : 4800

        for (let i = 0; i < LINE_COUNT; i++) {
            timers.current.push(setTimeout(() => {
                setShown(i + 1)
                if (i === 0) sfx.chime(2)
            }, start + i * gap))
        }
        timers.current.push(setTimeout(() => {
            setReady(true)
            onVisitorChange?.({ secretSeen: true })
            sfx.chime(4)
        }, start + (LINE_COUNT - 1) * gap + 3000))

        return clearTimers
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, [])

    // Tombol pilihan muncul: fokus ke tombol utama, supaya pengguna keyboard tidak "kehilangan"
    // fokus saat tombol Skip hilang. Tidak mencuri fokus jika pengguna sedang di tempat lain.
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!ready) return
        const a = document.activeElement
        if (!a || a === document.body || sectionRef.current?.contains(a)) {
            primaryRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true })
        }
    }, [ready])

    const handleSkip = () => {
        sfx.click()
        clearTimers()
        setShown(LINE_COUNT)
        setReady(true)
        onVisitorChange?.({ secretSeen: true })
    }

    const act = (fn) => () => {
        sfx.click()
        fn?.()
    }

    return (
        <section ref={sectionRef} id="secret-ending" className={styles.secret} aria-label="Thank you">
            <div className={styles.sky} aria-hidden="true">
                {dots.map((d, i) => (
                    <span
                        key={i}
                        className={styles.dot}
                        style={{
                            left: `${d.x}%`,
                            top: `${d.y}%`,
                            width: `${d.size}px`,
                            height: `${d.size}px`,
                            '--d': `${d.delay}s`,
                            '--dur': `${d.dur}s`,
                        }}
                    />
                ))}
            </div>

            <div className={styles.stage}>
                <div className={styles.inner}>
                    <div className={`${styles.starBox} ${seen ? styles.instant : ''}`} aria-hidden="true">
                        <div className={styles.halo} />
                        <svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" className={styles.starSvg}>
                            <path
                                d="M50 4 C53 38 62 47 96 50 C62 53 53 62 50 96 C47 62 38 53 4 50 C38 47 47 38 50 4Z"
                                fill="#fff"
                            />
                            <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="7" fill="#fff" />
                        </svg>
                    </div>

                    <p className={`${styles.tag} ${seen ? styles.instant : ''}`}>
                        ✦ {callsign}’s wish
                    </p>

                    <div className={styles.lines} aria-live="polite">
                        {lines.slice(0, shown).map((l) => (
                            <p
                                key={l.id}
                                className={`${styles.line} ${l.big ? styles.lineBig : ''} ${seen ? styles.instant : ''}`}
                            >
                                {l.lead}
                                {l.name && <span className={styles.name}>{l.name}</span>}
                                {l.tail}
                            </p>
                        ))}
                    </div>

                    {!ready && (
                        <button type="button" className={styles.skip} onClick={handleSkip}>
                            Skip ›
                        </button>
                    )}

                    {ready && (
                        <div className={styles.actions}>
                            <button
                                ref={primaryRef}
                                type="button"
                                className={`${styles.btn} ${styles.btnPrimary}`}
                                style={{ '--d': '0s' }}
                                onClick={act(onStay)}
                            >
                                Stay with my constellation
                            </button>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                className={`${styles.btn} ${styles.btnSecondary}`}
                                style={{ '--d': '0.15s' }}
                                onClick={act(onGallery)}
                            >
                                View the Gallery
                            </button>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                className={`${styles.btn} ${styles.btnGhost}`}
                                style={{ '--d': '0.3s' }}
                                onClick={act(onReturnHome)}
                            >
                                Return home
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>
    )
}

export default memo(SecretEnding)
===== END FILE =====

/* Secret Ending: wrapper setinggi layar (100dvh), tanpa scroll di level section.
   Fase 9B: token teks, scrim di belakang blok teks, glow berwarna pada teks diganti
   text-shadow gelap. Latar section hanya wash warna tipis (tidak menggelapkan),
   jadi tidak ada "ganda redup" dengan depth 0.8. */

        radial-gradient(ellipse 70% 45% at 50% 16%, rgba(85, 119, 255, 0.2), transparent 70%),

    .line {
        animation: fadeIn 1.4s ease forwards;
    }

    .btn {
        animation: fadeIn 0.8s ease var(--d, 0s) both;
    }

    .btn:hover {
        transform: none;
    }
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/sections/SolarSystem.jsx =====
import { useState, useRef, useMemo, useEffect, Suspense, memo } from 'react'
import { Canvas, useFrame, useLoader, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber'
import { Html, OrbitControls } from '@react-three/drei'
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { PLANETS } from '../data/index.js'
import { sfx } from '../hooks/useSfx.js'
import styles from './SolarSystem.module.css'

// ── 1. GENERATOR TEKSTUR GLOBAL (Di-cache untuk performa tinggi) ───────

// A. Tekstur Cincin Garis-garis (Saturnus/Uranus)
function createRingAlphaMap() {
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
  canvas.width = 1024; canvas.height = 1024
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')

  ctx.fillStyle = '#000000'
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 1024, 1024)

  for (let i = 0; i < 70; i++) {
    ctx.beginPath()
    const r = 290 + (i * 3.1)
    ctx.arc(512, 512, r, 0, Math.PI * 2)
    ctx.setLineDash([Math.random() * 200 + 30, Math.random() * 15])
    ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255, 255, 255, ${Math.random() * 0.9 + 0.1})`
    ctx.lineWidth = Math.random() * 3 + 0.5
    ctx.stroke()
  }
  return new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas)
}

// B. Tekstur Glow Atmosfer (Untuk Sprite Belakang Planet)
function createGlowTexture() {
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
  canvas.width = 256; canvas.height = 256
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
  const center = 128
  const gradient = ctx.createRadialGradient(center, center, 0, center, center, center)

  // Mereplika persis CSS: radial-gradient(circle, transparent 45%, rgba(...) 65%, rgba(...) 80%, transparent 100%)
  gradient.addColorStop(0.0, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0)')
  gradient.addColorStop(0.45, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0)')
  gradient.addColorStop(0.65, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.25)')
  gradient.addColorStop(0.80, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5)')
  gradient.addColorStop(1.0, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0)')

  ctx.fillStyle = gradient
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 256)
  return new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas)
}

// C. Tekstur Glow Matahari (gradien penuh, hangat)
function createSunTexture() {
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
  canvas.width = 512; canvas.height = 512
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
  const c = 256
  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(c, c, 0, c, c, c)
  g.addColorStop(0.00, 'rgba(255, 255, 240, 1)')
  g.addColorStop(0.10, 'rgba(255, 236, 170, 0.95)')
  g.addColorStop(0.28, 'rgba(255, 176, 72, 0.55)')
  g.addColorStop(0.55, 'rgba(255, 112, 40, 0.16)')
  g.addColorStop(1.00, 'rgba(255, 80, 20, 0)')
  ctx.fillStyle = g
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 512, 512)
  return new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas)
}

const sharedRingTexture = createRingAlphaMap()
const sharedGlowTexture = createGlowTexture()
const sharedSunTexture = createSunTexture()

// ── Fungsi Warna Atmosfer Ceria (Vivid) ────────────────────────────────
function getVibrantAtmosphere(planetName, defaultColor) {
  const vividColors = {
    'Mercury': '#ffbbaa',
    'Venus': '#ffcc55',
    'Earth': '#44baff',
    'Mars': '#ff5533',
    'Jupiter': '#ffaa66',
    'Saturn': '#ffdd77',
    'Uranus': '#00eeff',
    'Neptune': '#3366ff'
  }
  return vividColors[planetName] || defaultColor
}

// ── 2. Komponen Khusus Bumi (Sesuai Section Intro) ─────────────────────
function EarthMesh({ radius }) {
  const colorMap = useLoader(THREE.TextureLoader, '/images/earth.jpg')

  useEffect(() => {
    if (colorMap) {
      colorMap.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
      colorMap.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
      colorMap.anisotropy = 16;
    }
  }, [colorMap]);

  return (
    <mesh castShadow receiveShadow>
      <sphereGeometry args={[radius, 48, 48]} />
      <meshStandardMaterial
        map={colorMap}
        roughness={0.8}
        metalness={0.2}
        alphaTest={0.5}
      />
    </mesh>
  )
}

// ── 2b. Matahari: inti terang + corona yang berdenyut di tepi kiri layar ─
function Sun() {
  const { size } = useThree()
  const isMobile = size.width < 768
  const x = isMobile ? -15 : -26
  const r = isMobile ? 5 : 6.5

  const innerGlow = useRef()
  const outerGlow = useRef()
  const corona = useRef()

  useFrame((state) => {
    const t = state.clock.elapsedTime
    if (innerGlow.current) innerGlow.current.scale.setScalar(r * 5 * (1 + Math.sin(t * 0.8) * 0.04))
    if (outerGlow.current) outerGlow.current.scale.setScalar(r * 10 * (1 + Math.sin(t * 0.5 + 1) * 0.05))
    if (corona.current) {
      corona.current.material.rotation = t * 0.04
      corona.current.scale.setScalar(r * 3.4 * (1 + Math.sin(t * 1.3) * 0.03))
    }
  })

  const spriteProps = {
    transparent: true,
    depthWrite: false,
    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
  }

  // z = -1 agar planet (z = 0) menutupi glow, bukan tersapu olehnya
  return (
    <group position={[x, 0, -1]}>
      <sprite ref={outerGlow}>
        <spriteMaterial map={sharedSunTexture} color="#ff7a30" opacity={0.5} {...spriteProps} />
      </sprite>
      <sprite ref={innerGlow}>
        <spriteMaterial map={sharedSunTexture} color="#ffb347" opacity={0.85} {...spriteProps} />
      </sprite>
      <sprite ref={corona}>
        <spriteMaterial map={sharedGlowTexture} color="#ffd9a0" opacity={0.7} {...spriteProps} />
      </sprite>
      <mesh>
        <sphereGeometry args={[r, 48, 48]} />
        <meshBasicMaterial color="#ffe9b8" />
      </mesh>
      <mesh>
        <sphereGeometry args={[r * 1.05, 48, 48]} />
        <meshBasicMaterial color="#ffa040" transparent opacity={0.35} blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending} depthWrite={false} side={THREE.BackSide} />
      </mesh>
    </group>
  )
}

// ── 3. Kamera Sinematik & Interaktivitas 360° (OrbitControls) ──────────
function CameraRig({ focusedPos }) {
  const { size, camera } = useThree()
  const isMobile = size.width < 768
  const overviewZ = isMobile ? 34 : 26

  const controlsRef = useRef()
  const [transitioning, setTransitioning] = useState(false)
  const prevFocusedRef = useRef(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (focusedPos !== prevFocusedRef.current) {
      setTransitioning(true)
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        setTransitioning(false)
      }, 700)
      prevFocusedRef.current = focusedPos
      return () => clearTimeout(timer)
    }
  }, [focusedPos])

  useFrame(() => {
    // Mode Overview: Lerp kamera kembali ke posisi tengah
    if (!focusedPos) {
      camera.position.lerp(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, overviewZ), 0.05)
      const currentLookAt = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -1)
        .applyQuaternion(camera.quaternion)
        .add(camera.position)
      currentLookAt.lerp(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0), 0.05)
      camera.lookAt(currentLookAt)
      return
    }

    // Mode Transisi Zoom-in ke Planet
    if (transitioning) {
      const targetPos = new THREE.Vector3(focusedPos.x, focusedPos.y, 8)
      camera.position.lerp(targetPos, 0.08)

      const lookTarget = new THREE.Vector3(focusedPos.x, focusedPos.y, 0)
      const currentLookAt = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -1)
        .applyQuaternion(camera.quaternion)
        .add(camera.position)
      currentLookAt.lerp(lookTarget, 0.08)
      camera.lookAt(currentLookAt)

      if (controlsRef.current) {
        controlsRef.current.target.set(focusedPos.x, focusedPos.y, 0)
      }
    }
  })

  return focusedPos ? (
    <OrbitControls
      ref={controlsRef}
      enabled={!transitioning}
      target={[focusedPos.x, focusedPos.y, 0]}
      enablePan={false}
      enableZoom={true}
      minDistance={3.5}
      maxDistance={12}
      rotateSpeed={0.8}
      enableDamping={true}
      dampingFactor={0.06}
    />
  ) : null
}

// ── 4. Komponen 3D Planet (Adaptif Mobile 2x4 & Desktop 4x2) ────────
function Planet3D({ data, index, isFocused, onClick }) {
  const planetGroup = useRef()
  const ringMesh = useRef()
  const glowSprite = useRef()
  const { size } = useThree()
  const isMobile = size.width < 768

  // Responsif: 2 kolom di mobile agar pas sempurna di layar potret HP, 4 kolom di desktop
  const col = isMobile ? (index % 2) : (index % 4)
  const row = isMobile ? Math.floor(index / 2) : Math.floor(index / 4)
  const spacingX = isMobile ? 4.2 : 5.0
  const spacingY = isMobile ? 4.0 : 6.0   // sebelumnya 4.4
  const posX = isMobile ? (col - 0.5) * spacingX : (col - 1.5) * spacingX
  const posY = isMobile ? (1.5 - row) * spacingY - 0.4 : (row === 0 ? (spacingY / 2) : -(spacingY / 2))

  const baseSize = parseInt(data.size) || 40
  const radius = baseSize / 65

  const atmosphereColor = getVibrantAtmosphere(data.name, data.color)

  useFrame((state, delta) => {
    if (planetGroup.current) planetGroup.current.rotation.y += 0.3 * delta
    if (ringMesh.current) ringMesh.current.rotation.z -= 0.4 * delta

    if (glowSprite.current) {
      const scale = 1 + Math.sin(state.clock.elapsedTime * (Math.PI / 2)) * 0.05
      glowSprite.current.scale.set(radius * 3.5 * scale, radius * 3.5 * scale, 1)
    }
  })

  return (
    <group position={[posX, posY, 0]}>

      {/* 3D SPRITE GLOW */}
      <sprite ref={glowSprite}>
        <spriteMaterial
          map={sharedGlowTexture}
          color={atmosphereColor}
          transparent={true}
          opacity={0.8}
          depthWrite={false}
          blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending}
        />
      </sprite>

      <group rotation={[Math.PI * 0.12, 0, Math.PI * 0.08]} ref={planetGroup}>

        {/* Lapis 1: INTI GELAP (Optimasi segmen) */}
        <mesh>
          <sphereGeometry args={[radius * 0.98, 32, 32]} />
          <meshBasicMaterial color="#020813" />
        </mesh>

        {/* Lapis 2: PERMUKAAN PLANET (Solid Polos Matte Doff Elegan) */}
        <group
          onClick={(e) => {
            e.stopPropagation()
            onClick({ x: posX, y: posY })
          }}
          onPointerOver={() => document.body.style.cursor = 'pointer'}
          onPointerOut={() => document.body.style.cursor = 'auto'}
        >
          {data.name === 'Earth' ? (
            <EarthMesh radius={radius} />
          ) : (
            <mesh castShadow receiveShadow>
              <sphereGeometry args={[radius, 48, 48]} />
              <meshStandardMaterial
                color={data.color || '#cccccc'}
                roughness={0.7}
                metalness={0.05}
                alphaTest={0.5}
              />
            </mesh>
          )}
        </group>

        {/* Lapis 3: KACA ATMOSFER VIBRANT (Colorful & Transparan) */}
        <mesh>
          <sphereGeometry args={[radius * 1.035, 36, 36]} />
          <meshBasicMaterial
            color={atmosphereColor}
            transparent={true}
            opacity={0.18}
            blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending}
            depthWrite={false}
            side={THREE.FrontSide}
          />
        </mesh>

        {/* Lapis 4: GARIS BORDER CINCIN */}
        <mesh>
          <ringGeometry args={[radius * 1.15, radius * 1.18, 48]} />
          <meshBasicMaterial
            color={atmosphereColor}
            transparent={true}
            opacity={0.25}
            blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending}
            side={THREE.DoubleSide}
          />
        </mesh>

        {/* Lapis 5: CINCIN SATURNUS/URANUS */}
        {data.rings && (
          <mesh
            ref={ringMesh}
            rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}
            castShadow
            receiveShadow
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation()
              onClick({ x: posX, y: posY })
            }}
            onPointerOver={() => document.body.style.cursor = 'pointer'}
            onPointerOut={() => document.body.style.cursor = 'auto'}
          >
            <ringGeometry args={[radius * 1.35, radius * 2.3, 64]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial
              color={data.color || '#ead6b8'}
              side={THREE.DoubleSide}
              transparent={true}
              alphaMap={sharedRingTexture}
              opacity={0.85}
              roughness={0.6}
            />
          </mesh>
        )}
      </group>

      <Html position={[0, -radius - 1.2, 0]} center style={{ pointerEvents: 'none' }}>
        <div className={styles.planetLabel} style={{ opacity: isFocused ? 0 : 1 }}>
          {data.name}
        </div>
      </Html>
    </group>
  )
}

// ── 5. Komponen Utama Solar System ─────────────────────────────────────
function SolarSystem({ onFact, onNext }) {
  const [focusedPos, setFocusedPos] = useState(null)
  const [activePlanet, setActivePlanet] = useState(null)

  const handlePlanetClick = (planet, pos) => {
    sfx.chime(PLANETS.indexOf(planet))
    setFocusedPos(pos)
    setActivePlanet(planet.id)
    onFact({
      title: planet.name,
      subtitle: `${planet.type} · ${planet.moons} moon${planet.moons !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`,
      text: planet.fact,
      extra: `Orbital period: ${planet.orbit}`,
    })
  }

  const handleBackgroundClick = () => {
    if (focusedPos) sfx.close()
    setFocusedPos(null)
    setActivePlanet(null)
    onFact(null)
  }

  // Esc: kembali ke overview
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!focusedPos) return
    const onKey = (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') handleBackgroundClick() }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
  }, [focusedPos])

  return (
    <section id="solar" className={`cosmic-section ${styles.solar}`}>
      <div className="noise-overlay" />
      <div className={styles.bgGradient} />

      <div className={`${styles.header} ${focusedPos !== null ? styles.headerHidden : ''}`}>
        <p className="t-label">Stage I: 1 AU from Earth</p>
        <h2 className={styles.title}>The Solar System</h2>
        <p className={styles.hint}>Tap or click a planet to learn more</p>
      </div>

      <button
        className={`${styles.backBtn} ${focusedPos !== null ? styles.backBtnVisible : ''}`}
        onClick={handleBackgroundClick}
      >
        ← Overview
      </button>

      <div className={`${styles.orbitHint} ${focusedPos !== null ? styles.orbitHintVisible : ''}`}>
        <span>✦ Drag to rotate 360° · Scroll to zoom</span>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.canvasWrap}>
        <Canvas
          shadows
          dpr={[1, 1.5]}
          camera={{ position: [0, 0, 26], fov: 40 }}
          onPointerMissed={handleBackgroundClick}
        >
          <ambientLight intensity={0.05} color="#ffffff" />

          <directionalLight
            position={[-20, 6, 4]}
            intensity={3.5}
            color="#fff8f0"
            castShadow
            shadow-bias={-0.0005}
          >
            <orthographicCamera attach="shadow-camera" args={[-30, 30, 15, -15]} />
          </directionalLight>

          <Sun />

          <Suspense fallback={null}>
            {PLANETS.map((planet, i) => (
              <Planet3D
                key={planet.id}
                data={planet}
                index={i}
                isFocused={activePlanet === planet.id}
                onClick={(pos) => handlePlanetClick(planet, pos)}
              />
            ))}
          </Suspense>

          <CameraRig focusedPos={focusedPos} />
        </Canvas>
      </div>

      <div className={`${styles.nextNav} ${focusedPos !== null ? styles.nextNavHidden : ''}`}>
        <button className={styles.nextBtn} onClick={onNext}>
          Explore Cosmic Phenomena <span className={styles.arrow}>↓</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

export default memo(SolarSystem)
===== END FILE =====

  .nextNav {
    bottom: 1.25rem;
  }

  /* Hint dipindah ke bawah tombol Overview, jadi tidak tertutup popup fakta */
  .orbitHint,
  .orbitHintVisible {
    top: 62px;
    bottom: auto;
    left: 20px;
    transform: none;
    font-size: var(--fs-small);
    padding: 4px 10px;
  }

  .orbitHint {
    transform: translateY(-8px);
  }

  .orbitHintVisible {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/sections/Wishing.jsx =====
import { memo, useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { sfx } from '../hooks/useSfx.js'
import WishForm from '../components/WishForm.jsx'
import styles from './Wishing.module.css'

const rand = (a, b) => a + Math.random() * (b - a)
const randInt = (a, b) => Math.floor(rand(a, b + 1))

const HINT_AFTER = 20      // detik tanpa tangkapan sebelum glow bintang terang naik
const FADE_MS = 650        // bintang biasa memudar setelah di-tap
const BURST_MS = 1400      // ledakan cahaya bintang terang
const BURST_MS_REDUCED = 900

function Wishing({
    visitor,
    onVisitorChange,
    onGallery,
    onSubmitted,
    onBackToConstellation,
    onReturnHome,
}) {
    const uid = useId()
    const wrapRef = useRef(null)
    const canvasRef = useRef(null)
    const catchRef = useRef(null)        // diisi effect canvas: tangkap bintang terang lewat tombol bantu
    const leavingRef = useRef(false)     // true setelah Skip: abaikan onCaught yang terlambat
    const resumeBtnRef = useRef(null)
    const doneBtnRef = useRef(null)

    // Wish sudah terkirim di perjalanan ini (mis. kembali lewat NavDots dari Secret Ending / Gallery):
    // tidak ada permainan lagi supaya tidak terjadi kiriman ganda. Dibaca sekali saat mount.
    const alreadyDone = useRef(Boolean(visitor?.wishSubmitted)).current

    const [round, setRound] = useState(0)          // naik = main ulang (lepas bintang)
    const [caught, setCaught] = useState(false)    // bintang terang sudah tertangkap
    const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = useState(false)
    const [draft, setDraft] = useState(null)       // isi form yang tersimpan saat form ditutup

    // Dipanggil dari loop canvas (lewat ref agar effect tidak perlu dibuat ulang)
    const handleCaught = () => {
        if (leavingRef.current) return
        setCaught(true)
        setFormOpen(true)
    }
    const caughtCbRef = useRef(handleCaught)
    caughtCbRef.current = handleCaught

    useEffect(() => {
        if (alreadyDone) return
        const canvas = canvasRef.current
        const wrap = wrapRef.current
        if (!canvas || !wrap) return
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
        const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches

        // ── Ukuran canvas (DPR dibatasi 1.5 agar hemat di HP) ───────────
        let w = 0, h = 0
        let settled = false   // true = loop sudah berhenti (frame terakhir tampil), resize menggambar ulang
        const resize = () => {
            const r = wrap.getBoundingClientRect()
            w = r.width
            h = r.height
            const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 1.5)
            canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(w * dpr))
            canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(h * dpr))
            ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0)
            if (settled) draw()
        }
        resize()
        const ro = new ResizeObserver(resize)
        ro.observe(wrap)

        // ── State permainan (semua di ref/closure, tanpa re-render) ─────
        // Antrean: false = bintang biasa, true = bintang terang. Terang tidak pernah jadi yang pertama.
        const total = reduce ? randInt(5, 6) : randInt(5, 10)
        const queue = Array.from({ length: total - 1 }, () => false)
        queue.splice(randInt(1, queue.length), 0, true)

        const stars = []
        let time = 0
        let nextSpawn = 0.9
        let boost = 0                 // 0..1, glow petunjuk bintang terang
        let phase = 'play'            // 'play' | 'burst'
        let burst = null
        let done = false
        const burstMs = reduce ? BURST_MS_REDUCED : BURST_MS

        const spawn = (bright) => {
            const x = rand(0.06 * w, 0.94 * w)
            let dir = x < w / 2 ? 1 : -1            // arah ke sisi layar yang lebih lebar
            if (!bright && Math.random() < 0.2) dir = -dir
            const room = dir > 0 ? w * 0.97 - x : x - w * 0.03
            let ang = bright ? rand(0.3, 0.7) : rand(0.35, 1.0)
            if (!bright || dir === (x < w / 2 ? 1 : -1)) {
                ang = Math.min(ang, Math.atan(Math.max(room, 40) / (h * 0.75)))
            }
            ang = Math.max(ang, 0.18)
            const base = Math.min(Math.max(h, 360), 900)
            const speed = (bright ? rand(0.15, 0.19) : rand(0.16, 0.26)) * base * (reduce ? 0.6 : 1)
            stars.push({
                x,
                y: rand(-30, -10),
                vx: dir * Math.sin(ang) * speed,
                vy: Math.cos(ang) * speed,
                bright,
                r: bright ? 4.6 : rand(1.6, 2.6),
                tail: bright ? rand(190, 230) : rand(60, 120),
                state: 'fall',          // fall | fade | caught
                t: 0,
                rx: 0, ry: 0,           // posisi riak saat di-tap
                phase: rand(0, Math.PI * 2),
            })
        }

        const requeueBright = () => {
            if (phase !== 'play') return
            queue.splice(randInt(0, Math.min(1, queue.length)), 0, true)
            if (Math.random() < 0.6) queue.unshift(false)   // selingan bintang biasa
            nextSpawn = Math.min(nextSpawn, 1.0)
        }

        // Bintang terang tertangkap (lewat tap, atau lewat tombol bantu dengan hit = null / bintang di layar)
        const catchBright = (hit) => {
            phase = 'burst'
            if (hit) { hit.state = 'caught'; hit.t = 0 }
            burst = { x: hit ? hit.x : w / 2, y: hit ? hit.y : h * 0.35, t: 0 }
            stars.forEach((s) => {
                if (s !== hit && s.state === 'fall') {
                    s.state = 'fade'; s.t = 0; s.rx = s.x; s.ry = s.y
                    s.vx *= 0.3; s.vy *= 0.3
                }
            })
            sfx.reveal()
            sfx.chime(4)
        }

        // Jalur keyboard / screen reader
        catchRef.current = () => {
            if (phase !== 'play') return
            catchBright(stars.find((s) => s.bright && s.state === 'fall') || null)
        }

        // ── Interaksi: hitbox ≥ 48px (radius 26 / 34), mencakup sedikit ekor ──
        const onPointerDown = (e) => {
            if (phase !== 'play') return
            const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect()
            const px = e.clientX - rect.left
            const py = e.clientY - rect.top

            let hit = null
            let best = Infinity
            for (const s of stars) {
                if (s.state !== 'fall') continue
                const R = s.bright ? 34 : 26
                const len = Math.hypot(s.vx, s.vy) || 1
                const ax = s.x, ay = s.y
                const bx = s.x - (s.vx / len) * 30
                const by = s.y - (s.vy / len) * 30
                const abx = bx - ax, aby = by - ay
                let t = ((px - ax) * abx + (py - ay) * aby) / (abx * abx + aby * aby)
                t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t))
                const d = Math.hypot(px - (ax + abx * t), py - (ay + aby * t))
                if (d <= R) {
                    const score = s.bright ? d - 100 : d   // bintang terang diprioritaskan
                    if (score < best) { best = score; hit = s }
                }
            }
            if (!hit) return
            e.preventDefault()

            if (hit.bright) {
                catchBright(hit)
            } else {
                hit.state = 'fade'
                hit.t = 0
                hit.rx = hit.x
                hit.ry = hit.y
                hit.vx *= 0.3
                hit.vy *= 0.3
                sfx.click()
            }
        }
        canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown, { passive: false })

        // ── Update ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        const update = (dt) => {
            time += dt

            if (phase === 'play') {
                if (time > HINT_AFTER) boost = Math.min(1, (time - HINT_AFTER) / 4)

                nextSpawn -= dt
                if (nextSpawn <= 0 && queue.length) {
                    spawn(queue.shift())
                    nextSpawn = rand(1.2, 2.8) * (reduce ? 1.4 : 1)
                }
            } else if (burst) {
                burst.t = Math.min(burst.t + dt * 1000, burstMs)
                if (burst.t >= burstMs && !done) {
                    done = true
                    caughtCbRef.current()
                }
            }

            for (let i = stars.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                const s = stars[i]
                s.t += dt * 1000
                if (s.state === 'caught') continue
                s.x += s.vx * dt
                s.y += s.vy * dt
                if (s.state === 'fade') {
                    if (s.t >= FADE_MS) stars.splice(i, 1)
                    continue
                }
                const len = Math.hypot(s.vx, s.vy) || 1
                const tx = s.x - (s.vx / len) * s.tail
                const ty = s.y - (s.vy / len) * s.tail
                const gone = ty > h + 10 || (s.vx > 0 && tx > w + 10) || (s.vx < 0 && tx < -10)
                if (gone) {
                    stars.splice(i, 1)
                    if (s.bright) requeueBright()   // bintang terang lolos → muncul lagi
                }
            }
        }

        // ── Gambar ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        const drawStar = (s) => {
            if (s.state === 'caught') return
            const len = Math.hypot(s.vx, s.vy) || 1
            const ux = s.vx / len, uy = s.vy / len
            const alpha = s.state === 'fade' ? Math.max(0, 1 - s.t / FADE_MS) : 1

            ctx.globalAlpha = alpha

            // ekor gradasi
            const tx = s.x - ux * s.tail
            const ty = s.y - uy * s.tail
            const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(tx, ty, s.x, s.y)
            g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(170,200,255,0)')
            g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(235,243,255,${s.bright ? 0.95 : 0.8})`)
            ctx.strokeStyle = g
            ctx.lineWidth = s.bright ? 2.6 : 1.3
            ctx.lineCap = 'round'
            ctx.beginPath()
            ctx.moveTo(tx, ty)
            ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y)
            ctx.stroke()

            // halo (bintang terang berdenyut halus; naik saat petunjuk)
            let R, A
            if (s.bright) {
                const pulse = reduce ? 0.5 : 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(time * 2.4 + s.phase)
                R = 24 + pulse * 8 + boost * 10
                A = 0.22 + pulse * 0.12 + boost * 0.35
            } else {
                R = s.r * 5
                A = 0.4
            }
            const hg = ctx.createRadialGradient(s.x, s.y, 0, s.x, s.y, R)
            hg.addColorStop(0, `rgba(225,236,255,${Math.min(1, A * 2)})`)
            hg.addColorStop(0.35, `rgba(160,190,255,${A})`)
            hg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(120,150,255,0)')
            ctx.fillStyle = hg
            ctx.beginPath()
            ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, R, 0, Math.PI * 2)
            ctx.fill()

            ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'
            ctx.beginPath()
            ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, s.r, 0, Math.PI * 2)
            ctx.fill()

            ctx.globalAlpha = 1

            // riak kecil saat bintang biasa di-tap
            if (s.state === 'fade') {
                const p = Math.min(1, s.t / FADE_MS)
                ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(170,200,255,${0.5 * (1 - p)})`
                ctx.lineWidth = 1.2
                ctx.beginPath()
                ctx.arc(s.rx, s.ry, 6 + 34 * p, 0, Math.PI * 2)
                ctx.stroke()
            }
        }

        const drawBurst = () => {
            const p = burst.t / burstMs
            const e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3)               // ease-out
            const diag = Math.hypot(w, h)
            const A = p < 0.5 ? p * 2 : 1 - (p - 0.5) * 0.9 // naik cepat, mengendap ke ~0.55
            const R = 20 + e * diag * 0.6

            const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(burst.x, burst.y, 0, burst.x, burst.y, R)
            g.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255,255,255,${A})`)
            g.addColorStop(0.25, `rgba(205,222,255,${A * 0.8})`)
            g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(120,150,255,0)')
            ctx.fillStyle = g
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h)

            // inti bintang membesar
            ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${Math.min(1, 0.4 + p)})`
            ctx.beginPath()
            ctx.arc(burst.x, burst.y, 5 + e * 38, 0, Math.PI * 2)
            ctx.fill()

            // gelombang kejut (dilewati saat reduced motion)
            if (!reduce) {
                ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(190,210,255,${Math.max(0, 0.7 * (1 - p))})`
                ctx.lineWidth = 2
                ctx.beginPath()
                ctx.arc(burst.x, burst.y, e * Math.min(w, h) * 0.55, 0, Math.PI * 2)
                ctx.stroke()
            }
        }

        function draw() {
            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h)
            ctx.save()
            if (burst && !reduce && burst.t < 350) {      // getaran singkat, tanpa saat reduced motion
                const k = 3 * (1 - burst.t / 350)
                ctx.translate((Math.random() - 0.5) * k, (Math.random() - 0.5) * k)
            }
            stars.forEach(drawStar)
            if (burst) drawBurst()
            ctx.restore()
        }

        let raf = 0
        let last = performance.now()
        const frame = (now) => {
            const dt = Math.min((now - last) / 1000, 0.05)
            last = now
            update(dt)
            draw()
            // Setelah ledakan selesai hanya bintang tertangkap yang tersisa: tidak ada yang bergerak lagi.
            // Hentikan loop agar HP tidak menggambar ulang 60fps di belakang form (backdrop blur).
            if (done && stars.every((s) => s.state === 'caught')) {
                settled = true
                raf = 0
                return
            }
            raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame)
        }
        raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame)

        return () => {
            cancelAnimationFrame(raf)
            canvas.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown)
            ro.disconnect()
            catchRef.current = null
        }
    }, [round, alreadyDone])

    // Fokus: panel "sudah jadi bintang" saat mount; kartu resume saat form ditutup
    useEffect(() => {
        if (alreadyDone) doneBtnRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true })
    }, [alreadyDone])

    useEffect(() => {
        if (caught && !formOpen) resumeBtnRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true })
    }, [caught, formOpen])

    const handleSkip = () => {
        sfx.click()
        leavingRef.current = true
        onVisitorChange?.({ skipped: true })   // flag: skip TIDAK membuka Secret Ending
        onGallery?.()
    }

    // Form ditutup tanpa kirim: simpan isi form, tawarkan buka lagi / lepas bintang
    const handleFormClose = (savedDraft) => {
        setDraft(savedDraft)
        setFormOpen(false)
    }

    // Kiriman sukses: simpan ke state App (mySubmission)
    const handleSaved = (record) => {
        onVisitorChange?.({ mySubmission: record, wishSubmitted: true, skipped: false })
    }

    // Animasi bintang naik selesai → Secret Ending
    const handleDone = () => {
        onSubmitted?.()
    }

    const handleRelease = () => {
        sfx.click()
        setFormOpen(false)
        setCaught(false)
        setRound((r) => r + 1)
    }

    const handleReopen = () => {
        sfx.open()
        setFormOpen(true)
    }

    const act = (fn) => () => {
        sfx.click()
        leavingRef.current = true
        fn?.()
    }

    // ── Wish sudah terkirim: tidak ada permainan, hanya pilihan lanjut ──
    if (alreadyDone) {
        return (
            <section id="wishing" className={styles.wishing}>
                <div className={styles.resume} role="region" aria-labelledby={`${uid}-done`}>
                    <div className={styles.card}>
                        <p className={styles.cardStar} aria-hidden="true">✦</p>
                        <p id={`${uid}-done`} className={styles.cardTitle}>Your wish is already a star</p>
                        <p className={styles.cardText}>
                            You can make one wish per journey.
                            Return home to start a new one.
                        </p>
                        <div className={styles.cardActions}>
                            <button ref={doneBtnRef} className={styles.cardBtn} onClick={act(onBackToConstellation)}>
                                Stay with my constellation
                            </button>
                            <button className={styles.cardBtn} onClick={act(onGallery)}>
                                View the Gallery
                            </button>
                            <button className={styles.cardBtn} onClick={act(onReturnHome)}>
                                Return home
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </section>
        )
    }

    return (
        <section id="wishing" className={styles.wishing}>
            <div ref={wrapRef} className={styles.sky}>
                <canvas
                    ref={canvasRef}
                    className={styles.canvas}
                    role="img"
                    aria-label="Night sky with falling stars. Tap the brightest one to make a wish."
                />
            </div>

            {/* Jalur keyboard / screen reader: terlihat hanya saat difokus */}
            {!caught && (
                <button type="button" className={styles.assist} onClick={() => catchRef.current?.()}>
                    Catch the brightest star
                </button>
            )}

            <div className={styles.caption}>
                <p className={styles.label}>Wishing Star</p>
                <h2 className={styles.title}>
                    Make <em>a wish</em>
                </h2>
                <p className={styles.hint}>Find the brightest falling star</p>
            </div>

            <button className={styles.skip} onClick={handleSkip}>
                Skip to gallery
            </button>

            {/* Form ditutup setelah bintang tertangkap: bisa dibuka lagi atau bintang dilepas */}
            {caught && !formOpen && (
                <div className={styles.resume}>
                    <div
                        className={styles.card}
                        role="dialog"
                        aria-modal="true"
                        aria-labelledby={`${uid}-resume`}
                    >
                        <p className={styles.cardStar} aria-hidden="true">✦</p>
                        <p id={`${uid}-resume`} className={styles.cardTitle}>Your star is still glowing</p>
                        <p className={styles.cardText}>
                            {draft?.message?.trim()
                                ? 'Your words are safe. Pick up where you left off.'
                                : 'Whisper your wish whenever you are ready.'}
                        </p>
                        <div className={styles.cardActions}>
                            <button ref={resumeBtnRef} className={styles.cardBtn} onClick={handleReopen}>
                                Continue my wish
                            </button>
                            <button className={styles.cardBtn} onClick={handleRelease}>
                                Release the star
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {formOpen && (
                <WishForm
                    initialDraft={draft}
                    onClose={handleFormClose}
                    onSaved={handleSaved}
                    onDone={handleDone}
                />
            )}
        </section>
    )
}

export default memo(Wishing)
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: src/sections/Wishing.module.css =====
/* Tinggi layar = 100dvh, tanpa padding & min-height 0 → wrapper tidak bisa di-scroll.
   Fase 9B: latar disederhanakan. Tengah transparan agar bintang (yang sudah diredupkan
   oleh depth StarField 0.45) tidak diredupkan dua kali; hanya tepi yang digelapkan tipis. */
.wishing {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100vh;
    height: 100dvh;
    min-height: 0;
    padding: 0;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 30%, transparent 0%, rgba(0, 0, 6, 0.3) 85%);
}

/* Langit: StarField global tetap terlihat di belakang */
.sky {
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    z-index: 1;
    background:
        radial-gradient(ellipse 80% 35% at 50% 105%, rgba(90, 70, 200, 0.22) 0%, transparent 70%),
        radial-gradient(ellipse 60% 40% at 15% 20%, rgba(80, 120, 255, 0.07) 0%, transparent 70%);
}

.canvas {
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: block;
    touch-action: none;
    cursor: crosshair;
}

/* Judul kecil di bagian bawah: aman dari MusicPlayer (kanan atas) dan tidak menutupi langit.
   ::before = scrim gradien gelap halus di belakang blok teks (tanpa tepi kotak). */
.caption {
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    right: 0;
    bottom: calc(5.2rem + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
    z-index: 2;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.35rem;
    padding: 0 1.25rem;
    text-align: center;
    pointer-events: none;
}

.caption::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    right: 0;
    top: -3rem;
    bottom: -5.2rem;
    z-index: -1;
    background: linear-gradient(to top, rgba(1, 1, 12, 0.7) 0%, rgba(1, 1, 12, 0.5) 55%, rgba(1, 1, 12, 0) 100%);
    pointer-events: none;
}

.label {
    font-family: var(--font-body);
    font-size: var(--fs-small);
    font-weight: var(--fw-regular);
    letter-spacing: var(--ls-label);
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--accent-blue-text);
    text-shadow: var(--text-shadow-soft);
}

.title {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    font-weight: var(--fw-display);
    line-height: 1.1;
    font-size: clamp(1.5rem, 5vw, 2rem);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    text-shadow: var(--text-shadow-soft);
}

.title em {
    font-style: italic;
    color: #cfe0ff;
}

/* Petunjuk muncul, bertahan beberapa detik, lalu memudar */
.hint {
    font-family: var(--font-body);
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: var(--fw-regular);
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    max-width: var(--measure);
    line-height: 1.6;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    text-shadow: var(--text-shadow-soft);
    opacity: 0;
    animation: hintLife 9s ease forwards;
}

@keyframes hintLife {
    0% {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: translateY(6px);
    }

    12% {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translateY(0);
    }

    78% {
        opacity: 1;
    }

    100% {
        opacity: 0;
    }
}

/* Selalu terlihat, di tengah bawah (aman dari NavDots di kanan & player di atas) */
.skip {
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    bottom: calc(1rem + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
    transform: translateX(-50%);
    z-index: 5;
    min-height: 44px;
    padding: 0.6rem 1.4rem;
    background: rgba(8, 10, 28, 0.6);
    border: 1px solid rgba(136, 170, 255, 0.4);
    border-radius: 50px;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font-family: var(--font-body);
    font-size: 0.8125rem;
    font-weight: var(--fw-regular);
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    white-space: nowrap;
    cursor: pointer;
    backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
    transition: color 0.3s ease, border-color 0.3s ease;
}

.skip:hover,
.skip:focus-visible {
    color: #fff;
    border-color: rgba(180, 200, 255, 0.8);
    outline: none;
}

===== FILE: src/services/wishes.js =====
// ============================================================
// COSMIC JOURNEY — Wishes service (Fase 6: backend nyata)
// submitWish → POST /api/submit-wish, getWishes → GET /api/get-wishes
// Kontrak fungsi SAMA dengan versi dummy (Fase 2 & 4); UI tidak berubah.
// Tidak ada service key di sini: semua rahasia hanya ada di Netlify Functions.
// ============================================================

export const MESSAGE_MIN = 5
export const MESSAGE_MAX = 280
export const NAME_MAX = 30
export const PAGE_SIZE = 12

// id = nilai yang dikirim ke backend (whitelist server-side di netlify/lib/shared.js)
export const CATEGORIES = [
    { id: 'feedback', label: 'Feedback' },
    { id: 'suggestion', label: 'Suggestion' },
    { id: 'developer', label: 'About the developer' },
    { id: 'wish', label: 'Just a wish' },
]
const CATEGORY_IDS = new Set(CATEGORIES.map((c) => c.id))

const API_BASE = '/api'
const SUBMIT_TIMEOUT = 15000
const GET_TIMEOUT = 10000
const ERROR_CODES = new Set(['validation', 'rate_limit', 'network', 'server'])

// ── Callsign anonim: dibuat sekali per sesi (mis. "Astronaut-7F2") ──
const CALLSIGN_CHARS = '23456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ' // tanpa 0/O/1/I agar tidak membingungkan
let sessionCallsign = null

export function getSessionCallsign() {
    if (sessionCallsign) return sessionCallsign
    const buf = new Uint32Array(3)
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.crypto?.getRandomValues) {
        window.crypto.getRandomValues(buf)
    } else {
        for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i++) buf[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 4294967296)
    }
    const code = Array.from(buf, (n) => CALLSIGN_CHARS[n % CALLSIGN_CHARS.length]).join('')
    sessionCallsign = `Astronaut-${code}`
    return sessionCallsign
}

// Dipanggil saat pengunjung "pulang ke Bumi" (perjalanan baru = pengunjung baru)
export function resetSessionCallsign() {
    sessionCallsign = null
}

// ── Pembersihan teks (server melakukan hal yang sama dan tetap menjadi penentu) ──
const cleanName = (s) =>
    String(s ?? '').replace(/[\u0000-\u001F\u007F-\u009F]/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()

const cleanMessage = (s) =>
    String(s ?? '')
        .replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n')
        .replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F\u007F-\u009F]/g, '')
        .replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n')
        .trim()

const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms))

function fail(code, message) {
    const err = new Error(message)
    err.code = code
    return err
}

// ── Pemanggil HTTP: semua kegagalan diubah menjadi Error ber-.code ──
async function request(path, { method = 'GET', body, timeout }) {
    const ctrl = new AbortController()
    const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), timeout)
    try {
        let res
        try {
            res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/${path}`, {
                method,
                headers: body ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : undefined,
                body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
                signal: ctrl.signal,
            })
        } catch (_) {
            // offline, DNS, timeout, atau diblokir
            throw fail('network', 'Could not reach the observatory.')
        }

        // Bila backend mati / /api tidak ada, server bisa membalas HTML (bukan JSON) → data = null
        let data = null
        try { data = await res.json() } catch (_) { /* bukan JSON */ }

        if (!res.ok) {
            const e = data?.error
            if (e && ERROR_CODES.has(e.code)) {
                throw fail(e.code, typeof e.message === 'string' ? e.message : 'Something went wrong.')
            }
            throw fail(res.status === 429 ? 'rate_limit' : 'server', 'The observatory is quiet right now.')
        }
        if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') {
            throw fail('server', 'The observatory is quiet right now.')
        }
        return data
    } finally {
        clearTimeout(timer)
    }
}

/**
 * Kontrak (sama seperti sebelumnya):
 *   submitWish({ displayName, isAnonymous, category, message, rating, isPrivate, consent, website })
 *   → Promise<record>  atau throw Error dengan .code: 'validation' | 'rate_limit' | 'network' | 'server'
 *   record = { id, displayName, isAnonymous, category, message, rating, isPrivate, createdAt, status }
 * `website` = field honeypot (harus kosong; server membalas sukses palsu tanpa menyimpan bila terisi).
 * POST TIDAK di-retry otomatis agar tidak terjadi kiriman ganda.
 */
export async function submitWish(payload) {
    // Pemeriksaan cepat di klien (server tetap memvalidasi ulang secara ketat)
    const message = cleanMessage(payload?.message)
    const rating = Number(payload?.rating)
    const isPrivate = Boolean(payload?.isPrivate)

    if (message.length < MESSAGE_MIN || message.length > MESSAGE_MAX) {
        throw fail('validation', `Your message needs ${MESSAGE_MIN} to ${MESSAGE_MAX} characters.`)
    }
    if (!Number.isInteger(rating) || rating < 1 || rating > 5) {
        throw fail('validation', 'Please choose a rating from 1 to 5 stars.')
    }
    if (!CATEGORY_IDS.has(payload?.category)) {
        throw fail('validation', 'Please choose a category.')
    }
    if (!isPrivate && !payload?.consent) {
        throw fail('validation', 'Please agree to show your message in the Gallery, or send it privately.')
    }

    const isAnonymous = Boolean(payload?.isAnonymous)
    const displayName = cleanName(payload?.displayName).slice(0, NAME_MAX) || getSessionCallsign()

    const record = await request('submit-wish', {
        method: 'POST',
        timeout: SUBMIT_TIMEOUT,
        body: {
            displayName,
            isAnonymous,
            category: payload.category,
            message,
            rating,
            isPrivate,
            consent: Boolean(payload?.consent),
            website: typeof payload?.website === 'string' ? payload.website : '',
        },
    })

    if (!record.id || typeof record.message !== 'string') {
        throw fail('server', 'The observatory is quiet right now.')
    }
    return record
}

/**
 * Kontrak (sama seperti sebelumnya):
 *   getWishes({ sort = 'newest' | 'rating', category = 'all' | <id kategori>, cursor = null, limit = 12 })
 *   → Promise<{
 *        items: [{ id, displayName, isAnonymous, category, message, rating, createdAt }],
 *        nextCursor: string | null,     // opaque: kirim balik apa adanya; null = sudah habis
 *        total: number,                 // jumlah item yang cocok dengan filter kategori
 *        aggregate: { average: number, count: number }  // semua wish publik, TIDAK terpengaruh filter
 *     }>
 *   atau throw Error dengan .code: 'validation' | 'network' | 'server'
 * GET aman di-retry: satu kali ulang bila jaringan gagal.
 */
export async function getWishes({ sort = 'newest', category = 'all', cursor = null, limit = PAGE_SIZE } = {}) {
    if (sort !== 'newest' && sort !== 'rating') throw fail('validation', 'Unknown sort.')
    if (category !== 'all' && !CATEGORY_IDS.has(category)) throw fail('validation', 'Unknown category.')
    const pageSize = Math.min(50, Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(limit)) || PAGE_SIZE))

    const q = new URLSearchParams({ sort, category, limit: String(pageSize) })
    if (cursor) q.set('cursor', String(cursor))
    const path = `get-wishes?${q}`

    let data
    try {
        data = await request(path, { timeout: GET_TIMEOUT })
    } catch (err) {
        if (err.code !== 'network') throw err
        await wait(700)
        data = await request(path, { timeout: GET_TIMEOUT })
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(data.items) || !data.aggregate) {
        throw fail('server', 'The observatory is quiet right now.')
    }
    return {
        items: data.items,
        nextCursor: typeof data.nextCursor === 'string' ? data.nextCursor : null,
        total: Number(data.total) || 0,
        aggregate: {
            count: Number(data.aggregate.count) || 0,
            average: Number(data.aggregate.average) || 0,
        },
    }
}
===== END FILE =====
